# Autenticação + RBAC (Entrega 1) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Entregar autenticação (Keycloak via porta Adapter) e RBAC multi-tenant no `identity-service` + BFF, com telas de auth no web e no mobile, tudo executado localmente via docker-compose.

**Architecture:** Monorepo pnpm/Turborepo com dois serviços NestJS (BFF público e `identity-service` com Prisma/Postgres). O Keycloak cuida de credenciais/sessões atrás de `IdentityProviderPort`; tenant, memberships e permissões vivem no identity-service e o BFF resolve/cacheia o contexto de autorização e o repassa assinado aos serviços.

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript, NestJS, Prisma, PostgreSQL 16, Redis 7, Keycloak 26, OpenTelemetry, pnpm 9 + Turborepo, Vitest, Orval + TanStack Query + Zod, Vite + React, Expo + React Native, Docker Compose.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-19-rbac-auth-design.md`

## Global Constraints

- **Tudo roda em containers.** Nenhum comando exige Node/pnpm no host; use `docker compose run --rm workspace pnpm ...` (alias `make sh`/`make test`). O host só precisa de Docker.
- `docker-compose.yml` fica na **raiz** do repositório; configs em `infra/`.
- Node 22, pnpm 9.15.0, TypeScript estrito (`"strict": true`), NestJS 10.
- PKs: UUID nas tabelas transacionais, inteiro sequencial nas de configuração (`Role`, `Permission`).
- **Sem foreign key entre bancos/serviços.** Um database por serviço (`aura_identity`).
- Senha nunca no nosso banco. `tenant_id` nunca vem do cliente; deriva da membership ativa.
- Autorização por **permissão** (`recurso:ação`, catálogo em `@aura/authz`), nunca por nome de papel.
- Cadastro público cria só `personal`; `aluno` só por convite; `admin` só por seed/CLI.
- Access token curto (~10 min), só em memória no web; refresh em cookie `HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax` (web) ou SecureStore (mobile); refresh com rotação e detecção de reuso.
- Erros: `{ code, message, correlationId }`, sem stack/SQL/paths. Erros de auth genéricos.
- Produção: `NODE_ENV=production` desliga Swagger UI e rotas de debug; boot falha se faltar variável obrigatória.
- Headers via helmet completo, allowlist de Host, CORS por allowlist (sem `*` com credenciais), HSTS ≥ 1 ano.
- Supply chain: `pnpm install --frozen-lockfile`, `pnpm audit` bloqueia alta/crítica, gitleaks, Trivy, SBOM CycloneDX.
- Commits pequenos, um por task (mensagens em inglês, convencionais).

---

## File Structure

```
docker-compose.yml                       raiz; todos os serviços de dev
.env.example                             variáveis locais (copiar para .env)
Makefile                                 atalhos docker compose (up, down, sh, test, migrate, gen)
package.json / pnpm-workspace.yaml / turbo.json / .npmrc
infra/
  postgres/init.sql                      cria aura_identity e aura_keycloak
  keycloak/realm-aura.json               realm importado
  otel/collector.yaml
  docker/node.Dockerfile                 stages: dev, build, prod (multi-stage, não-root)
packages/
  config/                                tsconfig.base.json
  authz/                                 catálogo de permissões + mapa papel→permissões
  observability/                         bootstrap OTel
  schemas/                               Zod compartilhado
  contracts/                             OpenAPI + clientes Orval
apps/
  identity-service/
    prisma/schema.prisma, seed.ts
    src/
      main.ts, app.module.ts
      config/ (env validation)
      shared/ (errors, security, audit)
      identity-provider/ (port, in-memory, keycloak)
      accounts/ (registro, convites, perfil)
      sessions/ (login, refresh, seleção de membership)
      authorization/ (contexto, permissões admin)
  bff/
    src/ (auth JWT, contexto, proxy, segurança)
  web/                                   Vite + React
  mobile/                                Expo
```

Responsabilidades: `identity-provider/` isola o Keycloak; `accounts/` é o domínio de cadastro; `sessions/` cuida de tokens/refresh; `authorization/` expõe o contexto que o BFF consome.

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
```

`docker-compose.yml`:

```yaml
name: aura-fit

x-node: &node
  build:
    context: .
    dockerfile: infra/docker/node.Dockerfile
    target: dev
  working_dir: /repo
  env_file: .env
  volumes:
    - .:/repo
    - pnpm-store:/pnpm-store
  environment:
    NODE_ENV: development
    PNPM_HOME: /pnpm-store

services:
  workspace:
    <<: *node
    profiles: ["tools"]
    command: ["sh"]

  postgres:
    image: postgres:16-alpine
    environment:
      POSTGRES_USER: ${POSTGRES_USER}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${POSTGRES_PASSWORD}
    ports: ["127.0.0.1:5432:5432"]
    volumes:
      - pgdata:/var/lib/postgresql/data
      - ./infra/postgres/init.sql:/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/init.sql:ro
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U ${POSTGRES_USER}"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 3s
      retries: 20

  redis:
    image: redis:7-alpine
    command: ["redis-server", "--requirepass", "${REDIS_PASSWORD}"]
    ports: ["127.0.0.1:6379:6379"]
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "redis-cli -a ${REDIS_PASSWORD} ping | grep PONG"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 3s
      retries: 20

  keycloak:
    image: quay.io/keycloak/keycloak:26.0
    command: ["start-dev", "--import-realm"]
    environment:
      KC_BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_USERNAME: ${KEYCLOAK_ADMIN}
      KC_BOOTSTRAP_ADMIN_PASSWORD: ${KEYCLOAK_ADMIN_PASSWORD}
      KC_DB: postgres
      KC_DB_URL: jdbc:postgresql://postgres:5432/aura_keycloak
      KC_DB_USERNAME: ${POSTGRES_USER}
      KC_DB_PASSWORD: ${POSTGRES_PASSWORD}
      KC_HEALTH_ENABLED: "true"
    ports: ["127.0.0.1:8081:8080"]
    volumes:
      - ./infra/keycloak/realm-aura.json:/opt/keycloak/data/import/realm-aura.json:ro
    depends_on:
      postgres: { condition: service_healthy }
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "exec 3<>/dev/tcp/localhost/9000 && echo -e 'GET /health/ready HTTP/1.1\\r\\nHost: localhost\\r\\nConnection: close\\r\\n\\r\\n' >&3 && grep -q UP <&3"]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 30
      start_period: 40s

  otel-collector:
    image: otel/opentelemetry-collector-contrib:0.111.0
    command: ["--config=/etc/otel/collector.yaml"]
    volumes:
      - ./infra/otel/collector.yaml:/etc/otel/collector.yaml:ro

volumes:
  pgdata:
  pnpm-store:
```

```yaml
processors:
  batch: {}
exporters:
  debug: { verbosity: basic }
service:
  pipelines:
    traces:  { receivers: [otlp], processors: [batch], exporters: [debug] }
    metrics: { receivers: [otlp], processors: [batch], exporters: [debug] }
    logs:    { receivers: [otlp], processors: [batch], exporters: [debug] }
```

```dockerfile
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1
FROM node:22-alpine AS base
ENV PNPM_HOME=/pnpm-store
RUN corepack enable && corepack prepare pnpm@9.15.0 --activate \
    && apk add --no-cache openssl bash

FROM base AS dev
WORKDIR /repo

FROM base AS build
WORKDIR /repo
COPY . .
RUN pnpm install --frozen-lockfile && pnpm turbo run build

FROM base AS prod
ENV NODE_ENV=production
WORKDIR /app
COPY --from=build --chown=node:node /repo /app
USER node
```

### Task 1: `@aura/authz` — catálogo de permissões

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/authz/package.json`, `packages/authz/tsconfig.json`, `packages/authz/src/index.ts`, `packages/authz/src/permissions.ts`, `packages/authz/src/roles.ts`
- Test: `packages/authz/src/authz.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `PERMISSIONS`: objeto `as const` de strings `recurso:ação`
  - `type Permission`
  - `type RoleName = 'personal' | 'aluno' | 'admin'`
  - `ROLE_PERMISSIONS: Record<RoleName, readonly Permission[]>`
  - `isPermission(value: string): value is Permission`
  - `hasPermission(granted: readonly string[], required: Permission): boolean`

- [ ] **Step 1: Escrever o teste que falha**

`packages/authz/src/authz.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { PERMISSIONS, ROLE_PERMISSIONS, hasPermission, isPermission } from './index';

describe('authz catalog', () => {
  const all = Object.values(PERMISSIONS);

  it('uses the recurso:acao format', () => {
    for (const p of all) expect(p).toMatch(/^[a-z]+:[a-z]+$/);
  });

  it('only grants permissions that exist in the catalog', () => {
    for (const perms of Object.values(ROLE_PERMISSIONS)) {
      for (const p of perms) expect(isPermission(p)).toBe(true);
    }
  });

  it('aluno cannot create invites nor manage roles', () => {
    expect(hasPermission(ROLE_PERMISSIONS.aluno, PERMISSIONS.INVITES_CREATE)).toBe(false);
    expect(hasPermission(ROLE_PERMISSIONS.aluno, PERMISSIONS.ROLES_MANAGE)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('personal can create invites but not manage roles', () => {
    expect(hasPermission(ROLE_PERMISSIONS.personal, PERMISSIONS.INVITES_CREATE)).toBe(true);
    expect(hasPermission(ROLE_PERMISSIONS.personal, PERMISSIONS.ROLES_MANAGE)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('admin manages roles and users', () => {
    expect(hasPermission(ROLE_PERMISSIONS.admin, PERMISSIONS.ROLES_MANAGE)).toBe(true);
    expect(hasPermission(ROLE_PERMISSIONS.admin, PERMISSIONS.USERS_MANAGE)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('rejects unknown strings', () => {
    expect(isPermission('students:hack')).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`packages/authz/src/permissions.ts`:

```ts
export const PERMISSIONS = {
  PROFILE_READ: 'profile:read',
  PROFILE_UPDATE: 'profile:update',
  INVITES_CREATE: 'invites:create',
  INVITES_READ: 'invites:read',
  USERS_READ: 'users:read',
  USERS_MANAGE: 'users:manage',
  ROLES_READ: 'roles:read',
  ROLES_MANAGE: 'roles:manage',
  TENANTS_READ: 'tenants:read',
  AUDIT_READ: 'audit:read',
} as const;

export type Permission = (typeof PERMISSIONS)[keyof typeof PERMISSIONS];

const ALL = new Set<string>(Object.values(PERMISSIONS));

export function isPermission(value: string): value is Permission {
  return ALL.has(value);
}

export function hasPermission(granted: readonly string[], required: Permission): boolean {
  return granted.includes(required);
}
```

`packages/authz/src/roles.ts`:

```ts
import { PERMISSIONS as P, type Permission } from './permissions';

export type RoleName = 'personal' | 'aluno' | 'admin';

export const ROLE_PERMISSIONS: Record<RoleName, readonly Permission[]> = {
  aluno: [P.PROFILE_READ, P.PROFILE_UPDATE],
  personal: [P.PROFILE_READ, P.PROFILE_UPDATE, P.INVITES_CREATE, P.INVITES_READ],
  admin: [
    P.PROFILE_READ, P.PROFILE_UPDATE,
    P.USERS_READ, P.USERS_MANAGE,
    P.ROLES_READ, P.ROLES_MANAGE,
    P.TENANTS_READ, P.AUDIT_READ,
  ],
};
```

`packages/authz/src/index.ts`:

```ts
export * from './permissions';
export * from './roles';
```

### Task 2: identity-service — scaffold, schema Prisma e seed RBAC

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/identity-service/package.json`, `tsconfig.json`, `tsconfig.build.json`, `vitest.config.ts`, `test/setup.ts`
- Create: `apps/identity-service/prisma/schema.prisma`, `prisma/seed.ts`
- Create: `apps/identity-service/src/prisma/prisma.service.ts`, `src/authorization/seed-rbac.ts`
- Test: `apps/identity-service/src/authorization/seed-rbac.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ROLE_PERMISSIONS`, `PERMISSIONS`, `RoleName` de `@aura/authz`.
- Produces: `PrismaService extends PrismaClient`; `seedRolesAndPermissions(prisma: PrismaClient): Promise<void>` (idempotente); modelos Prisma `User, Tenant, Membership, Role, Permission, RolePermission, Invite, AuditLog`.

Decisão registrada: refresh com rotação e detecção de reuso é **nativo do Keycloak** (`revokeRefreshToken=true`, `refreshTokenMaxReuse=0`); não há tabela de refresh token. A membership ativa da sessão fica no Redis (`session:{sid}:membership`), ver Task 6.

- [ ] **Step 1: Escrever o teste que falha**

`apps/identity-service/src/authorization/seed-rbac.test.ts`:

```ts
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';
import { ROLE_PERMISSIONS } from '@aura/authz';
import { afterAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { seedRolesAndPermissions } from './seed-rbac';

const prisma = new PrismaClient();
afterAll(() => prisma.$disconnect());

describe('seedRolesAndPermissions', () => {
  it('creates roles with the permissions from @aura/authz', async () => {
    await seedRolesAndPermissions(prisma);
    for (const [name, perms] of Object.entries(ROLE_PERMISSIONS)) {
      const role = await prisma.role.findUniqueOrThrow({
        where: { name },
        include: { permissions: { include: { permission: true } } },
      });
      expect(role.permissions.map((p) => p.permission.code).sort()).toEqual([...perms].sort());
    }
  });

  it('is idempotent', async () => {
    await seedRolesAndPermissions(prisma);
    await seedRolesAndPermissions(prisma);
    expect(await prisma.role.count()).toBe(3);
  });
});
```

`apps/identity-service/prisma/schema.prisma`:

```prisma
generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "postgresql"
  url      = env("DATABASE_URL_IDENTITY")
}

enum UserStatus {
  PENDING
  ACTIVE
  SUSPENDED
}

enum MembershipStatus {
  ACTIVE
  SUSPENDED
}

enum TenantStatus {
  ACTIVE
  SUSPENDED
}

model Role {
  id          Int              @id @default(autoincrement())
  name        String           @unique
  permissions RolePermission[]
  memberships Membership[]
}

model Permission {
  id    Int              @id @default(autoincrement())
  code  String           @unique
  roles RolePermission[]
}

model RolePermission {
  roleId       Int
  permissionId Int
  role         Role       @relation(fields: [roleId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  permission   Permission @relation(fields: [permissionId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  @@id([roleId, permissionId])
}

model User {
  id              String       @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  externalId      String       @unique
  email           String       @unique
  name            String
  phone           String?
  status          UserStatus   @default(PENDING)
  emailVerified   Boolean      @default(false)
  termsAcceptedAt DateTime?
  termsVersion    String?
  createdAt       DateTime     @default(now())
  memberships     Membership[]
}

model Tenant {
  id          String       @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  name        String
  slug        String       @unique
  status      TenantStatus @default(ACTIVE)
  createdAt   DateTime     @default(now())
  memberships Membership[]
  invites     Invite[]
}

// tenantId nulo = admin da plataforma. Postgres trata NULLs como distintos no unique,
// então a unicidade do admin é garantida no código (AccountsService).
model Membership {
  id       String           @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  userId   String           @db.Uuid
  tenantId String?          @db.Uuid
  roleId   Int
  status   MembershipStatus @default(ACTIVE)
  user     User             @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  tenant   Tenant?          @relation(fields: [tenantId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
  role     Role             @relation(fields: [roleId], references: [id])

  @@unique([userId, tenantId, roleId])
  @@index([userId])
}

model Invite {
  id                    String    @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  codeHash              String    @unique
  tenantId              String    @db.Uuid
  createdByMembershipId String    @db.Uuid
  expiresAt             DateTime
  usedAt                DateTime?
  usedByUserId          String?   @db.Uuid
  tenant                Tenant    @relation(fields: [tenantId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)
}

model AuditLog {
  id          String   @id @default(uuid()) @db.Uuid
  actorUserId String?  @db.Uuid
  action      String
  targetType  String?
  targetId    String?
  metadata    Json?
  ip          String?
  createdAt   DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([createdAt])
  @@index([actorUserId])
}
```

`apps/identity-service/src/authorization/seed-rbac.ts`:

```ts
import type { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';
import { PERMISSIONS, ROLE_PERMISSIONS } from '@aura/authz';

export async function seedRolesAndPermissions(prisma: PrismaClient): Promise<void> {
  for (const code of Object.values(PERMISSIONS)) {
    await prisma.permission.upsert({ where: { code }, update: {}, create: { code } });
  }
  for (const [name, codes] of Object.entries(ROLE_PERMISSIONS)) {
    const role = await prisma.role.upsert({ where: { name }, update: {}, create: { name } });
    const perms = await prisma.permission.findMany({ where: { code: { in: [...codes] } } });
    await prisma.rolePermission.deleteMany({ where: { roleId: role.id } });
    await prisma.rolePermission.createMany({
      data: perms.map((p) => ({ roleId: role.id, permissionId: p.id })),
    });
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Escrever a suíte de contrato e o teste que falha**

`provider.contract.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { IdentityProviderPort } from './identity-provider.port';
import { InvalidCredentialsError, InvalidRefreshTokenError, UserAlreadyExistsError } from './errors';

const uniq = () => `u${Date.now()}${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1e6)}@example.com`;

export function runProviderContract(name: string, make: () => Promise<IdentityProviderPort>) {
  describe(`IdentityProviderPort contract: ${name}`, () => {
    it('creates a user and authenticates with the right password', async () => {
      const p = await make();
      const email = uniq();
      const user = await p.createUser({ email, name: 'Rafael', password: 'Sup3r-secret-pw' });
      const tokens = await p.authenticate(email, 'Sup3r-secret-pw');
      expect(tokens.subject).toBe(user.externalId);
      expect(tokens.emailVerified).toBe(false);
      expect(tokens.accessToken).toBeTruthy();
    });

    it('creates a pre-verified user when asked (used only by the admin bootstrap)', async () => {
      const p = await make();
      const email = uniq();
      await p.createUser({ email, name: 'Admin', password: 'Sup3r-secret-pw', emailVerified: true });
      expect((await p.authenticate(email, 'Sup3r-secret-pw')).emailVerified).toBe(true);
    });

    it('rejects wrong password and unknown email with the same error', async () => {
      const p = await make();
      const email = uniq();
      await p.createUser({ email, name: 'A', password: 'Sup3r-secret-pw' });
      await expect(p.authenticate(email, 'nope')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidCredentialsError);
      await expect(p.authenticate(uniq(), 'nope')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidCredentialsError);
    });

    it('rejects duplicate e-mail', async () => {
      const p = await make();
      const email = uniq();
      await p.createUser({ email, name: 'A', password: 'Sup3r-secret-pw' });
      await expect(p.createUser({ email, name: 'B', password: 'Sup3r-secret-pw' }))
        .rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UserAlreadyExistsError);
    });

    it('rotates refresh tokens and revokes the family on reuse', async () => {
      const p = await make();
      const email = uniq();
      await p.createUser({ email, name: 'A', password: 'Sup3r-secret-pw' });
      const first = await p.authenticate(email, 'Sup3r-secret-pw');
      const second = await p.refresh(first.refreshToken);
      expect(second.refreshToken).not.toBe(first.refreshToken);
      await expect(p.refresh(first.refreshToken)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidRefreshTokenError);
      await expect(p.refresh(second.refreshToken)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidRefreshTokenError);
    });

    it('revokeSession invalidates the refresh token', async () => {
      const p = await make();
      const email = uniq();
      await p.createUser({ email, name: 'A', password: 'Sup3r-secret-pw' });
      const t = await p.authenticate(email, 'Sup3r-secret-pw');
      await p.revokeSession(t.refreshToken);
      await expect(p.refresh(t.refreshToken)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidRefreshTokenError);
    });

    it('password reset never reveals whether the e-mail exists', async () => {
      const p = await make();
      await expect(p.sendPasswordReset(uniq())).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    });

    it('deleteUser removes the account', async () => {
      const p = await make();
      const email = uniq();
      const u = await p.createUser({ email, name: 'A', password: 'Sup3r-secret-pw' });
      await p.deleteUser(u.externalId);
      await expect(p.authenticate(email, 'Sup3r-secret-pw')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidCredentialsError);
    });
  });
}
```

`identity-provider.port.ts`:

```ts
export const IDENTITY_PROVIDER = Symbol('IDENTITY_PROVIDER');

// id do provedor = alias do Identity Provider no Keycloak. Quem pode ser usado é decidido por config
// (SOCIAL_PROVIDERS, hoje só "google"), não por tipo: incluir outro provedor não exige mudar a porta.
export type SocialProvider = string;

export interface TokenSet {
  accessToken: string;
  refreshToken: string;
  expiresIn: number;
  sessionId: string;
  subject: string;
  email: string;
  emailVerified: boolean;
}

export interface ProviderUser {
  externalId: string;
  email: string;
  name: string;
  emailVerified: boolean;
}

export interface IdentityProviderPort {
  createUser(input: { email: string; name: string; password: string; emailVerified?: boolean }): Promise<ProviderUser>;
  deleteUser(externalId: string): Promise<void>;
  authenticate(email: string, password: string): Promise<TokenSet>;
  refresh(refreshToken: string): Promise<TokenSet>;
  revokeSession(refreshToken: string): Promise<void>;
  sendVerificationEmail(externalId: string): Promise<void>;
  sendPasswordReset(email: string): Promise<void>;
  buildSocialLoginUrl(input: {
    provider: SocialProvider; redirectUri: string; state: string; codeChallenge: string;
  }): string;
  exchangeAuthorizationCode(input: {
    code: string; redirectUri: string; codeVerifier: string;
  }): Promise<TokenSet>;
}
```

`in-memory.provider.ts`:

```ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { IdentityProviderPort, ProviderUser, SocialProvider, TokenSet } from './identity-provider.port';
import { InvalidCredentialsError, InvalidRefreshTokenError, UserAlreadyExistsError } from './errors';

interface Account extends ProviderUser { password: string }
interface Family { sessionId: string; current: string; alive: boolean; subject: string }

export class InMemoryIdentityProvider implements IdentityProviderPort {
  private accounts = new Map<string, Account>();
  private byEmail = new Map<string, string>();
  private refreshIndex = new Map<string, Family>();
  private families = new Map<string, Family>();
  readonly sentVerification: string[] = [];
  readonly sentReset: string[] = [];

  async createUser(i: { email: string; name: string; password: string; emailVerified?: boolean }): Promise<ProviderUser> {
    const email = i.email.toLowerCase();
    if (this.byEmail.has(email)) throw new UserAlreadyExistsError();
    const acc: Account = { externalId: randomUUID(), email, name: i.name, emailVerified: i.emailVerified ?? false, password: i.password };
    this.accounts.set(acc.externalId, acc);
    this.byEmail.set(email, acc.externalId);
    return { externalId: acc.externalId, email, name: i.name, emailVerified: acc.emailVerified };
  }

  async deleteUser(externalId: string): Promise<void> {
    const acc = this.accounts.get(externalId);
    if (acc) this.byEmail.delete(acc.email);
    this.accounts.delete(externalId);
  }

  markEmailVerified(externalId: string) {
    const acc = this.accounts.get(externalId);
    if (acc) acc.emailVerified = true;
  }

  async authenticate(email: string, password: string): Promise<TokenSet> {
    const id = this.byEmail.get(email.toLowerCase());
    const acc = id ? this.accounts.get(id) : undefined;
    if (!acc || acc.password !== password) throw new InvalidCredentialsError();
    return this.issue(acc, randomUUID());
  }

  async refresh(refreshToken: string): Promise<TokenSet> {
    const fam = this.refreshIndex.get(refreshToken);
    if (!fam) throw new InvalidRefreshTokenError();
    if (!fam.alive || fam.current !== refreshToken) {
      fam.alive = false; // reuso: revoga a família inteira
      throw new InvalidRefreshTokenError();
    }
    const acc = this.accounts.get(fam.subject);
    if (!acc) throw new InvalidRefreshTokenError();
    return this.issue(acc, fam.sessionId);
  }

  async revokeSession(refreshToken: string): Promise<void> {
    const fam = this.refreshIndex.get(refreshToken);
    if (fam) fam.alive = false;
  }

  async sendVerificationEmail(externalId: string): Promise<void> { this.sentVerification.push(externalId); }
  async sendPasswordReset(email: string): Promise<void> { this.sentReset.push(email.toLowerCase()); }

  buildSocialLoginUrl(i: { provider: SocialProvider; redirectUri: string; state: string; codeChallenge: string }): string {
    return `https://idp.test/auth?provider=${i.provider}&state=${i.state}&cc=${i.codeChallenge}`;
  }

  async exchangeAuthorizationCode(): Promise<TokenSet> {
    throw new InvalidCredentialsError();
  }

  private issue(acc: Account, sessionId: string): TokenSet {
    const refreshToken = randomUUID();
    const fam = this.families.get(sessionId)
      ?? { sessionId, current: refreshToken, alive: true, subject: acc.externalId };
    fam.current = refreshToken;
    this.families.set(sessionId, fam);
    this.refreshIndex.set(refreshToken, fam);
    return {
      accessToken: `at-${randomUUID()}`,
      refreshToken,
      expiresIn: 600,
      sessionId,
      subject: acc.externalId,
      email: acc.email,
      emailVerified: acc.emailVerified,
    };
  }
}
```

`keycloak.provider.ts`:

```ts
import type { IdentityProviderPort, ProviderUser, SocialProvider, TokenSet } from './identity-provider.port';
import { InvalidCredentialsError, InvalidRefreshTokenError, UserAlreadyExistsError } from './errors';

export interface KeycloakConfig {
  baseUrl: string;
  realm: string;
  clientId: string;
  clientSecret: string;
  webClientId: string;
}

const FORM = { 'content-type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' };

export class KeycloakProvider implements IdentityProviderPort {
  constructor(private readonly cfg: KeycloakConfig) {}

  private get realmUrl() { return `${this.cfg.baseUrl}/realms/${this.cfg.realm}`; }
  private get adminUrl() { return `${this.cfg.baseUrl}/admin/realms/${this.cfg.realm}`; }
  private get tokenUrl() { return `${this.realmUrl}/protocol/openid-connect/token`; }

  private async adminToken(): Promise<string> {
    const res = await fetch(this.tokenUrl, {
      method: 'POST', headers: FORM,
      body: new URLSearchParams({
        grant_type: 'client_credentials', client_id: this.cfg.clientId, client_secret: this.cfg.clientSecret,
      }),
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('keycloak_admin_auth_failed');
    return ((await res.json()) as { access_token: string }).access_token;
  }

  private async admin(path: string, init: RequestInit = {}): Promise<Response> {
    const token = await this.adminToken();
    return fetch(`${this.adminUrl}${path}`, {
      ...init,
      headers: { ...(init.headers ?? {}), authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'content-type': 'application/json' },
    });
  }

  async createUser(i: { email: string; name: string; password: string; emailVerified?: boolean }): Promise<ProviderUser> {
    const email = i.email.toLowerCase();
    const [firstName, ...rest] = i.name.trim().split(' ');
    const res = await this.admin('/users', {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify({
        username: email, email, firstName, lastName: rest.join(' ') || '-',
        enabled: true, emailVerified: i.emailVerified ?? false,
        credentials: [{ type: 'password', value: i.password, temporary: false }],
      }),
    });
    if (res.status === 409) throw new UserAlreadyExistsError();
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`keycloak_create_user_failed:${res.status}`);
    const externalId = res.headers.get('location')!.split('/').pop()!;
    return { externalId, email, name: i.name, emailVerified: i.emailVerified ?? false };
  }

  async deleteUser(externalId: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.admin(`/users/${externalId}`, { method: 'DELETE' });
  }

  async authenticate(email: string, password: string): Promise<TokenSet> {
    const res = await fetch(this.tokenUrl, {
      method: 'POST', headers: FORM,
      body: new URLSearchParams({
        grant_type: 'password', client_id: this.cfg.clientId, client_secret: this.cfg.clientSecret,
        username: email.toLowerCase(), password, scope: 'openid email profile',
      }),
    });
    if (res.status === 400 || res.status === 401) throw new InvalidCredentialsError();
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`keycloak_token_failed:${res.status}`);
    return this.toTokenSet(await res.json());
  }

  async refresh(refreshToken: string): Promise<TokenSet> {
    const res = await fetch(this.tokenUrl, {
      method: 'POST', headers: FORM,
      body: new URLSearchParams({
        grant_type: 'refresh_token', client_id: this.cfg.clientId,
        client_secret: this.cfg.clientSecret, refresh_token: refreshToken,
      }),
    });
    if (res.status === 400 || res.status === 401) throw new InvalidRefreshTokenError();
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`keycloak_refresh_failed:${res.status}`);
    return this.toTokenSet(await res.json());
  }

  async revokeSession(refreshToken: string): Promise<void> {
    await fetch(`${this.realmUrl}/protocol/openid-connect/logout`, {
      method: 'POST', headers: FORM,
      body: new URLSearchParams({
        client_id: this.cfg.clientId, client_secret: this.cfg.clientSecret, refresh_token: refreshToken,
      }),
    });
  }

  async sendVerificationEmail(externalId: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.admin(`/users/${externalId}/execute-actions-email?lifespan=86400`, {
      method: 'PUT', body: JSON.stringify(['VERIFY_EMAIL']),
    });
  }

  async sendPasswordReset(email: string): Promise<void> {
    const res = await this.admin(`/users?email=${encodeURIComponent(email.toLowerCase())}&exact=true`);
    const users = (await res.json()) as { id: string }[];
    if (!users[0]) return; // resposta sempre genérica
    await this.admin(`/users/${users[0].id}/execute-actions-email?lifespan=900`, {
      method: 'PUT', body: JSON.stringify(['UPDATE_PASSWORD']),
    });
  }

  buildSocialLoginUrl(i: { provider: SocialProvider; redirectUri: string; state: string; codeChallenge: string }): string {
    const q = new URLSearchParams({
      client_id: this.cfg.webClientId, response_type: 'code', scope: 'openid email profile',
      redirect_uri: i.redirectUri, state: i.state,
      code_challenge: i.codeChallenge, code_challenge_method: 'S256', kc_idp_hint: i.provider,
    });
    return `${this.realmUrl}/protocol/openid-connect/auth?${q}`;
  }

  async exchangeAuthorizationCode(i: { code: string; redirectUri: string; codeVerifier: string }): Promise<TokenSet> {
    const res = await fetch(this.tokenUrl, {
      method: 'POST', headers: FORM,
      body: new URLSearchParams({
        grant_type: 'authorization_code', client_id: this.cfg.webClientId,
        code: i.code, redirect_uri: i.redirectUri, code_verifier: i.codeVerifier,
      }),
    });
    if (!res.ok) throw new InvalidCredentialsError();
    return this.toTokenSet(await res.json());
  }

  private toTokenSet(body: { access_token: string; refresh_token: string; expires_in: number }): TokenSet {
    // Token vindo direto do Keycloak no back-channel: só lemos claims. A verificação de assinatura é do BFF.
    const claims = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(body.access_token.split('.')[1], 'base64url').toString()) as {
      sub: string; sid: string; email: string; email_verified: boolean;
    };
    return {
      accessToken: body.access_token, refreshToken: body.refresh_token, expiresIn: body.expires_in,
      sessionId: claims.sid, subject: claims.sub, email: claims.email, emailVerified: claims.email_verified,
    };
  }
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { signContext, verifyContext, type InternalContext } from './sign';

const secret = 'a'.repeat(32);
const ctx: InternalContext = {
  userId: 'u1', tenantId: 't1', membershipId: 'm1', role: 'personal',
  permissions: ['profile:read'], correlationId: 'c1',
};

describe('internal context', () => {
  it('round-trips a signed context', () => {
    expect(verifyContext(signContext(ctx, secret), secret)).toMatchObject(ctx);
  });
  it('rejects a tampered payload', () => {
    const [p, s] = signContext(ctx, secret).split('.');
    const forged = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ ...ctx, role: 'admin', exp: Date.now() + 60000 })).toString('base64url');
    expect(verifyContext(`${forged}.${s}`, secret)).toBeNull();
    expect(p).toBeTruthy();
  });
  it('rejects a wrong secret', () => {
    expect(verifyContext(signContext(ctx, secret), 'b'.repeat(32))).toBeNull();
  });
  it('rejects an expired token', () => {
    const t = signContext(ctx, secret, 1_000, 60_000);
    expect(verifyContext(t, secret, 1_000 + 60_001)).toBeNull();
  });
  it('refuses to sign with a short secret', () => {
    expect(() => signContext(ctx, 'short')).toThrow();
  });
});
```

`guard.test.ts`:

```ts
import { ForbiddenException, UnauthorizedException, type ExecutionContext } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { AuthContextGuard } from './guard';
import { PERMISSION_KEY, PUBLIC_KEY } from './decorators';
import { INTERNAL_CONTEXT_HEADER } from './sign';
import { signContext, type InternalContext } from './sign';

const secret = 'a'.repeat(32);
const ctx: InternalContext = {
  userId: 'u', tenantId: 't', membershipId: 'm', role: 'aluno',
  permissions: ['profile:read'], correlationId: 'c',
};

function exec(headers: Record<string, string>, meta: Record<string, unknown>) {
  const req: any = { headers };
  const handler = () => undefined;
  const reflector = { getAllAndOverride: (k: string) => meta[k] } as unknown as Reflector;
  const guard = new AuthContextGuard(reflector, secret);
  const context = {
    switchToHttp: () => ({ getRequest: () => req }),
    getHandler: () => handler,
    getClass: () => class {},
  } as unknown as ExecutionContext;
  return { run: () => guard.canActivate(context), req };
}

describe('AuthContextGuard', () => {
  it('lets @Public routes through', () => {
    expect(exec({}, { [PUBLIC_KEY]: true }).run()).toBe(true);
  });
  it('401 without context header', () => {
    expect(() => exec({}, {}).run()).toThrow(UnauthorizedException);
  });
  it('401 with forged context', () => {
    expect(() => exec({ [INTERNAL_CONTEXT_HEADER]: 'x.y' }, {}).run()).toThrow(UnauthorizedException);
  });
  it('403 when a required permission is missing', () => {
    const h = { [INTERNAL_CONTEXT_HEADER]: signContext(ctx, secret) };
    expect(() => exec(h, { [PERMISSION_KEY]: ['invites:create'] }).run()).toThrow(ForbiddenException);
  });
  it('passes and attaches ctx when permission present', () => {
    const h = { [INTERNAL_CONTEXT_HEADER]: signContext(ctx, secret) };
    const e = exec(h, { [PERMISSION_KEY]: ['profile:read'] });
    expect(e.run()).toBe(true);
    expect(e.req.authContext.userId).toBe('u');
  });
});
```

`src/sign.ts`:

```ts
import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';
import type { RoleName } from '@aura/authz';

export const INTERNAL_CONTEXT_HEADER = 'x-internal-context';

export interface InternalContext {
  userId: string;
  tenantId: string | null;
  membershipId: string;
  role: RoleName;
  permissions: string[];
  correlationId: string;
}

const mac = (payload: string, secret: string) =>
  createHmac('sha256', secret).update(payload).digest('base64url');

export function signContext(ctx: InternalContext, secret: string, now = Date.now(), ttlMs = 60_000): string {
  if (secret.length < 32) throw new Error('internal context secret must be >= 32 chars');
  const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ ...ctx, exp: now + ttlMs })).toString('base64url');
  return `${payload}.${mac(payload, secret)}`;
}

export function verifyContext(token: string, secret: string, now = Date.now()): InternalContext | null {
  const [payload, sig] = token.split('.');
  if (!payload || !sig || secret.length < 32) return null;
  const expected = Buffer.from(mac(payload, secret));
  const given = Buffer.from(sig);
  if (expected.length !== given.length || !timingSafeEqual(expected, given)) return null;
  try {
    const { exp, ...ctx } = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(payload, 'base64url').toString()) as InternalContext & { exp: number };
    return exp > now ? ctx : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}
```

`src/decorators.ts`:

```ts
import { createParamDecorator, SetMetadata, type ExecutionContext } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { Permission } from '@aura/authz';
import type { InternalContext } from './sign';

export const PUBLIC_KEY = 'auth:public';
export const PERMISSION_KEY = 'auth:permissions';

export const Public = () => SetMetadata(PUBLIC_KEY, true);
export const RequirePermission = (...perms: Permission[]) => SetMetadata(PERMISSION_KEY, perms);
export const CurrentContext = createParamDecorator(
  (_: unknown, ctx: ExecutionContext): InternalContext => ctx.switchToHttp().getRequest().authContext,
);
```

`src/guard.ts`:

```ts
import {
  CanActivate, DynamicModule, ExecutionContext, ForbiddenException, Inject, Injectable, Module, UnauthorizedException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { APP_GUARD, Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';
import { PERMISSION_KEY, PUBLIC_KEY } from './decorators';
import { INTERNAL_CONTEXT_HEADER, verifyContext } from './sign';

export const INTERNAL_CONTEXT_SECRET = 'INTERNAL_CONTEXT_SECRET';

@Injectable()
export class AuthContextGuard implements CanActivate {
  constructor(
    private readonly reflector: Reflector,
    @Inject(INTERNAL_CONTEXT_SECRET) private readonly secret: string,
  ) {}

  canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): boolean {
    const targets = [context.getHandler(), context.getClass()];
    if (this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<boolean>(PUBLIC_KEY, targets)) return true;

    const req = context.switchToHttp().getRequest();
    const raw = req.headers[INTERNAL_CONTEXT_HEADER];
    const ctx = typeof raw === 'string' ? verifyContext(raw, this.secret) : null;
    if (!ctx) throw new UnauthorizedException({ code: 'unauthorized', message: 'Unauthorized' });

    const required = this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<string[]>(PERMISSION_KEY, targets) ?? [];
    if (!required.every((p) => ctx.permissions.includes(p))) {
      throw new ForbiddenException({ code: 'forbidden', message: 'Access denied' });
    }
    req.authContext = ctx;
    return true;
  }
}

@Module({})
export class AuthContextModule {
  static forRoot(secret: string): DynamicModule {
    return {
      module: AuthContextModule,
      providers: [
        { provide: INTERNAL_CONTEXT_SECRET, useValue: secret },
        { provide: APP_GUARD, useClass: AuthContextGuard },
      ],
    };
  }
}
```

```ts
export * from './decorators';
export * from './guard';
```

```ts
describe('registerPersonal', () => {

  });

describe('invites and registerAluno', () => {

    await svc.registerPersonal(personal('owner@example.com'));

    const m = u.memberships[0];

    acceptTerms: true as const, termsVersion: '2025-04-12',

    await expect(svc.registerAluno(aluno('nope', 'f@example.com'))).rejects.toMatchObject({ response: { code: 'invalid_invite' } });

  it('never stores the invite code in clear text', async () => {
    const ctx = await tenantOwnerCtx();
    const { code } = await svc.createInvite(ctx, 48);
    const inv = await prisma.invite.findFirstOrThrow();
    expect(inv.codeHash).not.toContain(code);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';

export const TERMS_VERSION = '2025-04-12';

const password = z.string().min(10, 'min_10').max(128);
const base = {
  email: z.string().email().max(254).transform((v) => v.toLowerCase()),
  name: z.string().trim().min(2).max(120),
  phone: z.string().regex(/^\d{10,13}$/, 'digits_only').optional(),
  password,
  acceptTerms: z.literal(true),
  termsVersion: z.string().min(1).max(20),
};

export const registerPersonalSchema = z.object(base);
export const registerAlunoSchema = z.object({ ...base, code: z.string().min(8).max(64) });
export const createInviteSchema = z.object({ ttlHours: z.number().int().min(1).max(168).default(48) });
export const loginSchema = z.object({ email: base.email, password: z.string().min(1).max(128) });
export const forgotPasswordSchema = z.object({ email: base.email });
export const selectMembershipSchema = z.object({ membershipId: z.string().uuid() });
export const updateMeSchema = z.object({
  name: z.string().trim().min(2).max(120).optional(),
  phone: z.string().regex(/^\d{10,13}$/).nullable().optional(),
});
export const setPermissionsSchema = z.object({ permissions: z.array(z.string().max(64)).max(100) });
export const setStatusSchema = z.object({ status: z.enum(['ACTIVE', 'SUSPENDED']) });

export type RegisterPersonalInput = z.infer<typeof registerPersonalSchema>;
export type RegisterAlunoInput = z.infer<typeof registerAlunoSchema>;
export type LoginInput = z.infer<typeof loginSchema>;
```

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { Prisma } from '@prisma/client';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';

@Injectable()
export class AuditService {
  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}

  async log(
    action: string,
    data: { actorUserId?: string; targetType?: string; targetId?: string; metadata?: Record<string, unknown>; ip?: string } = {},
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.prisma.auditLog.create({
      data: { action, ...data, metadata: data.metadata as Prisma.InputJsonValue | undefined },
    });
  }
}
```

`apps/identity-service/src/shared/zod-validation.pipe.ts`:

```ts
import { BadRequestException, PipeTransform } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { ZodSchema } from 'zod';

export class ZodValidationPipe implements PipeTransform {
  constructor(private readonly schema: ZodSchema) {}
  transform(value: unknown) {
    const r = this.schema.safeParse(value);
    if (r.success) return r.data;
    // só caminhos e códigos, nunca os valores enviados
    throw new BadRequestException({
      code: 'validation_error',
      message: 'Invalid request',
      fields: r.error.issues.map((i) => ({ path: i.path.join('.'), code: i.message })),
    });
  }
}
```

`apps/identity-service/src/accounts/accounts.service.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash, randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { BadRequestException, Inject, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { RegisterAlunoInput, RegisterPersonalInput } from '@aura/schemas';
import type { InternalContext } from '@aura/internal-context';
import { IDENTITY_PROVIDER, type IdentityProviderPort } from '../identity-provider/identity-provider.port';
import { UserAlreadyExistsError } from '../identity-provider/errors';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { AuditService } from '../shared/audit.service';

const PENDING = { status: 'pending_verification' } as const;
const sha256 = (v: string) => createHash('sha256').update(v).digest('hex');
const slugify = (name: string) =>
  name.toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, '');

@Injectable()
export class AccountsService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    @Inject(IDENTITY_PROVIDER) private readonly provider: IdentityProviderPort,
    private readonly audit: AuditService,
  ) {}

  async registerPersonal(input: RegisterPersonalInput, ip?: string) {
    const created = await this.createProviderUser(input);
    if (!created) return PENDING; // e-mail já existente: mesma resposta, sem vazar
    const role = await this.prisma.role.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { name: 'personal' } });
    try {
      await this.prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
        const user = await tx.user.create({
          data: {
            externalId: created.externalId, email: created.email, name: input.name, phone: input.phone,
            termsAcceptedAt: new Date(), termsVersion: input.termsVersion,
          },
        });
        const tenant = await tx.tenant.create({
          data: { name: input.name, slug: `${slugify(input.name)}-${randomBytes(3).toString('hex')}` },
        });
        await tx.membership.create({ data: { userId: user.id, tenantId: tenant.id, roleId: role.id } });
      });
    } catch (e) {
      await this.provider.deleteUser(created.externalId); // compensação
      throw e;
    }
    await this.provider.sendVerificationEmail(created.externalId);
    await this.audit.log('account.registered', { targetType: 'user', targetId: created.externalId, ip, metadata: { role: 'personal' } });
    return PENDING;
  }

  async createInvite(ctx: InternalContext, ttlHours: number) {
    const code = randomBytes(16).toString('base64url');
    const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + ttlHours * 3_600_000);
    await this.prisma.invite.create({
      data: { codeHash: sha256(code), tenantId: ctx.tenantId!, createdByMembershipId: ctx.membershipId, expiresAt },
    });
    await this.audit.log('invite.created', { actorUserId: ctx.userId, targetType: 'tenant', targetId: ctx.tenantId! });
    return { code, expiresAt };
  }

  async registerAluno(input: RegisterAlunoInput, ip?: string) {
    const invite = await this.prisma.invite.findUnique({ where: { codeHash: sha256(input.code) } });
    if (!invite || invite.usedAt || invite.expiresAt < new Date()) throw this.invalidInvite();

    const created = await this.createProviderUser(input);
    if (!created) return PENDING;
    const role = await this.prisma.role.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { name: 'aluno' } });
    try {
      await this.prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
        // consumo atômico: só um cadastro vence a corrida pelo mesmo convite
        const claimed = await tx.invite.updateMany({ where: { id: invite.id, usedAt: null }, data: { usedAt: new Date() } });
        if (claimed.count !== 1) throw this.invalidInvite();
        const user = await tx.user.create({
          data: {
            externalId: created.externalId, email: created.email, name: input.name, phone: input.phone,
            termsAcceptedAt: new Date(), termsVersion: input.termsVersion,
          },
        });
        await tx.invite.update({ where: { id: invite.id }, data: { usedByUserId: user.id } });
        await tx.membership.create({ data: { userId: user.id, tenantId: invite.tenantId, roleId: role.id } });
      });
    } catch (e) {
      await this.provider.deleteUser(created.externalId);
      throw e;
    }
    await this.provider.sendVerificationEmail(created.externalId);
    await this.audit.log('account.registered', { targetType: 'user', targetId: created.externalId, ip, metadata: { role: 'aluno' } });
    return PENDING;
  }

  private async createProviderUser(i: { email: string; name: string; password: string }) {
    try {
      return await this.provider.createUser({ email: i.email, name: i.name, password: i.password });
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof UserAlreadyExistsError) return null;
      throw e;
    }
  }

  private invalidInvite() {
    return new BadRequestException({ code: 'invalid_invite', message: 'Invalid or expired invite' });
  }
}
```

```ts
import { Body, Controller, Ip, Post, UsePipes } from '@nestjs/common';
import { createInviteSchema, registerAlunoSchema, registerPersonalSchema } from '@aura/schemas';
import { CurrentContext, InternalContext, Public, RequirePermission } from '@aura/internal-context';
import { ZodValidationPipe } from '../shared/zod-validation.pipe';
import { AccountsService } from './accounts.service';

@Controller()
export class AccountsController {
  constructor(private readonly accounts: AccountsService) {}

  @Public()
  @Post('accounts/register')
  register(@Body(new ZodValidationPipe(registerPersonalSchema)) body: any, @Ip() ip: string) {
    return this.accounts.registerPersonal(body, ip);
  }

  @Public()
  @Post('accounts/register-aluno')
  registerAluno(@Body(new ZodValidationPipe(registerAlunoSchema)) body: any, @Ip() ip: string) {
    return this.accounts.registerAluno(body, ip);
  }

  @RequirePermission('invites:create')
  @Post('invites')
  invite(@CurrentContext() ctx: InternalContext, @Body(new ZodValidationPipe(createInviteSchema)) body: { ttlHours: number }) {
    return this.accounts.createInvite(ctx, body.ttlHours);
  }
}
```

```ts
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';
import { ForbiddenException, UnauthorizedException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { seedRolesAndPermissions } from '../authorization/seed-rbac';
import { InMemoryIdentityProvider } from '../identity-provider/in-memory.provider';
import { InvalidRefreshTokenError } from '../identity-provider/errors';
import { AuditService } from '../shared/audit.service';
import { InMemoryKeyValueStore } from '../shared/kv/in-memory.kv';
import { LoginThrottle } from './login-throttle';
import { SessionsService } from './sessions.service';
import { sessionMembershipKey } from '../shared/kv/kv.port';

const prisma = new PrismaClient();
const PW = 'Sup3r-secret-pw';
let provider: InMemoryIdentityProvider;
let kv: InMemoryKeyValueStore;
let svc: SessionsService;

async function makeUser(email: string, roles: Array<{ role: 'personal' | 'aluno'; tenant: string }>, verified = true) {
  const acc = await provider.createUser({ email, name: 'Fulano', password: PW });
  if (verified) provider.markEmailVerified(acc.externalId);
  const user = await prisma.user.create({ data: { externalId: acc.externalId, email, name: 'Fulano', status: 'ACTIVE', emailVerified: verified } });
  for (const r of roles) {
    const tenant = await prisma.tenant.create({ data: { name: r.tenant, slug: `${r.tenant}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}` } });
    const role = await prisma.role.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { name: r.role } });
    await prisma.membership.create({ data: { userId: user.id, tenantId: tenant.id, roleId: role.id } });
  }
  return { acc, user };
}

beforeAll(async () => { await seedRolesAndPermissions(prisma); });
afterAll(() => prisma.$disconnect());
beforeEach(async () => {
  await prisma.membership.deleteMany(); await prisma.tenant.deleteMany(); await prisma.user.deleteMany();
  provider = new InMemoryIdentityProvider();
  kv = new InMemoryKeyValueStore();
  svc = new SessionsService(prisma as any, provider, new AuditService(prisma as any), kv, new LoginThrottle(kv));
});

describe('login', () => {
  it('single membership: activates it for the session', async () => {
    await makeUser('a@x.com', [{ role: 'personal', tenant: 'Rafael' }]);
    const r = await svc.login('a@x.com', PW, '1.1.1.1');
    expect(r.memberships).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(r.activeMembershipId).toBe(r.memberships[0].id);
    expect(r.tokens.accessToken).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('several memberships: requires explicit selection', async () => {
    await makeUser('b@x.com', [{ role: 'personal', tenant: 'T1' }, { role: 'aluno', tenant: 'T2' }]);
    const r = await svc.login('b@x.com', PW);
    expect(r.activeMembershipId).toBeNull();
    expect(r.memberships).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('unverified e-mail: 403 email_not_verified and the session is revoked', async () => {
    await makeUser('c@x.com', [{ role: 'aluno', tenant: 'T' }], false);
    await expect(svc.login('c@x.com', PW)).rejects.toMatchObject({ response: { code: 'email_not_verified' } });
    await expect(svc.login('c@x.com', PW)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenException);
  });

  it('syncs emailVerified locally after verification', async () => {
    const { acc, user } = await makeUser('d@x.com', [{ role: 'aluno', tenant: 'T' }], false);
    provider.markEmailVerified(acc.externalId);
    await svc.login('d@x.com', PW);
    expect((await prisma.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: user.id } })).emailVerified).toBe(true);
  });

  it('wrong password and unknown e-mail give the same 401', async () => {
    await makeUser('e@x.com', [{ role: 'aluno', tenant: 'T' }]);
    const a = await svc.login('e@x.com', 'wrong').catch((e) => e);
    const b = await svc.login('nobody@x.com', 'wrong').catch((e) => e);
    expect(a).toBeInstanceOf(UnauthorizedException);
    expect(a.getResponse()).toEqual(b.getResponse());
  });

  it('locks out after 5 failures even with the right password', async () => {
    await makeUser('f@x.com', [{ role: 'aluno', tenant: 'T' }]);
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) await svc.login('f@x.com', 'wrong', '9.9.9.9').catch(() => undefined);
    await expect(svc.login('f@x.com', PW, '9.9.9.9')).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 429 });
  });

  it('suspended users cannot log in (generic 401)', async () => {
    const { user } = await makeUser('g@x.com', [{ role: 'aluno', tenant: 'T' }]);
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: user.id }, data: { status: 'SUSPENDED' } });
    await expect(svc.login('g@x.com', PW)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UnauthorizedException);
  });

  it('ignores suspended memberships', async () => {
    const { user } = await makeUser('h@x.com', [{ role: 'personal', tenant: 'A' }, { role: 'aluno', tenant: 'B' }]);
    const first = await prisma.membership.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { userId: user.id } });
    await prisma.membership.update({ where: { id: first.id }, data: { status: 'SUSPENDED' } });
    const r = await svc.login('h@x.com', PW);
    expect(r.memberships).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});

describe('selectMembership', () => {
  it('stores the active membership for the session', async () => {
    await makeUser('i@x.com', [{ role: 'personal', tenant: 'T1' }, { role: 'aluno', tenant: 'T2' }]);
    const login = await svc.login('i@x.com', PW);
    const t = await provider.refresh(login.tokens.refreshToken); // só para obter o sessionId estável
    const subject = t.subject; const sessionId = t.sessionId;
    await svc.selectMembership({ subject, sessionId, membershipId: login.memberships[1].id });
    expect(await kv.get(sessionMembershipKey(sessionId))).toBe(login.memberships[1].id);
  });

  it("refuses another user's membership (IDOR) with 403", async () => {
    await makeUser('j@x.com', [{ role: 'aluno', tenant: 'T1' }]);
    const other = await makeUser('k@x.com', [{ role: 'aluno', tenant: 'T2' }]);
    const otherM = await prisma.membership.findFirstOrThrow({ where: { userId: other.user.id } });
    const login = await svc.login('j@x.com', PW);
    const t = await provider.refresh(login.tokens.refreshToken);
    await expect(svc.selectMembership({ subject: t.subject, sessionId: t.sessionId, membershipId: otherM.id }))
      .rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ForbiddenException);
  });
});

describe('refresh and logout', () => {
  it('rotates and rejects reuse', async () => {
    await makeUser('l@x.com', [{ role: 'aluno', tenant: 'T' }]);
    const { tokens } = await svc.login('l@x.com', PW);
    const next = await svc.refresh(tokens.refreshToken);
    expect(next.refreshToken).not.toBe(tokens.refreshToken);
    await expect(svc.refresh(tokens.refreshToken)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UnauthorizedException);
  });

  it('logout revokes the session', async () => {
    await makeUser('m@x.com', [{ role: 'aluno', tenant: 'T' }]);
    const { tokens } = await svc.login('m@x.com', PW);
    await svc.logout(tokens.refreshToken);
    await expect(provider.refresh(tokens.refreshToken)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidRefreshTokenError);
  });

  it('refuses refresh for a user suspended after login', async () => {
    const { user } = await makeUser('n@x.com', [{ role: 'aluno', tenant: 'T' }]);
    const { tokens } = await svc.login('n@x.com', PW);
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: user.id }, data: { status: 'SUSPENDED' } });
    await expect(svc.refresh(tokens.refreshToken)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(UnauthorizedException);
  });
});

describe('forgotPassword and resendVerification', () => {
  it('forgotPassword resolves for unknown e-mails and forwards for known ones', async () => {
    await makeUser('o@x.com', [{ role: 'aluno', tenant: 'T' }]);
    await expect(svc.forgotPassword('ghost@x.com')).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    await svc.forgotPassword('o@x.com');
    expect(provider.sentReset).toContain('o@x.com');
  });

  it('resendVerification sends at most once per minute', async () => {
    const { acc } = await makeUser('p@x.com', [{ role: 'aluno', tenant: 'T' }], false);
    await svc.resendVerification('p@x.com');
    await svc.resendVerification('p@x.com');
    expect(provider.sentVerification.filter((id) => id === acc.externalId)).toHaveLength(1);
    await expect(svc.resendVerification('ghost@x.com')).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

```ts
export const KV_STORE = Symbol('KV_STORE');
export const SESSION_TTL_SEC = 604_800;
export const sessionMembershipKey = (sessionId: string) => `session:${sessionId}:membership`;

export interface KeyValueStore {
  get(key: string): Promise<string | null>;
  set(key: string, value: string, ttlSec?: number): Promise<void>;
  incr(key: string, ttlSec?: number): Promise<number>;
  del(key: string): Promise<void>;
}
```

`src/shared/kv/in-memory.kv.ts`:

```ts
import type { KeyValueStore } from './kv.port';

export class InMemoryKeyValueStore implements KeyValueStore {
  private data = new Map<string, { v: string; exp?: number }>();
  constructor(private now: () => number = Date.now) {}

  private live(key: string) {
    const e = this.data.get(key);
    if (e?.exp && e.exp <= this.now()) { this.data.delete(key); return undefined; }
    return e;
  }
  async get(key: string) { return this.live(key)?.v ?? null; }
  async set(key: string, v: string, ttlSec?: number) {
    this.data.set(key, { v, exp: ttlSec ? this.now() + ttlSec * 1000 : undefined });
  }
  async incr(key: string, ttlSec?: number) {
    const cur = this.live(key);
    const n = (cur ? Number(cur.v) : 0) + 1;
    this.data.set(key, { v: String(n), exp: cur?.exp ?? (ttlSec ? this.now() + ttlSec * 1000 : undefined) });
    return n;
  }
  async del(key: string) { this.data.delete(key); }
}
```

`src/shared/kv/redis.kv.ts`:

```ts
import type Redis from 'ioredis';
import type { KeyValueStore } from './kv.port';

export class RedisKeyValueStore implements KeyValueStore {
  constructor(private readonly redis: Redis) {}
  get(key: string) { return this.redis.get(key); }
  async set(key: string, value: string, ttlSec?: number) {
    if (ttlSec) await this.redis.set(key, value, 'EX', ttlSec); else await this.redis.set(key, value);
  }
  async incr(key: string, ttlSec?: number) {
    const n = await this.redis.incr(key);
    if (n === 1 && ttlSec) await this.redis.expire(key, ttlSec);
    return n;
  }
  async del(key: string) { await this.redis.del(key); }
}
```

`src/sessions/login-throttle.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { HttpException, HttpStatus, Inject, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { KV_STORE, type KeyValueStore } from '../shared/kv/kv.port';

const h = (v: string) => createHash('sha256').update(v.toLowerCase()).digest('hex').slice(0, 32);
const WINDOW = 900;
const PAIR_LIMIT = 5;   // e-mail + IP
const IP_LIMIT = 30;    // IP sozinho (evita enumeração em massa sem travar a vítima de outro IP)

@Injectable()
export class LoginThrottle {
  constructor(@Inject(KV_STORE) private readonly kv: KeyValueStore) {}

  async assertAllowed(email: string, ip: string) {
    const [pair, byIp] = await Promise.all([
      this.kv.get(`login:lock:${h(email + '|' + ip)}`),
      this.kv.get(`login:lock:ip:${h(ip)}`),
    ]);
    if (pair || byIp) {
      throw new HttpException({ code: 'too_many_attempts', message: 'Too many attempts' }, HttpStatus.TOO_MANY_REQUESTS);
    }
  }

  async recordFailure(email: string, ip: string) {
    const pairKey = h(email + '|' + ip);
    const n = await this.kv.incr(`login:fail:${pairKey}`, WINDOW);
    if (n >= PAIR_LIMIT) {
      const lockSec = Math.min(60 * 2 ** (n - PAIR_LIMIT), WINDOW); // progressivo, máx. 15 min
      await this.kv.set(`login:lock:${pairKey}`, '1', lockSec);
    }
    const ipN = await this.kv.incr(`login:fail:ip:${h(ip)}`, WINDOW);
    if (ipN >= IP_LIMIT) await this.kv.set(`login:lock:ip:${h(ip)}`, '1', WINDOW);
  }

  async reset(email: string, ip: string) {
    await this.kv.del(`login:fail:${h(email + '|' + ip)}`);
  }
}
```

`src/sessions/sessions.service.ts`:

```ts
import { ForbiddenException, Inject, Injectable, UnauthorizedException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { IDENTITY_PROVIDER, type IdentityProviderPort, type TokenSet } from '../identity-provider/identity-provider.port';
import { InvalidCredentialsError, InvalidRefreshTokenError } from '../identity-provider/errors';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { AuditService } from '../shared/audit.service';
import { KV_STORE, SESSION_TTL_SEC, sessionMembershipKey, type KeyValueStore } from '../shared/kv/kv.port';
import { LoginThrottle } from './login-throttle';

export interface MembershipDto { id: string; role: string; tenantId: string | null; tenantName: string | null }
export interface TokenPair { accessToken: string; refreshToken: string; expiresIn: number }
export interface LoginResult { tokens: TokenPair; memberships: MembershipDto[]; activeMembershipId: string | null }

const GENERIC = { code: 'invalid_credentials', message: 'Invalid credentials' };
const pair = (t: TokenSet): TokenPair => ({ accessToken: t.accessToken, refreshToken: t.refreshToken, expiresIn: t.expiresIn });

@Injectable()
export class SessionsService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    @Inject(IDENTITY_PROVIDER) private readonly provider: IdentityProviderPort,
    private readonly audit: AuditService,
    @Inject(KV_STORE) private readonly kv: KeyValueStore,
    private readonly throttle: LoginThrottle,
  ) {}

  async login(email: string, password: string, ip = 'unknown'): Promise<LoginResult> {
    await this.throttle.assertAllowed(email, ip);
    let tokens: TokenSet;
    try {
      tokens = await this.provider.authenticate(email, password);
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof InvalidCredentialsError) {
        await this.throttle.recordFailure(email, ip);
        await this.audit.log('login.failed', { ip });
        throw new UnauthorizedException(GENERIC);
      }
      throw e;
    }
    const user = await this.prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { externalId: tokens.subject } });
    if (!user || user.status === 'SUSPENDED') {
      await this.provider.revokeSession(tokens.refreshToken);
      throw new UnauthorizedException(GENERIC);
    }
    if (!tokens.emailVerified) {
      await this.provider.revokeSession(tokens.refreshToken);
      throw new ForbiddenException({ code: 'email_not_verified', message: 'E-mail not verified' });
    }
    if (!user.emailVerified) {
      await this.prisma.user.update({ where: { id: user.id }, data: { emailVerified: true, status: 'ACTIVE' } });
    }
    const memberships = await this.activeMemberships(user.id);
    if (memberships.length === 0) {
      await this.provider.revokeSession(tokens.refreshToken);
      throw new ForbiddenException({ code: 'no_membership', message: 'No active profile' });
    }
    let active: string | null = null;
    if (memberships.length === 1) {
      active = memberships[0].id;
      await this.kv.set(sessionMembershipKey(tokens.sessionId), active, SESSION_TTL_SEC);
    }
    await this.throttle.reset(email, ip);
    await this.audit.log('login.success', { actorUserId: user.id, ip });
    return { tokens: pair(tokens), memberships, activeMembershipId: active };
  }

  async refresh(refreshToken: string): Promise<TokenPair> {
    let tokens: TokenSet;
    try {
      tokens = await this.provider.refresh(refreshToken);
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof InvalidRefreshTokenError) throw new UnauthorizedException({ code: 'session_expired', message: 'Session expired' });
      throw e;
    }
    const user = await this.prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { externalId: tokens.subject } });
    if (!user || user.status === 'SUSPENDED') {
      await this.provider.revokeSession(tokens.refreshToken);
      throw new UnauthorizedException({ code: 'session_expired', message: 'Session expired' });
    }
    return pair(tokens);
  }

  async logout(refreshToken: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.provider.revokeSession(refreshToken);
    await this.audit.log('logout');
  }

  async selectMembership(i: { subject: string; sessionId: string; membershipId: string }): Promise<void> {
    const user = await this.prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { externalId: i.subject } });
    const owned = user
      ? (await this.activeMemberships(user.id)).some((m) => m.id === i.membershipId)
      : false;
    if (!user || !owned) throw new ForbiddenException({ code: 'forbidden', message: 'Access denied' });
    await this.kv.set(sessionMembershipKey(i.sessionId), i.membershipId, SESSION_TTL_SEC);
    await this.audit.log('membership.selected', { actorUserId: user.id, targetType: 'membership', targetId: i.membershipId });
  }

  async forgotPassword(email: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.provider.sendPasswordReset(email);
    await this.audit.log('password.reset_requested');
  }

  async resendVerification(email: string, ip?: string): Promise<void> {
    const n = await this.kv.incr(`resend:${email.toLowerCase()}`, 60);
    if (n > 1) return;
    const user = await this.prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email: email.toLowerCase() } });
    if (user && !user.emailVerified) await this.provider.sendVerificationEmail(user.externalId);
    await this.audit.log('verification.resent', { ip });
  }

  private async activeMemberships(userId: string): Promise<MembershipDto[]> {
    const rows = await this.prisma.membership.findMany({
      where: { userId, status: 'ACTIVE', OR: [{ tenantId: null }, { tenant: { status: 'ACTIVE' } }] },
      include: { role: true, tenant: true },
    });
    return rows.map((m) => ({ id: m.id, role: m.role.name, tenantId: m.tenantId, tenantName: m.tenant?.name ?? null }));
  }
}
```

`src/shared/service-key.guard.ts`:

```ts
import { CanActivate, ExecutionContext, Injectable, UnauthorizedException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';

@Injectable()
export class ServiceKeyGuard implements CanActivate {
  canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): boolean {
    const given = Buffer.from(String(context.switchToHttp().getRequest().headers['x-service-key'] ?? ''));
    const expected = Buffer.from(process.env.INTERNAL_SERVICE_KEY ?? '');
    if (expected.length < 32 || given.length !== expected.length || !timingSafeEqual(given, expected)) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException({ code: 'unauthorized', message: 'Unauthorized' });
    }
    return true;
  }
}
```

```ts
import { Body, Controller, HttpCode, Ip, Post, UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { forgotPasswordSchema, loginSchema, selectMembershipSchema } from '@aura/schemas';
import { Public } from '@aura/internal-context';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { ServiceKeyGuard } from '../shared/service-key.guard';
import { ZodValidationPipe } from '../shared/zod-validation.pipe';
import { SessionsService } from './sessions.service';

const refreshSchema = z.object({ refreshToken: z.string().min(10).max(4096) });
const internalSelectSchema = selectMembershipSchema.extend({ subject: z.string().min(1), sessionId: z.string().min(1) });

@Controller()
export class SessionsController {
  constructor(private readonly sessions: SessionsService) {}

  @Public() @Post('sessions/login')
  login(@Body(new ZodValidationPipe(loginSchema)) b: { email: string; password: string }, @Ip() ip: string) {
    return this.sessions.login(b.email, b.password, ip);
  }

  @Public() @Post('sessions/refresh')
  refresh(@Body(new ZodValidationPipe(refreshSchema)) b: { refreshToken: string }) {
    return this.sessions.refresh(b.refreshToken);
  }

  @Public() @HttpCode(204) @Post('sessions/logout')
  async logout(@Body(new ZodValidationPipe(refreshSchema)) b: { refreshToken: string }) {
    await this.sessions.logout(b.refreshToken);
  }

  @Public() @HttpCode(204) @Post('accounts/forgot-password')
  async forgot(@Body(new ZodValidationPipe(forgotPasswordSchema)) b: { email: string }) {
    await this.sessions.forgotPassword(b.email);
  }

  @Public() @HttpCode(204) @Post('accounts/resend-verification')
  async resend(@Body(new ZodValidationPipe(forgotPasswordSchema)) b: { email: string }, @Ip() ip: string) {
    await this.sessions.resendVerification(b.email, ip);
  }

  @Public() @UseGuards(ServiceKeyGuard) @HttpCode(204) @Post('internal/sessions/select-membership')
  async select(@Body(new ZodValidationPipe(internalSelectSchema)) b: { subject: string; sessionId: string; membershipId: string }) {
    await this.sessions.selectMembership(b);
  }
}
```

### Task 8: identity — resolução de autorização, API de RBAC e perfil

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/identity-service/src/authorization/authorization.service.ts`, `authz-version.ts`, `authorization.controller.ts`, `roles.service.ts`, `roles.controller.ts`, `authorization.module.ts`
- Create: `apps/identity-service/src/accounts/profile.service.ts`, `profile.controller.ts` (rotas `GET/PATCH /me`)
- Test: `apps/identity-service/src/authorization/authorization.service.test.ts`, `roles.service.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `KV_STORE`, `sessionMembershipKey` (Task 7); `ServiceKeyGuard` (Task 7); `isPermission`, `PERMISSIONS` de `@aura/authz`.
- Produces:
  - `AUTHZ_VERSION_KEY = 'authz:version'`; `AuthzVersion.bump(): Promise<void>` e `current(): Promise<string>` (BFF lê a mesma chave para compor o cache).
  - `AuthorizationService.resolve(i: { subject: string; sessionId: string }): Promise<ResolveResult>` onde
    `ResolveResult = { status: 'ok'; context: Omit<InternalContext, 'correlationId'> } | { status: 'needs_selection'; memberships: MembershipDto[] } | { status: 'denied' }`
  - Rota interna (`ServiceKeyGuard`): `POST /internal/authorization/resolve` body `{ subject, sessionId }`.
  - `RolesService`: `list()`, `setPermissions(actorUserId: string, roleId: number, codes: string[]): Promise<void>`; `UsersAdminService`: `list(page, pageSize)`, `setStatus(actorUserId, targetUserId, status)`.
  - Rotas com contexto: `GET /roles` e `GET /permissions` (`roles:read`), `PUT /roles/:id/permissions` (`roles:manage`), `GET /users` (`users:read`), `PATCH /users/:id/status` (`users:manage`), `GET /me` (`profile:read`), `PATCH /me` (`profile:update`).
  - Regras: permissões desconhecidas → 400 `unknown_permission`; remover `roles:manage`/`users:manage` do papel `admin` → 400 `protected_permission`; admin não pode suspender a si mesmo → 400 `cannot_suspend_self`. Toda mudança chama `AuthzVersion.bump()` e grava `AuditLog`.

```ts
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';
import { ROLE_PERMISSIONS } from '@aura/authz';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { InMemoryKeyValueStore } from '../shared/kv/in-memory.kv';
import { sessionMembershipKey } from '../shared/kv/kv.port';
import { seedRolesAndPermissions } from './seed-rbac';
import { AuthorizationService } from './authorization.service';

const prisma = new PrismaClient();
let kv: InMemoryKeyValueStore;
let svc: AuthorizationService;

async function setup(role: 'personal' | 'aluno', extra?: { suspendTenant?: boolean }) {
  const user = await prisma.user.create({ data: { externalId: `ext-${Math.random()}`, email: `${Math.random()}@x.com`, name: 'N', status: 'ACTIVE', emailVerified: true } });
  const tenant = await prisma.tenant.create({ data: { name: 'T', slug: `t-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`, status: extra?.suspendTenant ? 'SUSPENDED' : 'ACTIVE' } });
  const r = await prisma.role.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { name: role } });
  const m = await prisma.membership.create({ data: { userId: user.id, tenantId: tenant.id, roleId: r.id } });
  return { user, tenant, m };
}

beforeAll(async () => { await seedRolesAndPermissions(prisma); });
afterAll(() => prisma.$disconnect());
beforeEach(async () => {
  await prisma.membership.deleteMany(); await prisma.tenant.deleteMany(); await prisma.user.deleteMany();
  kv = new InMemoryKeyValueStore();
  svc = new AuthorizationService(prisma as any, kv);
});

describe('AuthorizationService.resolve', () => {
  it('returns tenant, role and permissions from the DB for the active membership', async () => {
    const { user, tenant, m } = await setup('personal');
    await kv.set(sessionMembershipKey('sid1'), m.id);
    const r = await svc.resolve({ subject: user.externalId, sessionId: 'sid1' });
    expect(r).toEqual({
      status: 'ok',
      context: {
        userId: user.id, tenantId: tenant.id, membershipId: m.id, role: 'personal',
        permissions: expect.arrayContaining([...ROLE_PERMISSIONS.personal]),
      },
    });
  });

  it('asks for profile selection when the session has no active membership', async () => {
    const { user } = await setup('aluno');
    const r = await svc.resolve({ subject: user.externalId, sessionId: 'nosid' });
    expect(r.status).toBe('needs_selection');
  });

  it('denies when the stored membership belongs to someone else (stale/forged session key)', async () => {
    const a = await setup('aluno'); const b = await setup('aluno');
    await kv.set(sessionMembershipKey('sid2'), b.m.id);
    expect((await svc.resolve({ subject: a.user.externalId, sessionId: 'sid2' })).status).toBe('denied');
  });

  it('denies suspended users, suspended memberships and suspended tenants', async () => {
    const s = await setup('aluno');
    await kv.set(sessionMembershipKey('s3'), s.m.id);
    await prisma.user.update({ where: { id: s.user.id }, data: { status: 'SUSPENDED' } });
    expect((await svc.resolve({ subject: s.user.externalId, sessionId: 's3' })).status).toBe('denied');

    const t = await setup('aluno', { suspendTenant: true });
    await kv.set(sessionMembershipKey('s4'), t.m.id);
    expect((await svc.resolve({ subject: t.user.externalId, sessionId: 's4' })).status).toBe('denied');
  });

  it('denies unknown subjects', async () => {
    expect((await svc.resolve({ subject: 'nobody', sessionId: 'x' })).status).toBe('denied');
  });
});
```

```ts
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';
import { PERMISSIONS } from '@aura/authz';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { AuditService } from '../shared/audit.service';
import { InMemoryKeyValueStore } from '../shared/kv/in-memory.kv';
import { AuthzVersion } from './authz-version';
import { RolesService, UsersAdminService } from './roles.service';
import { seedRolesAndPermissions } from './seed-rbac';

const prisma = new PrismaClient();
let kv: InMemoryKeyValueStore;
let version: AuthzVersion;
let roles: RolesService;
let users: UsersAdminService;

beforeAll(async () => { await seedRolesAndPermissions(prisma); });
afterAll(() => prisma.$disconnect());
beforeEach(async () => {
  await seedRolesAndPermissions(prisma);
  await prisma.auditLog.deleteMany(); await prisma.membership.deleteMany(); await prisma.user.deleteMany();
  kv = new InMemoryKeyValueStore();
  version = new AuthzVersion(kv);
  const audit = new AuditService(prisma as any);
  roles = new RolesService(prisma as any, audit, version);
  users = new UsersAdminService(prisma as any, audit, version);
});

describe('RolesService.setPermissions', () => {
  it('replaces the permission set, audits it and bumps the authz version', async () => {
    const before = await version.current();
    const personal = await prisma.role.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { name: 'personal' } });
    await roles.setPermissions('actor', personal.id, [PERMISSIONS.PROFILE_READ]);
    const list = await roles.list();
    expect(list.find((r) => r.name === 'personal')!.permissions).toEqual(['profile:read']);
    expect(await version.current()).not.toBe(before);
    expect(await prisma.auditLog.count({ where: { action: 'role.permissions_changed' } })).toBe(1);
  });

  it('rejects unknown permission codes', async () => {
    const personal = await prisma.role.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { name: 'personal' } });
    await expect(roles.setPermissions('a', personal.id, ['students:hack'])).rejects.toMatchObject({ response: { code: 'unknown_permission' } });
  });

  it('protects admin from losing roles:manage and users:manage', async () => {
    const admin = await prisma.role.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { name: 'admin' } });
    await expect(roles.setPermissions('a', admin.id, [PERMISSIONS.PROFILE_READ])).rejects.toMatchObject({ response: { code: 'protected_permission' } });
  });
});

describe('UsersAdminService.setStatus', () => {
  it('suspends a user and bumps the version', async () => {
    const u = await prisma.user.create({ data: { externalId: 'e1', email: 'u1@x.com', name: 'U', status: 'ACTIVE' } });
    const before = await version.current();
    await users.setStatus('someone-else', u.id, 'SUSPENDED');
    expect((await prisma.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: u.id } })).status).toBe('SUSPENDED');
    expect(await version.current()).not.toBe(before);
  });

  it('does not let an admin suspend themselves', async () => {
    const u = await prisma.user.create({ data: { externalId: 'e2', email: 'u2@x.com', name: 'U', status: 'ACTIVE' } });
    await expect(users.setStatus(u.id, u.id, 'SUSPENDED')).rejects.toMatchObject({ response: { code: 'cannot_suspend_self' } });
  });

  it('list paginates without exposing internal fields', async () => {
    await prisma.user.create({ data: { externalId: 'e3', email: 'u3@x.com', name: 'U3', status: 'ACTIVE' } });
    const page = await users.list(1, 50);
    expect(page.items[0]).not.toHaveProperty('externalId');
    expect(page.items[0]).toHaveProperty('email');
  });
});
```
