`src/authorization/authz-version.ts`:

```ts
import { Inject, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { KV_STORE, type KeyValueStore } from '../shared/kv/kv.port';

export const AUTHZ_VERSION_KEY = 'authz:version';

@Injectable()
export class AuthzVersion {
  constructor(@Inject(KV_STORE) private readonly kv: KeyValueStore) {}
  async bump(): Promise<void> { await this.kv.incr(AUTHZ_VERSION_KEY); }
  async current(): Promise<string> { return (await this.kv.get(AUTHZ_VERSION_KEY)) ?? '0'; }
}
```

`src/authorization/authorization.service.ts`:

```ts
import { Inject, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { InternalContext } from '@aura/internal-context';
import type { RoleName } from '@aura/authz';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { KV_STORE, sessionMembershipKey, type KeyValueStore } from '../shared/kv/kv.port';

export type ResolveResult =
  | { status: 'ok'; context: Omit<InternalContext, 'correlationId'> }
  | { status: 'needs_selection'; memberships: { id: string; role: string; tenantId: string | null; tenantName: string | null }[] }
  | { status: 'denied' };

@Injectable()
export class AuthorizationService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    @Inject(KV_STORE) private readonly kv: KeyValueStore,
  ) {}

  async resolve(i: { subject: string; sessionId: string }): Promise<ResolveResult> {
    const user = await this.prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { externalId: i.subject } });
    if (!user || user.status !== 'ACTIVE') return { status: 'denied' };

    const activeId = await this.kv.get(sessionMembershipKey(i.sessionId));
    if (!activeId) {
      const rows = await this.prisma.membership.findMany({
        where: { userId: user.id, status: 'ACTIVE', OR: [{ tenantId: null }, { tenant: { status: 'ACTIVE' } }] },
        include: { role: true, tenant: true },
      });
      return {
        status: 'needs_selection',
        memberships: rows.map((m) => ({ id: m.id, role: m.role.name, tenantId: m.tenantId, tenantName: m.tenant?.name ?? null })),
      };
    }

    const m = await this.prisma.membership.findFirst({
      where: { id: activeId, userId: user.id, status: 'ACTIVE' },
      include: { role: { include: { permissions: { include: { permission: true } } } }, tenant: true },
    });
    if (!m || (m.tenant && m.tenant.status !== 'ACTIVE')) return { status: 'denied' };

    return {
      status: 'ok',
      context: {
        userId: user.id,
        tenantId: m.tenantId,
        membershipId: m.id,
        role: m.role.name as RoleName,
        permissions: m.role.permissions.map((p) => p.permission.code),
      },
    };
  }
}
```

`src/authorization/roles.service.ts`:

```ts
import { BadRequestException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PERMISSIONS, isPermission } from '@aura/authz';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { AuditService } from '../shared/audit.service';
import { AuthzVersion } from './authz-version';

const PROTECTED_FOR_ADMIN = [PERMISSIONS.ROLES_MANAGE, PERMISSIONS.USERS_MANAGE];

@Injectable()
export class RolesService {
  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService, private readonly audit: AuditService, private readonly version: AuthzVersion) {}

  async list() {
    const rows = await this.prisma.role.findMany({
      orderBy: { id: 'asc' },
      include: { permissions: { include: { permission: true } }, _count: { select: { memberships: true } } },
    });
    return rows.map((r) => ({
      id: r.id, name: r.name, users: r._count.memberships,
      permissions: r.permissions.map((p) => p.permission.code).sort(),
    }));
  }

  async listPermissions() {
    return Object.values(PERMISSIONS);
  }

  async setPermissions(actorUserId: string, roleId: number, codes: string[]): Promise<void> {
    if (!codes.every(isPermission)) {
      throw new BadRequestException({ code: 'unknown_permission', message: 'Unknown permission' });
    }
    const role = await this.prisma.role.findUnique({ where: { id: roleId } });
    if (!role) throw new BadRequestException({ code: 'unknown_role', message: 'Unknown role' });
    if (role.name === 'admin' && !PROTECTED_FOR_ADMIN.every((p) => codes.includes(p))) {
      throw new BadRequestException({ code: 'protected_permission', message: 'Protected permission' });
    }
    const perms = await this.prisma.permission.findMany({ where: { code: { in: codes } } });
    await this.prisma.$transaction([
      this.prisma.rolePermission.deleteMany({ where: { roleId } }),
      this.prisma.rolePermission.createMany({ data: perms.map((p) => ({ roleId, permissionId: p.id })) }),
    ]);
    await this.audit.log('role.permissions_changed', { actorUserId, targetType: 'role', targetId: String(roleId), metadata: { permissions: codes } });
    await this.version.bump();
  }
}

@Injectable()
export class UsersAdminService {
  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService, private readonly audit: AuditService, private readonly version: AuthzVersion) {}

  async list(page: number, pageSize: number) {
    const [items, total] = await Promise.all([
      this.prisma.user.findMany({
        skip: (page - 1) * pageSize, take: pageSize, orderBy: { createdAt: 'desc' },
        select: { id: true, email: true, name: true, status: true, emailVerified: true, createdAt: true,
          memberships: { select: { id: true, status: true, role: { select: { name: true } } } } },
      }),
      this.prisma.user.count(),
    ]);
    return { items, total, page, pageSize };
  }

  async setStatus(actorUserId: string, targetUserId: string, status: 'ACTIVE' | 'SUSPENDED'): Promise<void> {
    if (actorUserId === targetUserId && status === 'SUSPENDED') {
      throw new BadRequestException({ code: 'cannot_suspend_self', message: 'Cannot suspend yourself' });
    }
    await this.prisma.user.update({ where: { id: targetUserId }, data: { status } });
    await this.audit.log('user.status_changed', { actorUserId, targetType: 'user', targetId: targetUserId, metadata: { status } });
    await this.version.bump();
  }
}
```

`src/authorization/authorization.controller.ts` (rota interna) e `roles.controller.ts`:

```ts
// authorization.controller.ts
import { Body, Controller, HttpCode, Post, UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Public } from '@aura/internal-context';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { ServiceKeyGuard } from '../shared/service-key.guard';
import { ZodValidationPipe } from '../shared/zod-validation.pipe';
import { AuthorizationService } from './authorization.service';
import { AuthzVersion } from './authz-version';

const resolveSchema = z.object({ subject: z.string().min(1), sessionId: z.string().min(1) });

@Controller('internal/authorization')
export class AuthorizationController {
  constructor(private readonly authz: AuthorizationService, private readonly version: AuthzVersion) {}

  @Public() @UseGuards(ServiceKeyGuard) @HttpCode(200) @Post('resolve')
  async resolve(@Body(new ZodValidationPipe(resolveSchema)) b: { subject: string; sessionId: string }) {
    return { ...(await this.authz.resolve(b)), version: await this.version.current() };
  }
}
```

```ts
// roles.controller.ts
import { Body, Controller, Get, HttpCode, Param, ParseIntPipe, Patch, Put, Query } from '@nestjs/common';
import { CurrentContext, InternalContext, RequirePermission } from '@aura/internal-context';
import { setPermissionsSchema, setStatusSchema } from '@aura/schemas';
import { ZodValidationPipe } from '../shared/zod-validation.pipe';
import { RolesService, UsersAdminService } from './roles.service';

@Controller()
export class RolesController {
  constructor(private readonly roles: RolesService, private readonly users: UsersAdminService) {}

  @RequirePermission('roles:read') @Get('roles')
  list() { return this.roles.list(); }

  @RequirePermission('roles:read') @Get('permissions')
  permissions() { return this.roles.listPermissions(); }

  @RequirePermission('roles:manage') @HttpCode(204) @Put('roles/:id/permissions')
  async set(
    @CurrentContext() ctx: InternalContext,
    @Param('id', ParseIntPipe) id: number,
    @Body(new ZodValidationPipe(setPermissionsSchema)) b: { permissions: string[] },
  ) { await this.roles.setPermissions(ctx.userId, id, b.permissions); }

  @RequirePermission('users:read') @Get('users')
  listUsers(@Query('page') page = '1', @Query('pageSize') pageSize = '50') {
    return this.users.list(Math.max(1, Number(page) || 1), Math.min(100, Math.max(1, Number(pageSize) || 50)));
  }

  @RequirePermission('users:manage') @HttpCode(204) @Patch('users/:id/status')
  async setStatus(
    @CurrentContext() ctx: InternalContext,
    @Param('id') id: string,
    @Body(new ZodValidationPipe(setStatusSchema)) b: { status: 'ACTIVE' | 'SUSPENDED' },
  ) { await this.users.setStatus(ctx.userId, id, b.status); }
}
```

`src/accounts/profile.controller.ts` (o serviço é um `select`/`update` direto por `ctx.userId`, nunca por id vindo do cliente):

```ts
import { Body, Controller, Get, Patch } from '@nestjs/common';
import { CurrentContext, InternalContext, RequirePermission } from '@aura/internal-context';
import { updateMeSchema } from '@aura/schemas';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { ZodValidationPipe } from '../shared/zod-validation.pipe';

@Controller('me')
export class ProfileController {
  constructor(private readonly prisma: PrismaService) {}

  @RequirePermission('profile:read') @Get()
  async me(@CurrentContext() ctx: InternalContext) {
    const u = await this.prisma.user.findUniqueOrThrow({
      where: { id: ctx.userId }, select: { id: true, email: true, name: true, phone: true, emailVerified: true },
    });
    return { ...u, role: ctx.role, tenantId: ctx.tenantId, permissions: ctx.permissions };
  }

  @RequirePermission('profile:update') @Patch()
  update(@CurrentContext() ctx: InternalContext, @Body(new ZodValidationPipe(updateMeSchema)) b: { name?: string; phone?: string | null }) {
    return this.prisma.user.update({
      where: { id: ctx.userId }, data: b, select: { id: true, email: true, name: true, phone: true },
    });
  }
}
```

```ts
import { BadRequestException, Controller, Get, NotFoundException, Post } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Test } from '@nestjs/testing';
import request from 'supertest';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
  AllExceptionsFilter, applySecurity, assertProductionSafe, correlationMiddleware, swaggerEnabled,
  type SecurityOptions,
} from './index';

@Controller()
class TestController {
  @Get('ok') ok() { return { ok: true }; }
  @Get('health') health() { return { status: 'up' }; }
  @Get('boom') boom() { throw new Error('SELECT * FROM users WHERE password = ...'); }
  @Get('nf') nf() { throw new NotFoundException(); }
  @Post('validate') validate() {
    throw new BadRequestException({ code: 'validation_error', message: 'Invalid request', fields: [{ path: 'email', code: 'invalid' }] });
  }
}

async function makeApp(over: Partial<SecurityOptions> = {}) {
  const mod = await Test.createTestingModule({ controllers: [TestController] }).compile();
  const app = mod.createNestApplication();
  app.use(correlationMiddleware);
  applySecurity(app, {
    allowedHosts: ['api.aura.test'], corsOrigins: ['https://app.aura.test'], corsCredentials: true, isProd: false, ...over,
  });
  app.useGlobalFilters(new AllExceptionsFilter());
  await app.init();
  return app;
}
const host = 'api.aura.test';

describe('headers', () => {
  it('sets hardened headers and hides x-powered-by (prod)', async () => {
    const app = await makeApp({ isProd: true });
    const r = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/ok').set('Host', host).set('X-Forwarded-Proto', 'https');
    expect(r.status).toBe(200);
    expect(r.headers['strict-transport-security']).toMatch(/max-age=31536000/);
    expect(r.headers['strict-transport-security']).toMatch(/includeSubDomains/);
    expect(r.headers['content-security-policy']).toContain("default-src 'none'");
    expect(r.headers['content-security-policy']).toContain("frame-ancestors 'none'");
    expect(r.headers['x-content-type-options']).toBe('nosniff');
    expect(r.headers['x-frame-options']).toBe('DENY');
    expect(r.headers['referrer-policy']).toBe('no-referrer');
    expect(r.headers['cross-origin-opener-policy']).toBe('same-origin');
    expect(r.headers['cross-origin-resource-policy']).toBe('same-origin');
    expect(r.headers['permissions-policy']).toContain('camera=()');
    expect(r.headers['x-powered-by']).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe('trusted host', () => {
  it('answers 421 for hosts outside the allowlist', async () => {
    const app = await makeApp();
    const r = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/ok').set('Host', 'evil.example');
    expect(r.status).toBe(421);
  });
  it('accepts allowlisted hosts with a port', async () => {
    const app = await makeApp();
    expect((await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/ok').set('Host', `${host}:3000`)).status).toBe(200);
  });
});

describe('https redirect', () => {
  it('redirects plain http in production, except /health', async () => {
    const app = await makeApp({ isProd: true });
    const r = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/ok?x=1').set('Host', host).set('X-Forwarded-Proto', 'http');
    expect(r.status).toBe(308);
    expect(r.headers.location).toBe('https://api.aura.test/ok?x=1');
    const h = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/health').set('Host', host).set('X-Forwarded-Proto', 'http');
    expect(h.status).toBe(200);
  });
  it('never redirects to an unlisted host (no open redirect)', async () => {
    const app = await makeApp({ isProd: true });
    const r = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/ok').set('Host', 'evil.example').set('X-Forwarded-Proto', 'http');
    expect(r.status).toBe(421);
  });
});

describe('cors', () => {
  it('echoes only allowlisted origins, with credentials', async () => {
    const app = await makeApp();
    const ok = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/ok').set('Host', host).set('Origin', 'https://app.aura.test');
    expect(ok.headers['access-control-allow-origin']).toBe('https://app.aura.test');
    expect(ok.headers['access-control-allow-credentials']).toBe('true');
    const bad = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/ok').set('Host', host).set('Origin', 'https://evil.example');
    expect(bad.headers['access-control-allow-origin']).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it('refuses a wildcard origin at startup', async () => {
    await expect(makeApp({ corsOrigins: ['*'] })).rejects.toThrow(/wildcard/i);
  });
  it('refuses non-https origins in production', async () => {
    await expect(makeApp({ isProd: true, corsOrigins: ['http://app.aura.test'] })).rejects.toThrow(/https/i);
  });
});

describe('error handling', () => {
  it('hides internals of unexpected errors', async () => {
    const app = await makeApp();
    const r = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/boom').set('Host', host);
    expect(r.status).toBe(500);
    expect(r.body).toEqual({ code: 'internal_error', message: 'Internal server error', correlationId: expect.any(String) });
    expect(JSON.stringify(r.body)).not.toMatch(/SELECT|password|stack|at /);
  });
  it('keeps structured HttpException codes and validation fields', async () => {
    const app = await makeApp();
    const r = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/validate').set('Host', host);
    expect(r.status).toBe(400);
    expect(r.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'validation_error', fields: [{ path: 'email', code: 'invalid' }] });
  });
  it('gives generic bodies to framework errors', async () => {
    const app = await makeApp();
    const r = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/nf').set('Host', host);
    expect(r.status).toBe(404);
    expect(r.body.code).toBe('not_found');
    expect(r.body.message).not.toMatch(/Cannot GET/);
  });
  it('replaces malicious correlation ids and keeps valid ones', async () => {
    const app = await makeApp();
    const bad = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/ok').set('Host', host).set('x-correlation-id', 'a\r\nb<script>');
    expect(bad.headers['x-correlation-id']).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/);
    const good = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/ok').set('Host', host).set('x-correlation-id', 'abc-12345678');
    expect(good.headers['x-correlation-id']).toBe('abc-12345678');
  });
});

describe('production guard', () => {
  const strong = 'x'.repeat(40);
  const good = {
    NODE_ENV: 'production', INTERNAL_CONTEXT_SECRET: strong + 'a', INTERNAL_SERVICE_KEY: strong + 'b',
    KEYCLOAK_CLIENT_SECRET: strong + 'c', ALLOWED_HOSTS: 'api.aura.test', CORS_ORIGINS: 'https://app.aura.test',
  } as NodeJS.ProcessEnv;

  it('passes with safe production config and is a no-op outside production', () => {
    expect(() => assertProductionSafe(good)).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => assertProductionSafe({ NODE_ENV: 'development' })).not.toThrow();
  });
  it('fails on debug flags, dev secrets and wildcard CORS', () => {
    expect(() => assertProductionSafe({ ...good, ENABLE_SWAGGER: 'true' })).toThrow(/ENABLE_SWAGGER/);
    expect(() => assertProductionSafe({ ...good, ENABLE_DEBUG_ROUTES: 'true' })).toThrow(/ENABLE_DEBUG_ROUTES/);
    expect(() => assertProductionSafe({ ...good, INTERNAL_SERVICE_KEY: 'dev-internal-service-key-change-me-please!!' })).toThrow(/INTERNAL_SERVICE_KEY/);
    expect(() => assertProductionSafe({ ...good, CORS_ORIGINS: '*' })).toThrow(/CORS_ORIGINS/);
    expect(() => assertProductionSafe({ ...good, ALLOWED_HOSTS: '' })).toThrow(/ALLOWED_HOSTS/);
  });
  it('disables swagger in production regardless of the flag', () => {
    expect(swaggerEnabled({ NODE_ENV: 'production', ENABLE_SWAGGER: 'true' })).toBe(false);
    expect(swaggerEnabled({ NODE_ENV: 'development' })).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

`src/correlation.ts`:

```ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from 'express';

const VALID = /^[A-Za-z0-9-]{8,64}$/;

export function correlationMiddleware(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) {
  const incoming = req.headers['x-correlation-id'];
  const id = typeof incoming === 'string' && VALID.test(incoming) ? incoming : randomUUID();
  (req as any).correlationId = id;
  res.setHeader('x-correlation-id', id);
  next();
}
```

`src/apply-security.ts`:

```ts
import type { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { NextFunction, Request, Response } from 'express';
import helmet from 'helmet';

export interface SecurityOptions {
  allowedHosts: string[];
  corsOrigins: string[];
  corsCredentials?: boolean;
  isProd: boolean;
  trustProxy?: number;
}

export const parseList = (v: string | undefined) => (v ?? '').split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);

const stripPort = (h: string) => h.toLowerCase().replace(/:\d+$/, '');

function hostAllowlist(allowed: string[]) {
  const set = new Set(allowed.map((h) => h.toLowerCase()));
  return (req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    if (set.has(stripPort(req.headers.host ?? ''))) return next();
    res.status(421).json({ code: 'misdirected_request', message: 'Misdirected request' });
  };
}

function httpsRedirect(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) {
  if (req.secure || req.path === '/health') return next();
  // o Host já foi validado pela allowlist antes deste middleware
  res.redirect(308, `https://${req.headers.host}${req.originalUrl}`);
}

export function applySecurity(app: INestApplication, o: SecurityOptions): void {
  if (o.corsOrigins.includes('*')) throw new Error('CORS wildcard origin is not allowed');
  if (o.isProd && o.corsOrigins.some((x) => !x.startsWith('https://'))) throw new Error('CORS origins must be https in production');

  const http = app.getHttpAdapter().getInstance();
  http.disable('x-powered-by');
  if (o.isProd) http.set('trust proxy', o.trustProxy ?? 1);

  app.use(hostAllowlist(o.allowedHosts));
  if (o.isProd) app.use(httpsRedirect);

  app.use(
    helmet({
      contentSecurityPolicy: { useDefaults: false, directives: { defaultSrc: ["'none'"], frameAncestors: ["'none'"] } },
      hsts: o.isProd ? { maxAge: 31_536_000, includeSubDomains: true, preload: true } : false,
      referrerPolicy: { policy: 'no-referrer' },
      crossOriginOpenerPolicy: { policy: 'same-origin' },
      crossOriginResourcePolicy: { policy: 'same-origin' },
      frameguard: { action: 'deny' },
    }),
  );
  app.use((_req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) => {
    res.setHeader('Permissions-Policy', 'camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=(), payment=(), usb=()');
    next();
  });

  const allowed = new Set(o.corsOrigins);
  app.enableCors({
    origin: (origin, cb) => cb(null, !origin || allowed.has(origin)),
    credentials: o.corsCredentials ?? false,
    methods: ['GET', 'POST', 'PUT', 'PATCH', 'DELETE'],
    allowedHeaders: ['content-type', 'authorization', 'x-csrf-token', 'x-correlation-id', 'x-client'],
    exposedHeaders: ['x-correlation-id'],
    maxAge: 600,
  });
}
```

Observação: com `origin` retornando `false`, o pacote `cors` simplesmente não emite os headers CORS, e o navegador bloqueia. Requisições sem `Origin` (server-to-server) passam.

`src/exception.filter.ts`:

```ts
import { ArgumentsHost, Catch, ExceptionFilter, HttpException, Logger } from '@nestjs/common';

const CODES: Record<number, string> = {
  400: 'bad_request', 401: 'unauthorized', 403: 'forbidden', 404: 'not_found', 405: 'method_not_allowed',
  409: 'conflict', 413: 'payload_too_large', 422: 'unprocessable', 429: 'too_many_requests',
};
const MESSAGES: Record<number, string> = {
  400: 'Bad request', 401: 'Unauthorized', 403: 'Access denied', 404: 'Not found', 405: 'Method not allowed',
  409: 'Conflict', 413: 'Payload too large', 422: 'Unprocessable', 429: 'Too many requests',
};

@Catch()
export class AllExceptionsFilter implements ExceptionFilter {
  private readonly log = new Logger('Exceptions');

  catch(exception: unknown, host: ArgumentsHost) {
    const http = host.switchToHttp();
    const req = http.getRequest();
    const res = http.getResponse();
    const correlationId: string = req.correlationId ?? 'unknown';

    if (exception instanceof HttpException && exception.getStatus() < 500) {
      const status = exception.getStatus();
      const raw = exception.getResponse();
      const obj = typeof raw === 'object' && raw !== null ? (raw as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
      res.status(status).json({
        code: typeof obj.code === 'string' ? obj.code : (CODES[status] ?? 'error'),
        message: typeof obj.code === 'string' && typeof obj.message === 'string' ? obj.message : (MESSAGES[status] ?? 'Error'),
        ...(Array.isArray(obj.fields) ? { fields: obj.fields } : {}),
        correlationId,
      });
      return;
    }

    // 5xx e erros inesperados: detalhes só no log estruturado, nunca na resposta
    this.log.error(JSON.stringify({ correlationId, error: exception instanceof Error ? exception.message : 'unknown' }),
      exception instanceof Error ? exception.stack : undefined);
    res.status(500).json({ code: 'internal_error', message: 'Internal server error', correlationId });
  }
}
```

`src/production-guard.ts`:

```ts
import { parseList } from './apply-security';

const WEAK = /dev|change-?me|example|secret123|password/i;

export function assertProductionSafe(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv): void {
  if (env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') return;
  const problems: string[] = [];
  if (env.ENABLE_SWAGGER === 'true') problems.push('ENABLE_SWAGGER must not be true');
  if (env.ENABLE_DEBUG_ROUTES === 'true') problems.push('ENABLE_DEBUG_ROUTES must not be true');
  for (const k of ['INTERNAL_CONTEXT_SECRET', 'INTERNAL_SERVICE_KEY', 'KEYCLOAK_CLIENT_SECRET']) {
    const v = env[k];
    if (!v || v.length < 32 || WEAK.test(v)) problems.push(`${k} is missing, too short or a dev value`);
  }
  if (parseList(env.ALLOWED_HOSTS).length === 0) problems.push('ALLOWED_HOSTS is required');
  const origins = parseList(env.CORS_ORIGINS);
  if (origins.length === 0 || origins.some((o) => o === '*' || !o.startsWith('https://'))) {
    problems.push('CORS_ORIGINS must be explicit https origins');
  }
  if (problems.length) throw new Error(`Unsafe production configuration: ${problems.join('; ')}`);
}
```

`src/swagger.ts`:

```ts
import type { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { DocumentBuilder, SwaggerModule } from '@nestjs/swagger';

export const swaggerEnabled = (env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv) =>
  env.NODE_ENV !== 'production' && env.ENABLE_SWAGGER !== 'false';

export function maybeSetupSwagger(app: INestApplication, o: { title: string }, env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): boolean {
  if (!swaggerEnabled(env)) return false;
  const doc = SwaggerModule.createDocument(app, new DocumentBuilder().setTitle(o.title).setVersion('1').addBearerAuth().build());
  SwaggerModule.setup('docs', app, doc, { jsonDocumentUrl: 'docs-json' });
  return true;
}
```

```ts
import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Test } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';
import { signContext } from '@aura/internal-context';
import { AllExceptionsFilter, correlationMiddleware } from '@aura/http-security';
import request from 'supertest';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { AppModule } from '../src/app.module';
import { seedRolesAndPermissions } from '../src/authorization/seed-rbac';
import { IDENTITY_PROVIDER } from '../src/identity-provider/identity-provider.port';
import { InMemoryIdentityProvider } from '../src/identity-provider/in-memory.provider';
import { InMemoryKeyValueStore } from '../src/shared/kv/in-memory.kv';
import { KV_STORE } from '../src/shared/kv/kv.port';

const SECRET = 'e2e-internal-context-secret-32-chars-min!!';
process.env.INTERNAL_CONTEXT_SECRET = SECRET;
process.env.INTERNAL_SERVICE_KEY = 'e2e-internal-service-key-32-chars-min!!!';

let app: INestApplication;
let provider: InMemoryIdentityProvider;
const prisma = new PrismaClient();
const PW = 'Sup3r-secret-pw';

const ctxHeader = (over: object = {}) => signContext({
  userId: 'u', tenantId: 't', membershipId: 'm', role: 'aluno', permissions: ['profile:read'], correlationId: 'c-12345678', ...over,
} as any, SECRET);

beforeAll(async () => {
  await seedRolesAndPermissions(prisma);
  provider = new InMemoryIdentityProvider();
  const mod = await Test.createTestingModule({ imports: [AppModule] })
    .overrideProvider(IDENTITY_PROVIDER).useValue(provider)
    .overrideProvider(KV_STORE).useValue(new InMemoryKeyValueStore())
    .compile();
  app = mod.createNestApplication();
  app.use(correlationMiddleware);
  app.useGlobalFilters(new AllExceptionsFilter());
  await app.init();
});
afterAll(async () => { await app.close(); await prisma.$disconnect(); });

describe('identity e2e', () => {
  it('GET /health is public', async () => {
    expect((await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/health')).body).toEqual({ status: 'up' });
  });

  it('protected routes need a valid signed context (401) and permission (403)', async () => {
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/me').expect(401);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/roles').set('x-internal-context', ctxHeader()).expect(403);
  });

  it('register → verify → login → resolve → GET /me', async () => {
    const email = `e2e${Date.now()}@example.com`;
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/accounts/register')
      .send({ email, name: 'Rafael Silva', password: PW, acceptTerms: true, termsVersion: '2025-04-12' })
      .expect(201).expect({ status: 'pending_verification' });

    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/sessions/login').send({ email, password: PW }).expect(403);

    const user = await prisma.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { email } });
    provider.markEmailVerified(user.externalId);
    const login = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/sessions/login').send({ email, password: PW }).expect(201);
    expect(login.body.activeMembershipId).toBeTruthy();

    const sessionId = (await provider.refresh(login.body.tokens.refreshToken)).sessionId;
    const resolved = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/internal/authorization/resolve')
      .set('x-service-key', process.env.INTERNAL_SERVICE_KEY!)
      .send({ subject: user.externalId, sessionId }).expect(200);
    expect(resolved.body.status).toBe('ok');
    expect(resolved.body.context.role).toBe('personal');

    const me = await request(app.getHttpServer()).get('/me')
      .set('x-internal-context', ctxHeader({ userId: user.id, permissions: ['profile:read'] })).expect(200);
    expect(me.body.email).toBe(email);
    expect(me.body).not.toHaveProperty('externalId');
  });

  it('internal routes reject a missing or wrong service key', async () => {
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/internal/authorization/resolve').send({ subject: 's', sessionId: 'x' }).expect(401);
    await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/internal/authorization/resolve').set('x-service-key', 'wrong').send({ subject: 's', sessionId: 'x' }).expect(401);
  });

  it('validation errors never echo submitted values', async () => {
    const r = await request(app.getHttpServer()).post('/accounts/register')
      .send({ email: 'not-an-email', password: 'short-secret-value', name: 'A', acceptTerms: false, termsVersion: '1' }).expect(400);
    expect(r.body.code).toBe('validation_error');
    expect(JSON.stringify(r.body)).not.toContain('short-secret-value');
    expect(JSON.stringify(r.body)).not.toContain('not-an-email');
  });
});
```

`packages/observability/src/index.ts`:

```ts
import { getNodeAutoInstrumentations } from '@opentelemetry/auto-instrumentations-node';
import { OTLPTraceExporter } from '@opentelemetry/exporter-trace-otlp-http';
import { Resource } from '@opentelemetry/resources';
import { NodeSDK } from '@opentelemetry/sdk-node';
import { ATTR_SERVICE_NAME } from '@opentelemetry/semantic-conventions';

export function initOtel(serviceName: string, env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): { shutdown(): Promise<void> } | null {
  if (env.OTEL_SDK_DISABLED === 'true') return null;
  const endpoint = env.OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT ?? 'http://otel-collector:4318';
  const sdk = new NodeSDK({
    resource: new Resource({ [ATTR_SERVICE_NAME]: serviceName }),
    traceExporter: new OTLPTraceExporter({ url: `${endpoint}/v1/traces` }),
    instrumentations: [getNodeAutoInstrumentations({ '@opentelemetry/instrumentation-fs': { enabled: false } })],
  });
  sdk.start();
  return { shutdown: () => sdk.shutdown() };
}
```

`src/config/env.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';

const schema = z.object({
  PORT: z.coerce.number().default(3001),
  DATABASE_URL_IDENTITY: z.string().url(),
  REDIS_URL: z.string().url(),
  KEYCLOAK_URL: z.string().url(),
  KEYCLOAK_REALM: z.string().default('aura'),
  KEYCLOAK_CLIENT_ID: z.string().default('aura-identity'),
  KEYCLOAK_CLIENT_SECRET: z.string().min(1),
  INTERNAL_CONTEXT_SECRET: z.string().min(32),
  INTERNAL_SERVICE_KEY: z.string().min(32),
  ALLOWED_HOSTS: z.string().min(1),
  CORS_ORIGINS: z.string().default(''),
});
export type IdentityEnv = z.infer<typeof schema>;

export function loadIdentityEnv(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): IdentityEnv {
  const r = schema.safeParse(env);
  if (!r.success) throw new Error(`Invalid environment: ${r.error.issues.map((i) => i.path.join('.')).join(', ')}`);
  return r.data;
}
```

```ts
        baseUrl: process.env.KEYCLOAK_URL!, realm: process.env.KEYCLOAK_REALM ?? 'aura',
        clientId: process.env.KEYCLOAK_CLIENT_ID ?? 'aura-identity', clientSecret: process.env.KEYCLOAK_CLIENT_SECRET!,

    { provide: KV_STORE, useFactory: () => new RedisKeyValueStore(new Redis(process.env.REDIS_URL!)) },

})
```

`src/main.ts`:

```ts
import { initOtel } from '@aura/observability';
initOtel('identity-service'); // antes de qualquer outro import instrumentável

import 'reflect-metadata';
import { NestFactory } from '@nestjs/core';
import {
  AllExceptionsFilter, applySecurity, assertProductionSafe, correlationMiddleware, maybeSetupSwagger, parseList,
} from '@aura/http-security';
import { AppModule } from './app.module';
import { loadIdentityEnv } from './config/env';

async function bootstrap() {
  assertProductionSafe(process.env);
  const env = loadIdentityEnv();
  const app = await NestFactory.create(AppModule);
  app.use(correlationMiddleware);
  applySecurity(app, {
    allowedHosts: parseList(env.ALLOWED_HOSTS),
    corsOrigins: [], // identity é interno: sem CORS de navegador
    isProd: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
  });
  app.useGlobalFilters(new AllExceptionsFilter());
  app.enableShutdownHooks();
  maybeSetupSwagger(app, { title: 'aura-fit identity-service' });
  await app.listen(env.PORT, '0.0.0.0');
}
bootstrap();
```

Teste que falha primeiro, `admin-bootstrap.service.test.ts`:

```ts
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { seedRolesAndPermissions } from '../authorization/seed-rbac';
import { InMemoryIdentityProvider } from '../identity-provider/in-memory.provider';
import { AuditService } from '../shared/audit.service';
import { AdminBootstrapService } from './admin-bootstrap.service';

const prisma = new PrismaClient();
let provider: InMemoryIdentityProvider;
let svc: AdminBootstrapService;
const PW = 'Sup3r-secret-admin-pw';

beforeAll(async () => { await seedRolesAndPermissions(prisma); });
afterAll(() => prisma.$disconnect());
beforeEach(async () => {
  await prisma.auditLog.deleteMany(); await prisma.membership.deleteMany(); await prisma.user.deleteMany();
  provider = new InMemoryIdentityProvider();
  svc = new AdminBootstrapService(prisma as any, provider, new AuditService(prisma as any));
});

describe('AdminBootstrapService', () => {
  it('creates a verified admin with a tenant-less admin membership that can log in', async () => {
    expect(await svc.ensureAdmin('admin@aura.test', 'Admin', PW)).toEqual({ created: true });
    const u = await prisma.user.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { email: 'admin@aura.test' }, include: { memberships: { include: { role: true } } } });
    expect(u.status).toBe('ACTIVE');
    expect(u.emailVerified).toBe(true);
    expect(u.memberships).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(u.memberships[0]).toMatchObject({ tenantId: null });
    expect(u.memberships[0].role.name).toBe('admin');
    expect((await provider.authenticate('admin@aura.test', PW)).emailVerified).toBe(true);
  });

  it('is idempotent: a second run creates nothing new', async () => {
    await svc.ensureAdmin('admin@aura.test', 'Admin', PW);
    expect(await svc.ensureAdmin('admin@aura.test', 'Admin', PW)).toEqual({ created: false });
    expect(await prisma.membership.count()).toBe(1);
  });

  it('refuses weak passwords', async () => {
    await expect(svc.ensureAdmin('a@aura.test', 'A', 'short')).rejects.toThrow(/12/);
  });

  it('removes the provider account if the local write fails', async () => {
    const original = prisma.$transaction.bind(prisma);
    (prisma as any).$transaction = async () => { throw new Error('db down'); };
    await expect(svc.ensureAdmin('b@aura.test', 'B', PW)).rejects.toThrow('db down');
    (prisma as any).$transaction = original;
    await expect(provider.authenticate('b@aura.test', PW)).rejects.toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

Implementação:

```ts
// admin-bootstrap.service.ts
import { Inject, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { IDENTITY_PROVIDER, type IdentityProviderPort } from '../identity-provider/identity-provider.port';
import { PrismaService } from '../prisma/prisma.service';
import { AuditService } from '../shared/audit.service';

@Injectable()
export class AdminBootstrapService {
  constructor(
    private readonly prisma: PrismaService,
    @Inject(IDENTITY_PROVIDER) private readonly provider: IdentityProviderPort,
    private readonly audit: AuditService,
  ) {}

  async ensureAdmin(email: string, name: string, password: string): Promise<{ created: boolean }> {
    if (password.length < 12) throw new Error('admin password must have at least 12 characters');
    const mail = email.toLowerCase();
    const role = await this.prisma.role.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { name: 'admin' } });

    const existing = await this.prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { email: mail }, include: { memberships: true } });
    if (existing) {
      // unicidade do admin garantida aqui (tenantId nulo não é único no Postgres)
      if (!existing.memberships.some((m) => m.roleId === role.id && m.tenantId === null)) {
        await this.prisma.membership.create({ data: { userId: existing.id, tenantId: null, roleId: role.id } });
      }
      return { created: false };
    }

    const acc = await this.provider.createUser({ email: mail, name, password, emailVerified: true });
    try {
      await this.prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
        const u = await tx.user.create({
          data: {
            externalId: acc.externalId, email: mail, name, status: 'ACTIVE', emailVerified: true,
            termsAcceptedAt: new Date(), termsVersion: 'admin-bootstrap',
          },
        });
        await tx.membership.create({ data: { userId: u.id, tenantId: null, roleId: role.id } });
      });
    } catch (e) {
      await this.provider.deleteUser(acc.externalId);
      throw e;
    }
    await this.audit.log('admin.bootstrapped', { targetType: 'user', targetId: acc.externalId });
    return { created: true };
  }
}
```

```ts
// src/cli/create-admin.ts (executado a partir do build: Nest precisa de decorator metadata, então não usar tsx)
import 'reflect-metadata';
import { NestFactory } from '@nestjs/core';
import { AppModule } from '../app.module';
import { AdminBootstrapService } from '../accounts/admin-bootstrap.service';

async function main() {
  const { ADMIN_EMAIL, ADMIN_NAME, ADMIN_PASSWORD } = process.env;
  if (!ADMIN_EMAIL || !ADMIN_PASSWORD) throw new Error('ADMIN_EMAIL and ADMIN_PASSWORD are required');
  const app = await NestFactory.createApplicationContext(AppModule, { logger: ['error'] });
  const r = await app.get(AdminBootstrapService).ensureAdmin(ADMIN_EMAIL, ADMIN_NAME ?? 'Admin', ADMIN_PASSWORD);
  console.log(r.created ? `admin created: ${ADMIN_EMAIL}` : `admin already exists: ${ADMIN_EMAIL}`);
  await app.close();
}
main().catch((e) => { console.error(e.message); process.exit(1); });
```

`scripts/demo-identity.sh` (roda no `workspace`; usa a rota interna com service key, sem precisar assinar contexto):

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
ID=${IDENTITY_URL:-http://identity-service:3001}; KC=${KEYCLOAK_URL:-http://keycloak:8080}
PW='Sup3r-secret-pw'; E="demo$(date +%s)@example.com"
j() { curl -sS -H 'content-type: application/json' "$@"; }
say() { printf '\n== %s\n' "$*"; }

say "1. cadastro do personal"; j -X POST "$ID/accounts/register" -d "{\"email\":\"$E\",\"name\":\"Rafael Silva\",\"password\":\"$PW\",\"acceptTerms\":true,\"termsVersion\":\"2025-04-12\"}"
say "2. login antes de verificar o e-mail (deve dar 403)"; curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' -H 'content-type: application/json' -X POST "$ID/sessions/login" -d "{\"email\":\"$E\",\"password\":\"$PW\"}"
say "3. e-mail de verificação chegou no Mailpit"; curl -sS http://mailpit:8025/api/v1/messages | jq -r '.messages[0] | "para: \(.To[0].Address) | assunto: \(.Subject)"'
KT=$(curl -sS -d "client_id=admin-cli&username=$KEYCLOAK_ADMIN&password=$KEYCLOAK_ADMIN_PASSWORD&grant_type=password" "$KC/realms/master/protocol/openid-connect/token" | jq -r .access_token)
UID_KC=$(curl -sS -H "authorization: Bearer $KT" "$KC/admin/realms/aura/users?email=$E&exact=true" | jq -r '.[0].id')
curl -sS -X PUT -H "authorization: Bearer $KT" -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"emailVerified":true}' "$KC/admin/realms/aura/users/$UID_KC" >/dev/null
say "4. login após verificar (simula o clique no link)"; L=$(j -X POST "$ID/sessions/login" -d "{\"email\":\"$E\",\"password\":\"$PW\"}"); echo "$L" | jq '{memberships, activeMembershipId}'
SID=$(echo "$L" | jq -r .tokens.accessToken | cut -d. -f2 | base64 -d 2>/dev/null | jq -r .sid); SUB=$(echo "$L" | jq -r .tokens.accessToken | cut -d. -f2 | base64 -d 2>/dev/null | jq -r .sub)
say "5. contexto de autorização resolvido pelo identity (o que o BFF consome)"; j -X POST "$ID/internal/authorization/resolve" -H "x-service-key: $INTERNAL_SERVICE_KEY" -d "{\"subject\":\"$SUB\",\"sessionId\":\"$SID\"}" | jq '.context | {role, tenantId, permissions}'
say "6. senha errada e e-mail inexistente dão a mesma resposta"; j -X POST "$ID/sessions/login" -d "{\"email\":\"$E\",\"password\":\"errada-errada\"}" | jq -c 'del(.correlationId)'; j -X POST "$ID/sessions/login" -d '{"email":"ninguem@example.com","password":"errada-errada"}' | jq -c 'del(.correlationId)'
```

### Task 11: BFF — JWT, contexto assinado, cookies/CSRF e encaminhamento

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/kv/package.json`, `tsconfig.json`, `src/index.ts` (movido do identity: `kv.port.ts`, `in-memory.kv.ts`, `redis.kv.ts`)
- Modify: imports do identity-service que usam `./shared/kv/*` → `@aura/kv`; `packages/http-security/src/apply-security.ts` e `exception.filter.ts`
- Create: `apps/bff/package.json`, `tsconfig.json`, `tsconfig.build.json`, `nest-cli.json`, `vitest.config.ts`
- Create: `apps/bff/src/main.ts`, `app.module.ts`, `config/env.ts`, `identity/identity-client.ts`, `auth/jwt-verifier.ts`, `auth/context-resolver.ts`, `auth/bearer.guard.ts`, `auth/decorators.ts`, `auth/csrf.ts`, `auth/auth.controller.ts`, `forward/forward.controller.ts`, `health.controller.ts`
- Modify: `docker-compose.yml` (serviço `bff`), `.env.example`
- Test: `apps/bff/test/bff.e2e.test.ts`, `packages/http-security/src/http-security.test.ts` (2 casos novos)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `signContext`, `AUTHZ_VERSION_KEY` (`@aura/internal-context`); rotas do identity das Tasks 6 a 8; `applySecurity`, `AllExceptionsFilter` (Task 9).
- Produces:
  - `@aura/kv`: `KV_STORE`, `KeyValueStore`, `InMemoryKeyValueStore`, `RedisKeyValueStore`, `SESSION_TTL_SEC`, `sessionMembershipKey`.
  - `IDENTITY_CLIENT` / `interface IdentityClient { call(r: { method: string; path: string; query?: string; body?: unknown; contextHeader?: string; serviceKey?: boolean; correlationId: string; ip?: string }): Promise<{ status: number; body: any }> }`; falha de rede/timeout → `BadGatewayException({ code: 'upstream_unavailable' })`.
  - `JWT_VERIFIER` / `interface JwtVerifier { verify(token: string): Promise<{ sub: string; sid: string; azp: string }> }`; expirado → erro com `code = 'token_expired'`.
  - `BFF_CONFIG: { internalContextSecret: string; corsOrigins: string[]; cookieSecure: boolean }`.
  - Decoradores `@Public()`, `@AllowWithoutMembership()`.
  - Rotas do BFF: `POST /auth/login|register|register-aluno|refresh|logout|forgot-password|resend-verification` (públicas), `POST /auth/select-membership` (Bearer, sem membership). Encaminhadas ao identity com contexto assinado: `/me`, `/invites`, `/roles`, `/roles/:id/permissions`, `/permissions`, `/users`, `/users/:id/status`.
  - Cookies do web: `aura_rt` (`HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax; Path=/auth; Secure` em prod) e `aura_csrf` (legível por JS, double-submit). Mobile (`x-client: mobile`) recebe `refreshToken` no corpo e nenhum cookie.

- [ ] **Step 1: Extrair `@aura/kv` (movimento puro) e ajustar http-security**

```bash
mkdir -p packages/kv/src
git mv apps/identity-service/src/shared/kv/kv.port.ts packages/kv/src/kv.port.ts
git mv apps/identity-service/src/shared/kv/in-memory.kv.ts packages/kv/src/in-memory.kv.ts
git mv apps/identity-service/src/shared/kv/redis.kv.ts packages/kv/src/redis.kv.ts
```

`packages/kv/src/index.ts`:

```ts
export * from './kv.port';
export * from './in-memory.kv';
export * from './redis.kv';
```

`packages/kv/package.json` (`main: dist/index.js`, `types: dist/index.d.ts`, script `build: tsc`, dependência `ioredis ^5.4.1`) e `tsconfig.json` como nos demais pacotes. Em todos os arquivos do identity-service (e testes), trocar `'../shared/kv/kv.port'`, `'../shared/kv/in-memory.kv'`, `'../shared/kv/redis.kv'` e `'./shared/kv/...'` por `'@aura/kv'`; adicionar `"@aura/kv": "workspace:*"` ao identity-service.

Em `packages/http-security/src/apply-security.ts`, o `trust proxy` também deve valer fora de produção quando `trustProxy` for informado (o identity confia no BFF para obter o IP real):

```ts
if (o.isProd || o.trustProxy !== undefined) http.set('trust proxy', o.trustProxy ?? 1);
```

Em `exception.filter.ts`, um `HttpException` com `code` explícito mantém seu status mesmo se ≥ 500 (ex.: `upstream_unavailable`):


```ts
import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Test } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { AUTHZ_VERSION_KEY, verifyContext } from '@aura/internal-context';
import { AllExceptionsFilter, applySecurity, correlationMiddleware } from '@aura/http-security';
import { InMemoryKeyValueStore, KV_STORE } from '@aura/kv';
import cookieParser from 'cookie-parser';
import { createLocalJWKSet, exportJWK, generateKeyPair, SignJWT } from 'jose';
import request from 'supertest';
import { beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { AppModule } from '../src/app.module';
import { BFF_CONFIG } from '../src/config/env';
import { IDENTITY_CLIENT, type IdentityClient } from '../src/identity/identity-client';
import { JWT_VERIFIER, JoseJwtVerifier } from '../src/auth/jwt-verifier';
import { BadGatewayException } from '@nestjs/common';

const SECRET = 'bff-internal-context-secret-32-chars!!';
const ISS = 'http://kc.test/realms/aura';
const ORIGIN = 'https://app.aura.test';
const HOST = 'api.aura.test';

let privateKey: CryptoKey;
let jwks: ReturnType<typeof createLocalJWKSet>;

async function token(over: { sub?: string; sid?: string; azp?: string; iss?: string; exp?: string } = {}) {
  return new SignJWT({ sid: over.sid ?? 'sid-1', azp: over.azp ?? 'aura-identity' })
    .setProtectedHeader({ alg: 'RS256', kid: 'k1' })
    .setSubject(over.sub ?? 'sub-1').setIssuer(over.iss ?? ISS)
    .setIssuedAt().setExpirationTime(over.exp ?? '10m').sign(privateKey);
}

class FakeIdentity implements IdentityClient {
  calls: Parameters<IdentityClient['call']>[0][] = [];
  version = '0';
  resolveStatus: 'ok' | 'needs_selection' | 'denied' = 'ok';
  down = false;
  async call(r: Parameters<IdentityClient['call']>[0]) {
    this.calls.push(r);
    if (this.down) throw new BadGatewayException({ code: 'upstream_unavailable', message: 'Upstream unavailable' });
    if (r.path === '/internal/authorization/resolve') {
      if (this.resolveStatus === 'ok') {
        return { status: 200, body: { status: 'ok', version: this.version, context: {
          userId: 'user-1', tenantId: 'tenant-1', membershipId: 'm-1', role: 'personal', permissions: ['profile:read'] } } };
      }
      return { status: 200, body: this.resolveStatus === 'needs_selection'
        ? { status: 'needs_selection', version: this.version, memberships: [{ id: 'm-1', role: 'personal', tenantId: 't', tenantName: 'T' }] }
        : { status: 'denied', version: this.version } };
    }
    if (r.path === '/sessions/login') {
      return { status: 201, body: { tokens: { accessToken: 'at-1', refreshToken: 'rt-1', expiresIn: 600 }, memberships: [], activeMembershipId: 'm-1' } };
    }
    if (r.path === '/sessions/refresh') return { status: 201, body: { accessToken: 'at-2', refreshToken: 'rt-2', expiresIn: 600 } };
    if (r.path === '/sessions/logout' || r.path === '/internal/sessions/select-membership') return { status: 204, body: undefined };
    if (r.path === '/me') return { status: 200, body: { id: 'user-1', email: 'a@x.com' } };
    return { status: 400, body: { code: 'validation_error', message: 'Invalid request' } }; // nunca 404: o teste de drift usa 404 como "rota inexistente no BFF"
  }
}

let app: INestApplication;
let identity: FakeIdentity;
let kv: InMemoryKeyValueStore;

beforeAll(async () => {
  const pair = await generateKeyPair('RS256');
  privateKey = pair.privateKey;
  jwks = createLocalJWKSet({ keys: [{ ...(await exportJWK(pair.publicKey)), kid: 'k1', alg: 'RS256' }] });
});

beforeEach(async () => {
  identity = new FakeIdentity();
  kv = new InMemoryKeyValueStore();
  const mod = await Test.createTestingModule({ imports: [AppModule] })
    .overrideProvider(IDENTITY_CLIENT).useValue(identity)
    .overrideProvider(KV_STORE).useValue(kv)
    .overrideProvider(BFF_CONFIG).useValue({ internalContextSecret: SECRET, corsOrigins: [ORIGIN], cookieSecure: false })
    .overrideProvider(JWT_VERIFIER).useValue(new JoseJwtVerifier(jwks, ISS, ['aura-identity', 'aura-web']))
    .compile();
  app = mod.createNestApplication();
  app.use(correlationMiddleware);
  app.use(cookieParser());
  applySecurity(app, { allowedHosts: [HOST], corsOrigins: [ORIGIN], corsCredentials: true, isProd: false, trustProxy: 1 });
  app.useGlobalFilters(new AllExceptionsFilter());
  await app.init();
});

const api = () => request(app.getHttpServer());
const get = (p: string) => api().get(p).set('Host', HOST);
const post = (p: string) => api().post(p).set('Host', HOST);

describe('bearer + context', () => {
  it('401 without a token', async () => {
    await get('/me').expect(401);
  });

  it('401 for wrong issuer, wrong azp and garbage; token_expired for expired', async () => {
    await get('/me').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${await token({ iss: 'http://evil/realms/aura' })}`).expect(401);
    await get('/me').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${await token({ azp: 'other-client' })}`).expect(401);
    await get('/me').set('Authorization', 'Bearer not.a.jwt').expect(401);
    const expired = await get('/me').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${await token({ exp: '-1m' })}`).expect(401);
    expect(expired.body.code).toBe('token_expired');
  });

  it('forwards with a signed context and ignores client-supplied internal headers', async () => {
    await get('/me')
      .set('Authorization', `Bearer ${await token()}`)
      .set('x-internal-context', 'forged').set('x-service-key', 'forged').expect(200);
    const fwd = identity.calls.find((c) => c.path === '/me')!;
    const ctx = verifyContext(fwd.contextHeader!, SECRET)!;
    expect(ctx).toMatchObject({ userId: 'user-1', tenantId: 'tenant-1', role: 'personal' });
    expect(fwd.contextHeader).not.toBe('forged');
    expect(fwd.serviceKey).toBeFalsy();
  });

  it('403 profile_selection_required when the session has no active membership', async () => {
    identity.resolveStatus = 'needs_selection';
    const r = await get('/me').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${await token()}`).expect(403);
    expect(r.body.code).toBe('profile_selection_required');
  });

  it('401 when identity denies the subject (suspended)', async () => {
    identity.resolveStatus = 'denied';
    await get('/me').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${await token()}`).expect(401);
  });

  it('caches the context per session and refreshes after an authz version bump', async () => {
    const t = await token();
    await get('/me').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${t}`).expect(200);
    await get('/me').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${t}`).expect(200);
    expect(identity.calls.filter((c) => c.path === '/internal/authorization/resolve')).toHaveLength(1);
    await kv.incr(AUTHZ_VERSION_KEY);
    await get('/me').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${t}`).expect(200);
    expect(identity.calls.filter((c) => c.path === '/internal/authorization/resolve')).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it('select-membership works without a membership and drops the cached context', async () => {
    identity.resolveStatus = 'needs_selection';
    const t = await token();
    await post('/auth/select-membership').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${t}`).send({ membershipId: '6f1c1f0e-8a3e-4f57-9a3e-1b2c3d4e5f60' }).expect(204);
    const sel = identity.calls.find((c) => c.path === '/internal/sessions/select-membership')!;
    expect(sel.body).toMatchObject({ subject: 'sub-1', sessionId: 'sid-1' });
    expect(sel.serviceKey).toBe(true);
    expect(await kv.get('authctx:sid-1')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('does not forward paths outside the allowlist', async () => {
    await get('/internal/authorization/resolve').set('Authorization', `Bearer ${await token()}`).expect(404);
  });
});

describe('auth endpoints', () => {
  it('web login: refresh token only in HttpOnly cookie, CSRF cookie readable', async () => {
    const r = await post('/auth/login').set('Origin', ORIGIN).send({ email: 'a@x.com', password: 'pw' }).expect(201);
    expect(r.body.accessToken).toBe('at-1');
    expect(r.body).not.toHaveProperty('refreshToken');
    const cookies = ([] as string[]).concat(r.headers['set-cookie'] as unknown as string[]);
    const rt = cookies.find((c) => c.startsWith('aura_rt='))!;
    expect(rt).toMatch(/HttpOnly/i);
    expect(rt).toMatch(/SameSite=Lax/i);
    expect(rt).toMatch(/Path=\/auth/);
    const csrf = cookies.find((c) => c.startsWith('aura_csrf='))!;
    expect(csrf).not.toMatch(/HttpOnly/i);
  });

  it('mobile login: refresh token in the body, no cookies', async () => {
    const r = await post('/auth/login').set('x-client', 'mobile').send({ email: 'a@x.com', password: 'pw' }).expect(201);
    expect(r.body.refreshToken).toBe('rt-1');
    expect(r.headers['set-cookie']).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('cookie refresh needs Origin + CSRF token (403 otherwise)', async () => {
    const cookie = 'aura_rt=rt-1; aura_csrf=abc123';
    await post('/auth/refresh').set('Cookie', cookie).expect(403);
    await post('/auth/refresh').set('Cookie', cookie).set('Origin', 'https://evil.example').set('x-csrf-token', 'abc123').expect(403);
    await post('/auth/refresh').set('Cookie', cookie).set('Origin', ORIGIN).set('x-csrf-token', 'wrong').expect(403);
    const ok = await post('/auth/refresh').set('Cookie', cookie).set('Origin', ORIGIN).set('x-csrf-token', 'abc123').expect(201);
    expect(ok.body.accessToken).toBe('at-2');
    expect(ok.body).not.toHaveProperty('refreshToken');
    expect(JSON.stringify(ok.headers['set-cookie'])).toContain('aura_rt=rt-2');
  });

  it('mobile refresh with a body token needs no CSRF', async () => {
    const ok = await post('/auth/refresh').send({ refreshToken: 'rt-mobile-token-123' }).expect(201);
    expect(ok.body.refreshToken).toBe('rt-2');
  });

  it('logout clears cookies (with CSRF) and is idempotent without a token', async () => {
    const r = await post('/auth/logout').set('Cookie', 'aura_rt=rt-1; aura_csrf=abc123')
      .set('Origin', ORIGIN).set('x-csrf-token', 'abc123').expect(204);
    expect(JSON.stringify(r.headers['set-cookie'])).toMatch(/aura_rt=;/);
    await post('/auth/logout').send({}).expect(204);
  });

  it('public auth routes need no Bearer token', async () => {
    await post('/auth/forgot-password').send({ email: 'a@x.com' }).expect((r) => expect(r.status).not.toBe(401));
  });
});

describe('resilience', () => {
  it('502 with a generic body when identity is down', async () => {
    identity.down = true;
    const r = await post('/auth/login').send({ email: 'a@x.com', password: 'pw' }).expect(502);
    expect(r.body).toMatchObject({ code: 'upstream_unavailable', message: 'Upstream unavailable' });
    expect(JSON.stringify(r.body)).not.toMatch(/ECONN|identity-service|stack/);
  });

  it('rate limits auth endpoints', async () => {
    let last = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 25; i++) last = (await post('/auth/login').send({ email: 'a@x.com', password: 'pw' })).status;
    expect(last).toBe(429);
  });
});
```

`src/config/env.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';

export const BFF_CONFIG = 'BFF_CONFIG';
export interface BffConfig { internalContextSecret: string; corsOrigins: string[]; cookieSecure: boolean }

const schema = z.object({
  PORT: z.coerce.number().default(3000),
  IDENTITY_URL: z.string().url(),
  INTERNAL_SERVICE_KEY: z.string().min(32),
  INTERNAL_CONTEXT_SECRET: z.string().min(32),
  KEYCLOAK_ISSUER: z.string().url(),
  KEYCLOAK_JWKS_URL: z.string().url(),
  ALLOWED_AZP: z.string().default('aura-identity,aura-web'),
  REDIS_URL: z.string().url(),
  ALLOWED_HOSTS: z.string().min(1),
  CORS_ORIGINS: z.string().min(1),
});
export type BffEnv = z.infer<typeof schema>;

export function loadBffEnv(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): BffEnv {
  const r = schema.safeParse(env);
  if (!r.success) throw new Error(`Invalid environment: ${r.error.issues.map((i) => i.path.join('.')).join(', ')}`);
  return r.data;
}
```

`src/identity/identity-client.ts`:

```ts
import { BadGatewayException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';

export const IDENTITY_CLIENT = Symbol('IDENTITY_CLIENT');

export interface IdentityCall {
  method: string; path: string; query?: string; body?: unknown;
  contextHeader?: string; serviceKey?: boolean; correlationId: string; ip?: string;
}
export interface IdentityClient { call(r: IdentityCall): Promise<{ status: number; body: any }> }

@Injectable()
export class HttpIdentityClient implements IdentityClient {
  async call(r: IdentityCall) {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-correlation-id': r.correlationId };
    if (r.contextHeader) headers['x-internal-context'] = r.contextHeader;
    if (r.serviceKey) headers['x-service-key'] = process.env.INTERNAL_SERVICE_KEY!;
    if (r.ip) headers['x-forwarded-for'] = r.ip;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`${process.env.IDENTITY_URL}${r.path}${r.query ?? ''}`, {
        method: r.method, headers, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000),
        body: r.body === undefined || r.method === 'GET' ? undefined : JSON.stringify(r.body),
      });
      const text = await res.text();
      return { status: res.status, body: text ? JSON.parse(text) : undefined };
    } catch {
      throw new BadGatewayException({ code: 'upstream_unavailable', message: 'Upstream unavailable' });
    }
  }
}
```

`src/auth/jwt-verifier.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { createRemoteJWKSet, errors, jwtVerify, type JWTVerifyGetKey } from 'jose';

export const JWT_VERIFIER = Symbol('JWT_VERIFIER');
export interface VerifiedClaims { sub: string; sid: string; azp: string }
export interface JwtVerifier { verify(token: string): Promise<VerifiedClaims> }

export class TokenError extends Error { constructor(public code: 'token_expired' | 'unauthorized') { super(code); } }

@Injectable()
export class JoseJwtVerifier implements JwtVerifier {
  constructor(private readonly keys: JWTVerifyGetKey, private readonly issuer: string, private readonly allowedAzp: string[]) {}

  static fromEnv(env = process.env) {
    return new JoseJwtVerifier(
      createRemoteJWKSet(new URL(env.KEYCLOAK_JWKS_URL!), { cooldownDuration: 30_000 }),
      env.KEYCLOAK_ISSUER!, (env.ALLOWED_AZP ?? 'aura-identity,aura-web').split(','),
    );
  }

  async verify(token: string): Promise<VerifiedClaims> {
    try {
      const { payload } = await jwtVerify(token, this.keys, { issuer: this.issuer, algorithms: ['RS256'] });
      const { sub, sid, azp } = payload as { sub?: string; sid?: string; azp?: string };
      if (!sub || !sid || !azp || !this.allowedAzp.includes(azp)) throw new TokenError('unauthorized');
      return { sub, sid, azp };
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof TokenError) throw e;
      throw new TokenError(e instanceof errors.JWTExpired ? 'token_expired' : 'unauthorized');
    }
  }
}
```

`src/auth/context-resolver.ts`:

```ts
import { BadGatewayException, Inject, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { AUTHZ_VERSION_KEY, type InternalContext } from '@aura/internal-context';
import { KV_STORE, type KeyValueStore } from '@aura/kv';
import { IDENTITY_CLIENT, type IdentityClient } from '../identity/identity-client';
import type { VerifiedClaims } from './jwt-verifier';

export type Resolved =
  | { status: 'ok'; context: Omit<InternalContext, 'correlationId'> }
  | { status: 'needs_selection'; memberships: unknown[] }
  | { status: 'denied' };

export const authCtxKey = (sid: string) => `authctx:${sid}`;

@Injectable()
export class ContextResolver {
  constructor(@Inject(IDENTITY_CLIENT) private readonly identity: IdentityClient, @Inject(KV_STORE) private readonly kv: KeyValueStore) {}

  async resolve(claims: VerifiedClaims, correlationId: string): Promise<Resolved> {
    const version = (await this.kv.get(AUTHZ_VERSION_KEY)) ?? '0';
    const cached = await this.kv.get(authCtxKey(claims.sid));
    if (cached) {
      const c = JSON.parse(cached) as { version: string; sub: string; context: Omit<InternalContext, 'correlationId'> };
      if (c.version === version && c.sub === claims.sub) return { status: 'ok', context: c.context };
    }
    const r = await this.identity.call({
      method: 'POST', path: '/internal/authorization/resolve', body: { subject: claims.sub, sessionId: claims.sid },
      serviceKey: true, correlationId,
    });
    if (r.status !== 200) throw new BadGatewayException({ code: 'upstream_unavailable', message: 'Upstream unavailable' });
    if (r.body.status === 'ok') {
      await this.kv.set(authCtxKey(claims.sid), JSON.stringify({ version: r.body.version, sub: claims.sub, context: r.body.context }), 60);
    }
    return r.body as Resolved;
  }
}
```

`src/auth/bearer.guard.ts`:

```ts
import { CanActivate, ExecutionContext, ForbiddenException, Inject, Injectable, UnauthorizedException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';
import { signContext } from '@aura/internal-context';
import { BFF_CONFIG, type BffConfig } from '../config/env';
import { ContextResolver } from './context-resolver';
import { NO_MEMBERSHIP_KEY, PUBLIC_KEY } from './decorators';
import { JWT_VERIFIER, TokenError, type JwtVerifier } from './jwt-verifier';

@Injectable()
export class BearerGuard implements CanActivate {
  constructor(
    private readonly reflector: Reflector,
    @Inject(JWT_VERIFIER) private readonly verifier: JwtVerifier,
    private readonly resolver: ContextResolver,
    @Inject(BFF_CONFIG) private readonly cfg: BffConfig,
  ) {}

  async canActivate(context: ExecutionContext): Promise<boolean> {
    const targets = [context.getHandler(), context.getClass()];
    if (this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<boolean>(PUBLIC_KEY, targets)) return true;

    const req = context.switchToHttp().getRequest();
    const m = /^Bearer (.+)$/.exec(req.headers.authorization ?? '');
    if (!m) throw new UnauthorizedException({ code: 'unauthorized', message: 'Unauthorized' });

    let claims;
    try {
      claims = await this.verifier.verify(m[1]);
    } catch (e) {
      const code = e instanceof TokenError ? e.code : 'unauthorized';
      throw new UnauthorizedException({ code, message: 'Unauthorized' });
    }
    req.claims = claims;

    const result = await this.resolver.resolve(claims, req.correlationId);
    if (result.status === 'denied') throw new UnauthorizedException({ code: 'unauthorized', message: 'Unauthorized' });
    if (result.status === 'needs_selection') {
      if (this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<boolean>(NO_MEMBERSHIP_KEY, targets)) return true;
      throw new ForbiddenException({ code: 'profile_selection_required', message: 'Profile selection required' });
    }
    req.internalContext = signContext({ ...result.context, correlationId: req.correlationId }, this.cfg.internalContextSecret);
    return true;
  }
}
```

`src/auth/csrf.ts`:

```ts
import { ForbiddenException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';

export const RT_COOKIE = 'aura_rt';
export const CSRF_COOKIE = 'aura_csrf';

const safeEqual = (a: string, b: string) => a.length === b.length && timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(a), Buffer.from(b));

/** Só para requisições que usam o cookie de refresh: Origin na allowlist + double-submit token. */
export function assertCsrf(req: any, allowedOrigins: string[]): void {
  const origin = req.headers.origin;
  const cookie = req.cookies?.[CSRF_COOKIE];
  const header = req.headers['x-csrf-token'];
  const ok = typeof origin === 'string' && allowedOrigins.includes(origin)
    && typeof cookie === 'string' && cookie.length > 0 && typeof header === 'string' && safeEqual(cookie, header);
  if (!ok) throw new ForbiddenException({ code: 'csrf', message: 'Access denied' });
}
```

```ts
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { Body, Controller, HttpCode, HttpException, Inject, Post, Req, Res } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Throttle } from '@nestjs/throttler';
import { KV_STORE, type KeyValueStore } from '@aura/kv';
import type { Request, Response } from 'express';
import { BFF_CONFIG, type BffConfig } from '../config/env';
import { IDENTITY_CLIENT, type IdentityClient } from '../identity/identity-client';
import { authCtxKey } from './context-resolver';
import { AllowWithoutMembership, Public } from './decorators';
import { assertCsrf, CSRF_COOKIE, RT_COOKIE } from './csrf';

const WEEK_MS = 7 * 24 * 3600 * 1000;

@Controller('auth')
@Throttle({ default: { limit: 20, ttl: 60_000 } })
export class AuthController {
  constructor(
    @Inject(IDENTITY_CLIENT) private readonly identity: IdentityClient,
    @Inject(KV_STORE) private readonly kv: KeyValueStore,
    @Inject(BFF_CONFIG) private readonly cfg: BffConfig,
  ) {}

  private async call(req: any, path: string, body: unknown, expected: number[]) {
    const r = await this.identity.call({ method: 'POST', path, body, correlationId: req.correlationId, ip: req.ip });
    if (!expected.includes(r.status)) throw new HttpException(r.body ?? { code: 'error', message: 'Error' }, r.status);
    return r.body;
  }

  private setSessionCookies(res: Response, refreshToken: string) {
    res.cookie(RT_COOKIE, refreshToken, { httpOnly: true, secure: this.cfg.cookieSecure, sameSite: 'lax', path: '/auth', maxAge: WEEK_MS });
    res.cookie(CSRF_COOKIE, randomBytes(32).toString('base64url'), { httpOnly: false, secure: this.cfg.cookieSecure, sameSite: 'lax', path: '/', maxAge: WEEK_MS });
  }

  private clearSessionCookies(res: Response) {
    res.clearCookie(RT_COOKIE, { path: '/auth' });
    res.clearCookie(CSRF_COOKIE, { path: '/' });
  }

  @Public() @Post('login')
  async login(@Body() body: unknown, @Req() req: Request, @Res({ passthrough: true }) res: Response) {
    const r = await this.call(req, '/sessions/login', body, [201]);
    const { tokens, ...rest } = r;
    if (req.headers['x-client'] === 'mobile') return { ...rest, accessToken: tokens.accessToken, expiresIn: tokens.expiresIn, refreshToken: tokens.refreshToken };
    this.setSessionCookies(res, tokens.refreshToken);
    return { ...rest, accessToken: tokens.accessToken, expiresIn: tokens.expiresIn };
  }

  @Public() @Post('refresh')
  async refresh(@Body() body: { refreshToken?: string }, @Req() req: Request, @Res({ passthrough: true }) res: Response) {
    const cookieToken = req.cookies?.[RT_COOKIE] as string | undefined;
    if (cookieToken) assertCsrf(req, this.cfg.corsOrigins);
    const refreshToken = cookieToken ?? body?.refreshToken;
    if (typeof refreshToken !== 'string' || refreshToken.length < 10) {
      throw new HttpException({ code: 'session_expired', message: 'Session expired' }, 401);
    }
    try {
      const t = await this.call(req, '/sessions/refresh', { refreshToken }, [201]);
      if (!cookieToken) return t;
      this.setSessionCookies(res, t.refreshToken);
      return { accessToken: t.accessToken, expiresIn: t.expiresIn };
    } catch (e) {
      if (cookieToken && e instanceof HttpException && e.getStatus() === 401) this.clearSessionCookies(res);
      throw e;
    }
  }

  @Public() @HttpCode(204) @Post('logout')
  async logout(@Body() body: { refreshToken?: string }, @Req() req: Request, @Res({ passthrough: true }) res: Response) {
    const cookieToken = req.cookies?.[RT_COOKIE] as string | undefined;
    if (cookieToken) assertCsrf(req, this.cfg.corsOrigins);
    const refreshToken = cookieToken ?? body?.refreshToken;
    if (typeof refreshToken === 'string' && refreshToken.length >= 10) await this.call(req, '/sessions/logout', { refreshToken }, [204]);
    this.clearSessionCookies(res);
  }

  @Public() @Post('register')
  register(@Body() body: unknown, @Req() req: Request) { return this.call(req, '/accounts/register', body, [201]); }

  @Public() @Post('register-aluno')
  registerAluno(@Body() body: unknown, @Req() req: Request) { return this.call(req, '/accounts/register-aluno', body, [201]); }

  @Public() @HttpCode(204) @Post('forgot-password')
  async forgot(@Body() body: unknown, @Req() req: Request) { await this.call(req, '/accounts/forgot-password', body, [204]); }

  @Public() @HttpCode(204) @Post('resend-verification')
  async resend(@Body() body: unknown, @Req() req: Request) { await this.call(req, '/accounts/resend-verification', body, [204]); }

  @AllowWithoutMembership() @HttpCode(204) @Post('select-membership')
  async select(@Body() body: { membershipId?: string }, @Req() req: any) {
    const r = await this.identity.call({
      method: 'POST', path: '/internal/sessions/select-membership', serviceKey: true, correlationId: req.correlationId,
      body: { subject: req.claims.sub, sessionId: req.claims.sid, membershipId: body?.membershipId },
    });
    if (r.status !== 204) throw new HttpException(r.body ?? { code: 'error', message: 'Error' }, r.status);
    await this.kv.del(authCtxKey(req.claims.sid));
  }
}
```

`src/forward/forward.controller.ts`:

```ts
import { All, Controller, Inject, Req, Res } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { Request, Response } from 'express';
import { IDENTITY_CLIENT, type IdentityClient } from '../identity/identity-client';

const ALLOWED_METHODS = new Set(['GET', 'POST', 'PUT', 'PATCH', 'DELETE']);

@Controller()
export class ForwardController {
  constructor(@Inject(IDENTITY_CLIENT) private readonly identity: IdentityClient) {}

  @All(['me', 'invites', 'roles', 'roles/*', 'permissions', 'users', 'users/*'])
  async forward(@Req() req: any, @Res() res: Response) {
    if (!ALLOWED_METHODS.has(req.method)) return res.status(405).json({ code: 'method_not_allowed', message: 'Method not allowed', correlationId: req.correlationId });
    const qs = req.url.includes('?') ? req.url.slice(req.url.indexOf('?')) : '';
    const r = await this.identity.call({
      method: req.method, path: req.path, query: qs, body: req.body,
      contextHeader: req.internalContext, correlationId: req.correlationId, ip: req.ip,
    });
    if (r.status === 204 || r.body === undefined) return res.status(r.status).end();
    return res.status(r.status).json(r.body);
  }
}
```

`src/health.controller.ts` (público, igual ao do identity mas com `Public` do BFF), `src/app.module.ts`:

```ts
import { Module } from '@nestjs/common';
import { APP_GUARD } from '@nestjs/core';
import { ThrottlerGuard, ThrottlerModule } from '@nestjs/throttler';
import { KV_STORE, RedisKeyValueStore } from '@aura/kv';
import Redis from 'ioredis';
import { AuthController } from './auth/auth.controller';
import { BearerGuard } from './auth/bearer.guard';
import { ContextResolver } from './auth/context-resolver';
import { JoseJwtVerifier, JWT_VERIFIER } from './auth/jwt-verifier';
import { BFF_CONFIG, loadBffEnv } from './config/env';
import { ForwardController } from './forward/forward.controller';
import { HealthController } from './health.controller';
import { HttpIdentityClient, IDENTITY_CLIENT } from './identity/identity-client';

@Module({
  imports: [ThrottlerModule.forRoot([{ ttl: 60_000, limit: 120 }])],
  controllers: [AuthController, ForwardController, HealthController],
  providers: [
    ContextResolver,
    { provide: APP_GUARD, useClass: ThrottlerGuard },
    { provide: APP_GUARD, useClass: BearerGuard },
    { provide: IDENTITY_CLIENT, useClass: HttpIdentityClient },
    { provide: JWT_VERIFIER, useFactory: () => JoseJwtVerifier.fromEnv() },
    { provide: KV_STORE, useFactory: () => new RedisKeyValueStore(new Redis(process.env.REDIS_URL!)) },
    {
      provide: BFF_CONFIG,
      useFactory: () => {
        const e = loadBffEnv();
        return { internalContextSecret: e.INTERNAL_CONTEXT_SECRET, corsOrigins: e.CORS_ORIGINS.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()), cookieSecure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' };
      },
    },
  ],
})
export class AppModule {}
```

`src/main.ts`:

```ts
import { initOtel } from '@aura/observability';
initOtel('bff');

import 'reflect-metadata';
import { NestFactory } from '@nestjs/core';
import { AllExceptionsFilter, applySecurity, assertProductionSafe, correlationMiddleware, maybeSetupSwagger, parseList } from '@aura/http-security';
import cookieParser from 'cookie-parser';
import { AppModule } from './app.module';
import { loadBffEnv } from './config/env';

async function bootstrap() {
  assertProductionSafe(process.env);
  const env = loadBffEnv();
  const app = await NestFactory.create(AppModule);
  app.use(correlationMiddleware);
  app.use(cookieParser());
  applySecurity(app, {
    allowedHosts: parseList(env.ALLOWED_HOSTS), corsOrigins: parseList(env.CORS_ORIGINS),
    corsCredentials: true, isProd: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production', trustProxy: 1,
  });
  app.useGlobalFilters(new AllExceptionsFilter());
  app.enableShutdownHooks();
  maybeSetupSwagger(app, { title: 'aura-fit BFF' });
  await app.listen(env.PORT, '0.0.0.0');
}
bootstrap();
```

No `main.ts` do identity-service, passar também `trustProxy: 1` em `applySecurity` (o BFF é o único cliente e encaminha o IP real).

`sessions.service.ts`:

```ts
async listMemberships(subject: string): Promise<MembershipDto[]> {
  const user = await this.prisma.user.findUnique({ where: { externalId: subject } });
  if (!user || user.status !== 'ACTIVE') throw new ForbiddenException({ code: 'forbidden', message: 'Access denied' });
  return this.activeMemberships(user.id);
}
```

```ts
@Public() @UseGuards(ServiceKeyGuard) @Post('internal/sessions/memberships')

  return this.sessions.listMemberships(b.subject);
}
```

`apps/bff/src/auth/auth.controller.ts` (importar `Get` de `@nestjs/common` e `sessionMembershipKey` de `@aura/kv`):

```ts
@AllowWithoutMembership() @Get('memberships')
async memberships(@Req() req: any) {
  const r = await this.identity.call({
    method: 'POST', path: '/internal/sessions/memberships', body: { subject: req.claims.sub },
    serviceKey: true, correlationId: req.correlationId,
  });
  if (r.status !== 201) throw new HttpException(r.body ?? { code: 'error', message: 'Error' }, r.status);
  return { memberships: r.body, activeMembershipId: await this.kv.get(sessionMembershipKey(req.claims.sid)) };
}
```

### Task 13: Contratos — Zod → OpenAPI → Orval + cliente HTTP compartilhado

**Decisão:** os DTOs são Zod e o BFF só encaminha para o identity; por isso a fonte de verdade do contrato do front é gerada **dos schemas Zod** (`@asteasolutions/zod-to-openapi`) e não de `@nestjs/swagger`. O Swagger UI segue disponível em dev só para exploração. Um teste de drift garante que toda rota documentada existe no BFF.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/schemas/src/responses.ts` (e export no `index.ts`)
- Create: `packages/contracts/package.json`, `tsconfig.json`, `orval.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`
- Create: `packages/contracts/src/registry.ts`, `src/build-openapi.ts`, `src/http/mutator.ts`, `src/index.ts`
- Test: `packages/contracts/src/openapi.test.ts`, `packages/contracts/src/http/mutator.test.ts`, `apps/bff/test/contract-drift.test.ts`
- Modify: `.gitignore` (`packages/contracts/openapi/`, `packages/contracts/src/generated/`), `Makefile` (alvo `gen` já existe)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `@aura/schemas`: `membershipSchema`, `loginResponseSchema`, `tokenResponseSchema`, `meSchema`, `roleSchema`, `usersPageSchema`, `inviteResponseSchema`, `registerResponseSchema`, `errorSchema`, `membershipsResponseSchema`
  - `buildOpenApi(): OpenAPIObject` com `operationId`s: `login`, `register`, `registerAluno`, `refresh`, `logout`, `forgotPassword`, `resendVerification`, `selectMembership`, `getMemberships`, `getMe`, `updateMe`, `createInvite`, `listRoles`, `listPermissions`, `setRolePermissions`, `listUsers`, `setUserStatus`
  - `configureApi(cfg: ApiConfig)`, `getHttp(): AxiosInstance`, `customInstance<T>(config: AxiosRequestConfig): Promise<T>`
  - `interface ApiConfig { baseURL: string; platform: 'web' | 'mobile'; getAccessToken(): string | null; refresh(): Promise<string | null>; onAuthLost(): void; onProfileSelectionRequired?(): void; getCsrfToken?(): string | null }`
  - Hooks Orval gerados (`useLogin`, `useGetMe`, `useListRoles`, …) em `@aura/contracts`; mutations recebem `{ data }`/`{ id, data }`.
