`packages/contracts/src/http/mutator.test.ts`:

```ts
import MockAdapter from 'axios-mock-adapter';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { configureApi, customInstance, getHttp } from './mutator';

let token: string | null;
let refresh: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
let onAuthLost: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
let onProfile: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
let mock: MockAdapter;

function setup(platform: 'web' | 'mobile' = 'web', csrf: string | null = 'csrf-1') {
  token = 'old';
  refresh = vi.fn(async () => { token = 'new'; return 'new'; });
  onAuthLost = vi.fn();
  onProfile = vi.fn();
  configureApi({
    baseURL: 'http://api.test', platform, getAccessToken: () => token, refresh, onAuthLost,
    onProfileSelectionRequired: onProfile, getCsrfToken: () => csrf,
  });
  mock = new MockAdapter(getHttp());
}

describe('api client', () => {
  beforeEach(() => setup());

  it('sends the bearer token', async () => {
    mock.onGet('/me').reply((c) => [200, { auth: c.headers?.Authorization }]);
    expect(await customInstance<{ auth: string }>({ url: '/me', method: 'GET' })).toEqual({ auth: 'Bearer old' });
  });

  it('refreshes once for concurrent token_expired and retries with the new token', async () => {
    mock.onGet('/me').reply((c) => (c.headers?.Authorization === 'Bearer new' ? [200, { ok: 1 }] : [401, { code: 'token_expired' }]));
    const [a, b] = await Promise.all([
      customInstance({ url: '/me', method: 'GET' }), customInstance({ url: '/me', method: 'GET' }),
    ]);
    expect(a).toEqual({ ok: 1 }); expect(b).toEqual({ ok: 1 });
    expect(refresh).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('calls onAuthLost and rejects when refresh fails', async () => {
    refresh.mockResolvedValueOnce(null);
    mock.onGet('/me').reply(401, { code: 'token_expired' });
    await expect(customInstance({ url: '/me', method: 'GET' })).rejects.toBeTruthy();
    expect(onAuthLost).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('does not retry on /auth/* 401 (no refresh loop)', async () => {
    mock.onPost('/auth/login').reply(401, { code: 'invalid_credentials' });
    await expect(customInstance({ url: '/auth/login', method: 'POST', data: {} })).rejects.toBeTruthy();
    expect(refresh).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('gives up after one retry', async () => {
    mock.onGet('/me').reply(401, { code: 'token_expired' });
    await expect(customInstance({ url: '/me', method: 'GET' })).rejects.toBeTruthy();
    expect(refresh).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('notifies profile_selection_required (403)', async () => {
    mock.onGet('/me').reply(403, { code: 'profile_selection_required' });
    await expect(customInstance({ url: '/me', method: 'GET' })).rejects.toBeTruthy();
    expect(onProfile).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('web: credentials + CSRF header only on refresh/logout', async () => {
    mock.onPost('/auth/refresh').reply((c) => [200, { csrf: c.headers?.['X-CSRF-Token'], cred: c.withCredentials }]);
    mock.onGet('/me').reply((c) => [200, { csrf: c.headers?.['X-CSRF-Token'] ?? null }]);
    expect(await customInstance({ url: '/auth/refresh', method: 'POST' })).toEqual({ csrf: 'csrf-1', cred: true });
    expect(await customInstance({ url: '/me', method: 'GET' })).toEqual({ csrf: null });
  });

  it('mobile: x-client header and no credentials', async () => {
    setup('mobile');
    mock.onGet('/me').reply((c) => [200, { client: c.headers?.['x-client'], cred: c.withCredentials }]);
    expect(await customInstance({ url: '/me', method: 'GET' })).toEqual({ client: 'mobile', cred: false });
  });
});
```

`packages/contracts/src/openapi.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { buildOpenApi } from './build-openapi';

const doc = buildOpenApi();
const ops = Object.entries(doc.paths).flatMap(([path, item]) =>
  Object.entries(item as Record<string, any>).map(([method, op]) => ({ path, method, op })));

describe('openapi', () => {
  it('has unique, expected operationIds', () => {
    const ids = ops.map((o) => o.op.operationId);
    expect(new Set(ids).size).toBe(ids.length);
    for (const id of ['login', 'register', 'registerAluno', 'refresh', 'logout', 'forgotPassword', 'resendVerification',
      'selectMembership', 'getMemberships', 'getMe', 'updateMe', 'createInvite', 'listRoles', 'listPermissions',
      'setRolePermissions', 'listUsers', 'setUserStatus']) expect(ids).toContain(id);
  });
  it('public auth routes have no security requirement, others require bearer', () => {
    const pub = ['login', 'register', 'registerAluno', 'refresh', 'logout', 'forgotPassword', 'resendVerification'];
    for (const { op } of ops) {
      if (pub.includes(op.operationId)) expect(op.security ?? []).toEqual([]);
      else expect(op.security).toEqual([{ bearer: [] }]);
    }
  });
  it('documents the standard error body as the default response', () => {
    for (const { op } of ops) expect(op.responses.default).toBeDefined();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { buildOpenApi } from '@aura/contracts/openapi';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import request from 'supertest';
import { makeApp } from './helpers';

describe('contract drift', () => {
  it('every documented path exists in the BFF (never 404)', async () => {
    const { app } = await makeApp();
    const doc = buildOpenApi();
    for (const [path, item] of Object.entries(doc.paths)) {
      for (const method of Object.keys(item as object)) {
        const url = path.replace(/\{[^}]+\}/g, '1');
        const res = await (request(app.getHttpServer()) as any)[method](url).set('Host', 'api.aura.test');
        expect(res.status, `${method.toUpperCase()} ${path}`).not.toBe(404);
      }
    }
  });
});
```

`packages/schemas/src/responses.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';

export const membershipSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().uuid(), role: z.string(), tenantId: z.string().uuid().nullable(), tenantName: z.string().nullable(),
});
export const loginResponseSchema = z.object({
  accessToken: z.string(), expiresIn: z.number(), refreshToken: z.string().optional(),
  memberships: z.array(membershipSchema), activeMembershipId: z.string().uuid().nullable(),
});
export const tokenResponseSchema = z.object({ accessToken: z.string(), expiresIn: z.number(), refreshToken: z.string().optional() });
export const membershipsResponseSchema = z.object({ memberships: z.array(membershipSchema), activeMembershipId: z.string().uuid().nullable() });
export const meSchema = z.object({
  id: z.string().uuid(), email: z.string().email(), name: z.string(), phone: z.string().nullable(),
  emailVerified: z.boolean(), role: z.enum(['personal', 'aluno', 'admin']),
  tenantId: z.string().uuid().nullable(), permissions: z.array(z.string()),
});
export const roleSchema = z.object({ id: z.number(), name: z.string(), users: z.number(), permissions: z.array(z.string()) });
export const usersPageSchema = z.object({
  items: z.array(z.object({
    id: z.string().uuid(), email: z.string(), name: z.string(), status: z.enum(['PENDING', 'ACTIVE', 'SUSPENDED']),
    emailVerified: z.boolean(), createdAt: z.string(),
    memberships: z.array(z.object({ id: z.string().uuid(), status: z.string(), role: z.object({ name: z.string() }) })),
  })),
  total: z.number(), page: z.number(), pageSize: z.number(),
});
export const inviteResponseSchema = z.object({ code: z.string(), expiresAt: z.string() });
export const registerResponseSchema = z.object({ status: z.literal('pending_verification') });
export const errorSchema = z.object({
  code: z.string(), message: z.string(), correlationId: z.string(),
  fields: z.array(z.object({ path: z.string(), code: z.string() })).optional(),
});
export const refreshBodySchema = z.object({ refreshToken: z.string().min(10).max(4096) }).partial();

export type Membership = z.infer<typeof membershipSchema>;
export type LoginResponse = z.infer<typeof loginResponseSchema>;
export type Me = z.infer<typeof meSchema>;
```

`packages/contracts/src/registry.ts`:

```ts
import { extendZodWithOpenApi, OpenAPIRegistry } from '@asteasolutions/zod-to-openapi';
import * as S from '@aura/schemas';
import { z } from 'zod';

extendZodWithOpenApi(z);
export const registry = new OpenAPIRegistry();
registry.registerComponent('securitySchemes', 'bearer', { type: 'http', scheme: 'bearer', bearerFormat: 'JWT' });

interface R {
  method: 'get' | 'post' | 'put' | 'patch' | 'delete'; path: string; id: string; tag: string;
  body?: z.ZodTypeAny; params?: z.AnyZodObject; query?: z.AnyZodObject; ok: number; res?: z.ZodTypeAny; auth?: boolean;
}
function route(r: R) {
  registry.registerPath({
    method: r.method, path: r.path, operationId: r.id, tags: [r.tag],
    security: r.auth === false ? [] : [{ bearer: [] }],
    request: {
      ...(r.params ? { params: r.params } : {}), ...(r.query ? { query: r.query } : {}),
      ...(r.body ? { body: { content: { 'application/json': { schema: r.body } } } } : {}),
    },
    responses: {
      [r.ok]: { description: 'OK', ...(r.res ? { content: { 'application/json': { schema: r.res } } } : {}) },
      default: { description: 'Error', content: { 'application/json': { schema: S.errorSchema } } },
    },
  });
}

const id = z.object({ id: z.string() });
const numId = z.object({ id: z.coerce.number() });
const email = z.object({ email: z.string().email() });

route({ method: 'post', path: '/auth/login', id: 'login', tag: 'auth', auth: false, body: S.loginSchema, ok: 201, res: S.loginResponseSchema });
route({ method: 'post', path: '/auth/register', id: 'register', tag: 'auth', auth: false, body: S.registerPersonalSchema, ok: 201, res: S.registerResponseSchema });
route({ method: 'post', path: '/auth/register-aluno', id: 'registerAluno', tag: 'auth', auth: false, body: S.registerAlunoSchema, ok: 201, res: S.registerResponseSchema });
route({ method: 'post', path: '/auth/refresh', id: 'refresh', tag: 'auth', auth: false, body: S.refreshBodySchema, ok: 201, res: S.tokenResponseSchema });
route({ method: 'post', path: '/auth/logout', id: 'logout', tag: 'auth', auth: false, body: S.refreshBodySchema, ok: 204 });
route({ method: 'post', path: '/auth/forgot-password', id: 'forgotPassword', tag: 'auth', auth: false, body: email, ok: 204 });
route({ method: 'post', path: '/auth/resend-verification', id: 'resendVerification', tag: 'auth', auth: false, body: email, ok: 204 });
route({ method: 'post', path: '/auth/select-membership', id: 'selectMembership', tag: 'auth', body: S.selectMembershipSchema, ok: 204 });
route({ method: 'get', path: '/auth/memberships', id: 'getMemberships', tag: 'auth', ok: 200, res: S.membershipsResponseSchema });
route({ method: 'get', path: '/me', id: 'getMe', tag: 'profile', ok: 200, res: S.meSchema });
route({ method: 'patch', path: '/me', id: 'updateMe', tag: 'profile', body: S.updateMeSchema, ok: 200, res: S.meSchema.pick({ id: true, email: true, name: true, phone: true }) });
route({ method: 'post', path: '/invites', id: 'createInvite', tag: 'invites', body: S.createInviteSchema, ok: 201, res: S.inviteResponseSchema });
route({ method: 'get', path: '/roles', id: 'listRoles', tag: 'rbac', ok: 200, res: z.array(S.roleSchema) });
route({ method: 'get', path: '/permissions', id: 'listPermissions', tag: 'rbac', ok: 200, res: z.array(z.string()) });
route({ method: 'put', path: '/roles/{id}/permissions', id: 'setRolePermissions', tag: 'rbac', params: numId, body: S.setPermissionsSchema, ok: 204 });
route({ method: 'get', path: '/users', id: 'listUsers', tag: 'rbac', query: z.object({ page: z.coerce.number().optional(), pageSize: z.coerce.number().optional() }), ok: 200, res: S.usersPageSchema });
route({ method: 'patch', path: '/users/{id}/status', id: 'setUserStatus', tag: 'rbac', params: id, body: S.setStatusSchema, ok: 204 });
```

`packages/contracts/src/http/mutator.ts`:

```ts
import axios, { AxiosError, type AxiosInstance, type AxiosRequestConfig, type InternalAxiosRequestConfig } from 'axios';

export interface ApiConfig {
  baseURL: string;
  platform: 'web' | 'mobile';
  getAccessToken(): string | null;
  /** Renova a sessão; devolve o novo access token ou null se falhou. */
  refresh(): Promise<string | null>;
  onAuthLost(): void;
  onProfileSelectionRequired?(): void;
  getCsrfToken?(): string | null;
}

type Retriable = InternalAxiosRequestConfig & { _retried?: boolean };
let cfg: ApiConfig;
let http: AxiosInstance;
let refreshing: Promise<string | null> | null = null;

const isAuthRoute = (url?: string) => !!url && url.startsWith('/auth/') && url !== '/auth/select-membership' && url !== '/auth/memberships';
const needsCsrf = (url?: string) => url === '/auth/refresh' || url === '/auth/logout';

export function configureApi(c: ApiConfig): void {
  cfg = c;
  http = axios.create({
    baseURL: c.baseURL, withCredentials: c.platform === 'web', timeout: 15_000,
    headers: c.platform === 'mobile' ? { 'x-client': 'mobile' } : {},
  });

  http.interceptors.request.use((req) => {
    const token = cfg.getAccessToken();
    if (token) req.headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);
    if (cfg.platform === 'web' && needsCsrf(req.url)) {
      const csrf = cfg.getCsrfToken?.();
      if (csrf) req.headers.set('X-CSRF-Token', csrf);
    }
    return req;
  });

  http.interceptors.response.use(undefined, async (error: AxiosError<{ code?: string }>) => {
    const original = error.config as Retriable | undefined;
    const status = error.response?.status;
    const code = error.response?.data?.code;

    if (status === 403 && code === 'profile_selection_required') cfg.onProfileSelectionRequired?.();

    if (status === 401 && original && !original._retried && !isAuthRoute(original.url)) {
      original._retried = true;
      refreshing ??= cfg.refresh().finally(() => { refreshing = null; });
      const token = await refreshing;
      if (!token) { cfg.onAuthLost(); throw error; }
      original.headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);
      return http.request(original);
    }
    throw error;
  });
}

export const getHttp = (): AxiosInstance => http;

export const customInstance = <T>(config: AxiosRequestConfig, options?: AxiosRequestConfig): Promise<T> =>
  http.request<T>({ ...config, ...options }).then((r) => r.data);

export type ErrorType<E> = AxiosError<E>;
```

### Task 14: Web — fundação (tokens do design system, AuthProvider, guards)

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/package.json`, `index.html`, `vite.config.ts`, `tsconfig.json`, `postcss.config.js`, `tailwind.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/main.tsx`, `src/App.tsx`, `src/styles.css`, `src/test/setup.ts`, `src/test/server.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/auth/AuthProvider.tsx`, `src/auth/guards.tsx`, `src/auth/csrf.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/ui/Button.tsx`, `src/ui/Field.tsx`, `src/ui/AuthLayout.tsx`
- Test: `apps/web/src/auth/auth.test.tsx`
- Modify: `docker-compose.yml` (serviço `web`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `configureApi`, hooks/funções Orval (`login`, `refresh`, `logout`, `getMemberships`, `selectMembership`, `getMe`) de `@aura/contracts`.
- Produces:
  - `AuthProvider`, `useAuth(): AuthApi` com `AuthApi = { status: 'loading' | 'anonymous' | 'needs_profile' | 'authenticated' | 'expired'; me: Me | null; memberships: Membership[]; login(email: string, password: string): Promise<LoginOutcome>; selectProfile(membershipId: string): Promise<void>; logout(): Promise<void>; can(permission: Permission): boolean }`; `type LoginOutcome = 'authenticated' | 'needs_profile'`
  - Erros de login lançados como `ApiError { code: string; status: number }` (`invalid_credentials`, `email_not_verified`, `too_many_attempts`).
  - `RequireAuth`, `RequirePermission({ permission, children })`, `<Spinner/>`
  - Rotas reservadas: `/login`, `/register`, `/forgot-password`, `/verify-email`, `/select-profile`, `/session-expired`, `/forbidden`, `/terms`, `/app/*` (protegidas).
  - Tokens de design (do mockup): primary `#b1111b`, gray `#b0b0b0`, dark `#1f1f1f`, light `#f5f5f5`; fonte Inter.
- Regra: o **access token vive só em memória**; nada de `localStorage`/`sessionStorage` para tokens ou dados de sessão.

`apps/web/src/auth/auth.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { API, M1, M2, ME, server } from '../test/server';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from './AuthProvider';
import { RequireAuth, RequirePermission } from './guards';

function Login() {
  const { login, status } = useAuth();
  return (
    <>
      <span data-testid="status">{status}</span>
      <button onClick={() => login('rafael@example.com', 'Sup3r-secret-pw').catch((e) => (document.title = e.code))}>entrar</button>
    </>
  );
}
const Page = ({ name }: { name: string }) => <div>{name}</div>;

function renderApp(initial = '/app') {
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={new QueryClient()}>
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[initial]}>
        <AuthProvider>
          <Routes>
            <Route path="/login" element={<><Page name="login-page" /><Login /></>} />
            <Route path="/select-profile" element={<Page name="select-profile-page" />} />
            <Route path="/session-expired" element={<Page name="expired-page" />} />
            <Route path="/verify-email" element={<Page name="verify-page" />} />
            <Route path="/forbidden" element={<Page name="forbidden-page" />} />
            <Route path="/app" element={<RequireAuth><Page name="dashboard" /></RequireAuth>} />
            <Route path="/app/admin" element={<RequireAuth><RequirePermission permission="roles:manage"><Page name="admin" /></RequirePermission></RequireAuth>} />
          </Routes>
        </AuthProvider>
      </MemoryRouter>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

const restoreOk = () => server.use(
  http.post(`${API}/auth/refresh`, () => HttpResponse.json({ accessToken: 'at-restored', expiresIn: 600 }, { status: 201 })),
);

describe('session restore', () => {
  it('restores the session silently from the refresh cookie', async () => {
    restoreOk();
    renderApp('/app');
    expect(await screen.findByText('dashboard')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('sends anonymous users to /login', async () => {
    renderApp('/app');
    expect(await screen.findByText('login-page')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('asks for a profile when the restored session has none active', async () => {
    restoreOk();
    server.use(http.get(`${API}/auth/memberships`, () => HttpResponse.json({ memberships: [M1, M2], activeMembershipId: null })));
    renderApp('/app');
    expect(await screen.findByText('select-profile-page')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe('login', () => {
  const loginOk = (body: object) => server.use(
    http.post(`${API}/auth/login`, () => HttpResponse.json(body, { status: 201 })),
  );

  it('single active profile → authenticated, and no token is persisted anywhere', async () => {
    loginOk({ accessToken: 'at-1', expiresIn: 600, memberships: [M1], activeMembershipId: M1.id });
    renderApp('/login');
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByText('entrar'));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByTestId('status')).toHaveTextContent('authenticated'));
    expect(JSON.stringify({ ...localStorage })).not.toMatch(/at-1|token/i);
    expect(JSON.stringify({ ...sessionStorage })).not.toMatch(/at-1|token/i);
  });

  it('several profiles → needs_profile', async () => {
    loginOk({ accessToken: 'at-1', expiresIn: 600, memberships: [M1, M2], activeMembershipId: null });
    renderApp('/login');
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByText('entrar'));
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByTestId('status')).toHaveTextContent('needs_profile'));
  });

  it('surfaces email_not_verified as an error code', async () => {
    server.use(http.post(`${API}/auth/login`, () => HttpResponse.json({ code: 'email_not_verified', message: 'x', correlationId: 'c' }, { status: 403 })));
    renderApp('/login');
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByText('entrar'));
    await waitFor(() => expect(document.title).toBe('email_not_verified'));
  });
});

describe('guards', () => {
  it('RequirePermission redirects to /forbidden without the permission', async () => {
    restoreOk();
    renderApp('/app/admin');
    expect(await screen.findByText('forbidden-page')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('RequirePermission renders children when the permission exists', async () => {
    restoreOk();
    server.use(http.get(`${API}/me`, () => HttpResponse.json({ ...ME, role: 'admin', permissions: ['roles:manage'] })));
    renderApp('/app/admin');
    expect(await screen.findByText('admin')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('a 401 that cannot be refreshed sends the user to /session-expired', async () => {
    restoreOk();
    renderApp('/app');
    await screen.findByText('dashboard');
    server.use(
      http.get(`${API}/me`, () => HttpResponse.json({ code: 'token_expired' }, { status: 401 })),
      http.post(`${API}/auth/refresh`, () => HttpResponse.json({ code: 'session_expired' }, { status: 401 })),
    );
    // dispara uma chamada autenticada qualquer
    const { getHttp } = await import('@aura/contracts');
    await getHttp().get('/me').catch(() => undefined);
    expect(await screen.findByText('expired-page')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`src/auth/AuthProvider.tsx`:

```tsx
import { PERMISSIONS, type Permission } from '@aura/authz';
import { configureApi, getMe, getMemberships, login as apiLogin, logout as apiLogout, refresh as apiRefresh, selectMembership } from '@aura/contracts';
import type { LoginResponse, Me, Membership } from '@aura/schemas';
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { readCsrfCookie } from './csrf';

export type AuthStatus = 'loading' | 'anonymous' | 'needs_profile' | 'authenticated' | 'expired';
export type LoginOutcome = 'authenticated' | 'needs_profile';
export class ApiError extends Error { constructor(public code: string, public status: number) { super(code); } }

export interface AuthApi {
  status: AuthStatus; me: Me | null; memberships: Membership[];
  login(email: string, password: string): Promise<LoginOutcome>;
  selectProfile(membershipId: string): Promise<void>;
  logout(): Promise<void>;
  can(permission: Permission): boolean;
}

const Ctx = createContext<AuthApi | null>(null);
export const useAuth = (): AuthApi => {
  const v = useContext(Ctx);
  if (!v) throw new Error('useAuth outside AuthProvider');
  return v;
};

const toApiError = (e: any) => new ApiError(e?.response?.data?.code ?? 'error', e?.response?.status ?? 0);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const token = useRef<string | null>(null); // access token: só em memória
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<AuthStatus>('loading');
  const [me, setMe] = useState<Me | null>(null);
  const [memberships, setMemberships] = useState<Membership[]>([]);

  const loadProfile = useCallback(async (active: string | null, list: Membership[]) => {
    setMemberships(list);
    if (!active) { setMe(null); setStatus('needs_profile'); return 'needs_profile' as const; }
    setMe(await getMe());
    setStatus('authenticated');
    return 'authenticated' as const;
  }, []);

  useMemo(() => {
    configureApi({
      baseURL: import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL, platform: 'web',
      getAccessToken: () => token.current,
      getCsrfToken: readCsrfCookie,
      refresh: async () => {
        try { token.current = (await apiRefresh({})).accessToken; return token.current; } catch { return null; }
      },
      onAuthLost: () => { token.current = null; setMe(null); setStatus('expired'); navigate('/session-expired', { replace: true }); },
      onProfileSelectionRequired: () => setStatus('needs_profile'),
    });
  }, [navigate]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    (async () => {
      try {
        token.current = (await apiRefresh({})).accessToken;
        const m = await getMemberships();
        if (alive) await loadProfile(m.activeMembershipId, m.memberships);
      } catch {
        if (alive) setStatus('anonymous');
      }
    })();
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, [loadProfile]);

  const api = useMemo<AuthApi>(() => ({
    status, me, memberships,
    async login(email, password) {
      let r: LoginResponse;
      try { r = await apiLogin({ email, password }); } catch (e) { throw toApiError(e); }
      token.current = r.accessToken;
      return loadProfile(r.activeMembershipId, r.memberships);
    },
    async selectProfile(membershipId) {
      await selectMembership({ membershipId });
      const m = await getMemberships();
      await loadProfile(m.activeMembershipId, m.memberships);
    },
    async logout() {
      try { await apiLogout({}); } finally { token.current = null; setMe(null); setMemberships([]); setStatus('anonymous'); }
    },
    can: (p) => me?.permissions.includes(p) ?? false,
  }), [status, me, memberships, loadProfile]);

  return <Ctx.Provider value={api}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

export { PERMISSIONS };
```

`src/auth/guards.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Permission } from '@aura/authz';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Navigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from './AuthProvider';

export const Spinner = () => <div role="status" aria-label="Carregando" className="p-8 text-gray">Carregando…</div>;

export function RequireAuth({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  const loc = useLocation();
  if (status === 'loading') return <Spinner />;
  if (status === 'expired') return <Navigate to="/session-expired" replace />;
  if (status === 'anonymous') return <Navigate to="/login" replace state={{ from: loc.pathname }} />;
  if (status === 'needs_profile') return <Navigate to="/select-profile" replace />;
  return <>{children}</>;
}

/** Deve ficar dentro de <RequireAuth>. A checagem é sempre por permissão, nunca por nome de papel. */
export function RequirePermission({ permission, children }: { permission: Permission; children: ReactNode }) {
  const { can } = useAuth();
  return can(permission) ? <>{children}</> : <Navigate to="/forbidden" replace />;
}
```

```tsx
// Button.tsx
import type { ButtonHTMLAttributes } from 'react';
const styles = {
  primary: 'bg-primary text-white hover:brightness-90',
  secondary: 'border border-primary text-primary bg-transparent hover:bg-primary/5',
  text: 'text-primary bg-transparent hover:underline',
};
export function Button({ variant = 'primary', className = '', ...p }: ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & { variant?: keyof typeof styles }) {
  return <button {...p} className={`rounded-md px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold disabled:opacity-50 ${styles[variant]} ${className}`} />;
}

// Field.tsx
import { forwardRef, type InputHTMLAttributes } from 'react';
export const Field = forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> & { label: string; error?: string }>(
  ({ label, error, id, ...p }, ref) => {
    const fid = id ?? `f-${label.replace(/\W+/g, '-').toLowerCase()}`;
    return (
      <div className="space-y-1">
        <label htmlFor={fid} className="text-sm font-medium">{label}</label>
        <input ref={ref} id={fid} aria-invalid={!!error} aria-describedby={error ? `${fid}-err` : undefined}
          className="w-full rounded-md border border-gray/60 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-primary" {...p} />
        {error && <p id={`${fid}-err`} role="alert" className="text-xs text-primary">{error}</p>}
      </div>
    );
  });

// AuthLayout.tsx — painel escuro à esquerda (marca) + formulário à direita, como nos mockups.
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
export function AuthLayout({ title, subtitle, children, aside }: { title: string; subtitle?: string; children: ReactNode; aside?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <main className="grid min-h-screen md:grid-cols-2">
      <aside className="hidden flex-col justify-between bg-dark p-10 text-white md:flex">
        <span className="text-2xl font-semibold">aura<span className="text-primary">-fit</span></span>
        {aside ?? <p className="text-3xl font-semibold leading-tight">Mais que treinos.<br />Resultados reais.</p>}
        <span className="text-xs text-gray">© aura-fit</span>
      </aside>
      <section className="flex items-center justify-center p-6">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md space-y-6">
          <header><h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">{title}</h1>{subtitle && <p className="text-sm text-gray">{subtitle}</p>}</header>
          {children}
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { render } from '@testing-library/react';
import type { ReactElement } from 'react';
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AuthProvider } from '../auth/AuthProvider';
import { installZodPt } from '../i18n/zod-pt';

installZodPt();
const Where = () => <div data-testid="where">{useLocation().pathname}</div>;

export function renderRoute(ui: ReactElement, opts: { path: string; initial?: string | { pathname: string; search?: string; state?: unknown } }) {
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } })}>
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[opts.initial ?? opts.path]}>
        <AuthProvider>
          <Routes>
            <Route path={opts.path} element={ui} />
            <Route path="*" element={<Where />} />
          </Routes>
        </AuthProvider>
      </MemoryRouter>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { ForbiddenPage, NotFoundPage, SessionExpiredPage } from './StatusPages';

const err = (code: string, status: number) => HttpResponse.json({ code, message: 'x', correlationId: 'c' }, { status });

describe('RegisterPage', () => {
  const fill = async (over: { terms?: boolean; confirm?: string } = {}) => {

    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('tab', { name: 'Aluno' }));
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Código de convite')).toHaveValue('ABCDEFGH12345678');
    await fill();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Criar conta' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(where()).toBe('/verify-email'));
    expect(body.code).toBe('ABCDEFGH12345678');
  });

  it('shows invalid_invite on the code field', async () => {
    server.use(http.post(`${API}/auth/register-aluno`, () => err('invalid_invite', 400)));
    renderRoute(<RegisterPage />, { path: '/register', initial: '/register?invite=ABCDEFGH12345678' });
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('tab', { name: 'Aluno' }));
    await fill();
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Criar conta' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Convite inválido ou expirado.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe('ForgotPasswordPage', () => {
  it('always shows the same generic confirmation', async () => {
    server.use(http.post(`${API}/auth/forgot-password`, () => new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 })));
    renderRoute(<ForgotPasswordPage />, { path: '/forgot-password' });

describe('VerifyEmailPage', () => {

    let calls = 0;

});

describe('SelectProfilePage', () => {
  it('lists profiles by friendly name and selects one', async () => {
    let selected: any;
    server.use(
      http.post(`${API}/auth/refresh`, () => HttpResponse.json({ accessToken: 'at', expiresIn: 600 }, { status: 201 })),
      http.get(`${API}/auth/memberships`, () => HttpResponse.json({ memberships: [M1, M2], activeMembershipId: null })),
      http.post(`${API}/auth/select-membership`, async ({ request }) => { selected = await request.json(); return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 }); }),

    renderRoute(<SelectProfilePage />, { path: '/select-profile' });
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('radio', { name: /Aluno/ }));
    server.use(http.get(`${API}/auth/memberships`, () => HttpResponse.json({ memberships: [M1, M2], activeMembershipId: M2.id })));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Continuar' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(where()).toBe('/app'));
    expect(selected).toEqual({ membershipId: M2.id });

  it('sends anonymous visitors to /login', async () => {
    renderRoute(<SelectProfilePage />, { path: '/select-profile' });
    await waitFor(() => expect(where()).toBe('/login'));

describe('status pages', () => {
  it('session expired links to login', async () => {
    renderRoute(<SessionExpiredPage />, { path: '/session-expired' });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('link', { name: 'Fazer login' })).toHaveAttribute('href', '/login');

  it('forbidden and 404 render their copy', async () => {
```

`src/i18n/zod-pt.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';

const map: z.ZodErrorMap = (issue, ctx) => {
  switch (issue.code) {
    case 'invalid_string': if (issue.validation === 'email') return { message: 'E-mail inválido.' }; break;
    case 'too_small': if (issue.type === 'string') return { message: `Mínimo de ${issue.minimum} caracteres.` }; break;
    case 'too_big': if (issue.type === 'string') return { message: `Máximo de ${issue.maximum} caracteres.` }; break;
    case 'invalid_literal': return { message: 'Você precisa aceitar os termos.' };
    case 'custom': return { message: issue.message ?? 'Campo inválido.' };
  }
  const known: Record<string, string> = { min_10: 'Mínimo de 10 caracteres.', digits_only: 'Somente números, com DDD.' };
  return { message: known[ctx.defaultError] ?? (ctx.data === undefined ? 'Campo obrigatório.' : 'Campo inválido.') };
};

export const installZodPt = () => z.setErrorMap(map);
```

`LoginPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { loginSchema, type LoginInput } from '@aura/schemas';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ApiError, useAuth } from '../../auth/AuthProvider';
import { AuthLayout } from '../../ui/AuthLayout';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { Field } from '../../ui/Field';

const MESSAGES: Record<string, string> = {
  invalid_credentials: 'E-mail ou senha inválidos.',
  too_many_attempts: 'Muitas tentativas. Tente novamente em alguns minutos.',
};

export function LoginPage() {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const from = (useLocation().state as { from?: string } | null)?.from ?? '/app';
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false);
  const { register, handleSubmit, formState: { errors, isSubmitting } } = useForm<LoginInput>({ resolver: zodResolver(loginSchema) });

  const onSubmit = handleSubmit(async (v) => {
    setError(null);
    try {
      const outcome = await login(v.email, v.password);
      navigate(outcome === 'authenticated' ? from : '/select-profile', { replace: true });
    } catch (e) {
      const code = e instanceof ApiError ? e.code : 'error';
      if (code === 'email_not_verified') return navigate('/verify-email', { state: { email: v.email } });
      setError(MESSAGES[code] ?? 'Não foi possível entrar. Tente novamente.');
    }
  });

  return (
    <AuthLayout title="Login" subtitle="Acesse sua conta para continuar">
      <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
        <Field label="E-mail" type="email" autoComplete="username" error={errors.email?.message} {...register('email')} />
        <div className="relative">
          <Field label="Senha" type={show ? 'text' : 'password'} autoComplete="current-password" error={errors.password?.message} {...register('password')} />
          <button type="button" onClick={() => setShow((s) => !s)} className="absolute right-3 top-8 text-xs text-gray" aria-label={show ? 'Ocultar senha' : 'Mostrar senha'}>
            {show ? 'Ocultar' : 'Mostrar'}
          </button>
        </div>
        {error && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-primary">{error}</p>}
        <Button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting} className="w-full">Entrar</Button>
        <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">
          <Link to="/forgot-password" className="text-primary">Esqueceu a senha?</Link>
          <Link to="/register" className="text-primary">Cadastre-se</Link>
        </div>
      </form>
      {/* Task 19 troca este bloco por <SocialButtons providers={SOCIAL_PROVIDERS} /> */}
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
```

`RegisterPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { register as registerPersonal, registerAluno } from '@aura/contracts';
import { registerAlunoSchema, registerPersonalSchema, TERMS_VERSION } from '@aura/schemas';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { Link, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { AuthLayout } from '../../ui/AuthLayout';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { Field } from '../../ui/Field';

const withConfirm = <T extends z.ZodTypeAny>(s: T) =>
  (s as any).and(z.object({ confirmPassword: z.string() })).refine((v: any) => v.password === v.confirmPassword, {
    path: ['confirmPassword'], message: 'As senhas não conferem.',
  });

type Kind = 'personal' | 'aluno';

export function RegisterPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const invite = params.get('invite') ?? '';
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<Kind>(invite ? 'aluno' : 'personal');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const schema = withConfirm(kind === 'aluno' ? registerAlunoSchema : registerPersonalSchema);
  const { register, handleSubmit, setError: setFieldError, formState: { errors, isSubmitting } } = useForm<any>({
    resolver: zodResolver(schema), defaultValues: { code: invite, termsVersion: TERMS_VERSION },
  });

  const onSubmit = handleSubmit(async (v) => {
    setError(null);
    const { confirmPassword: _omit, ...body } = v;
    try {
      if (kind === 'aluno') await registerAluno(body); else await registerPersonal(body);
      navigate('/verify-email', { state: { email: v.email } });
    } catch (e: any) {
      const data = e?.response?.data;
      if (data?.code === 'invalid_invite') return setFieldError('code', { message: 'Convite inválido ou expirado.' });
      if (data?.code === 'validation_error') {
        for (const f of data.fields ?? []) setFieldError(f.path, { message: 'Valor inválido.' });
        return;
      }
      setError('Não foi possível criar a conta. Tente novamente.');
    }
  });

  return (
    <AuthLayout title="Cadastro" subtitle="Preencha os dados para criar sua conta">
      <div role="tablist" className="grid grid-cols-2 rounded-md bg-white p-1 text-sm">
        {(['personal', 'aluno'] as const).map((k) => (
          <button key={k} role="tab" type="button" aria-selected={kind === k} onClick={() => setKind(k)}
            className={`rounded px-3 py-2 ${kind === k ? 'bg-primary text-white' : ''}`}>
            {k === 'personal' ? 'Personal Trainer' : 'Aluno'}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-3" noValidate>
        {kind === 'aluno' && <Field label="Código de convite" error={errors.code?.message as string} {...register('code')} />}
        <Field label="Nome completo" autoComplete="name" error={errors.name?.message as string} {...register('name')} />
        <Field label="E-mail" type="email" autoComplete="email" error={errors.email?.message as string} {...register('email')} />
        <Field label="Telefone (com DDD)" inputMode="numeric" error={errors.phone?.message as string} {...register('phone', { setValueAs: (v) => v || undefined })} />
        <Field label="Senha" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" error={errors.password?.message as string} {...register('password')} />
        <Field label="Confirmar senha" type="password" autoComplete="new-password" error={errors.confirmPassword?.message as string} {...register('confirmPassword')} />
        <label className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm">
          <input type="checkbox" {...register('acceptTerms')} />
          <span>Li e aceito os <Link to="/terms" className="text-primary">Termos de Uso e a Política de Privacidade</Link></span>
        </label>
        {errors.acceptTerms && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-primary">{errors.acceptTerms.message as string}</p>}
        {error && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-primary">{error}</p>}
        <Button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting} className="w-full">Criar conta</Button>
        <p className="text-center text-sm">Já tem uma conta? <Link to="/login" className="text-primary">Faça login</Link></p>
      </form>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
```

`ForgotPasswordPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { forgotPassword } from '@aura/contracts';
import { forgotPasswordSchema } from '@aura/schemas';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AuthLayout } from '../../ui/AuthLayout';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { Field } from '../../ui/Field';

export function ForgotPasswordPage() {
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const { register, handleSubmit, formState: { errors, isSubmitting } } = useForm<{ email: string }>({ resolver: zodResolver(forgotPasswordSchema) });

  const onSubmit = handleSubmit(async (v) => {
    setError(null);
    try { await forgotPassword(v); setSent(true); } catch { setError('Não foi possível enviar agora. Tente novamente.'); }
  });

  if (sent) {
    return (
      <AuthLayout title="Link enviado!">
        <p className="text-sm">Se o e-mail estiver cadastrado, você receberá um link para redefinir a senha. O link expira em 15 minutos.</p>
        <Link to="/login" className="text-primary text-sm">Voltar para o login</Link>
      </AuthLayout>
    );
  }
  return (
    <AuthLayout title="Esqueceu sua senha?" subtitle="Informe seu e-mail e enviaremos um link para redefinir sua senha.">
      <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4" noValidate>
        <Field label="E-mail" type="email" autoComplete="email" error={errors.email?.message} {...register('email')} />
        {error && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-primary">{error}</p>}
        <Button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting} className="w-full">Enviar link</Button>
        <Link to="/login" className="text-sm text-primary">Voltar para o login</Link>
      </form>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
```

`VerifyEmailPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { resendVerification } from '@aura/contracts';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { AuthLayout } from '../../ui/AuthLayout';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';

const COOLDOWN = 60;

export function VerifyEmailPage() {
  const email = (useLocation().state as { email?: string } | null)?.email;
  const [left, setLeft] = useState(0);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (left <= 0) return;
    const t = setTimeout(() => setLeft((s) => s - 1), 1000);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [left]);

  const resend = async () => {
    if (!email) return;
    setError(null);
    try { await resendVerification({ email }); setLeft(COOLDOWN); } catch { setError('Não foi possível reenviar agora.'); }
  };

  return (
    <AuthLayout title="Verifique seu e-mail" subtitle="Enviamos um link de verificação para o seu endereço de e-mail. Clique no link para ativar sua conta.">
      {email && <p className="text-sm text-gray">{email}</p>}
      {error && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-primary">{error}</p>}
      <Button onClick={resend} disabled={!email || left > 0} className="w-full">
        {left > 0 ? `Reenviar e-mail (${left}s)` : 'Reenviar e-mail'}
      </Button>
      <Link to="/login" className="text-sm text-primary">Voltar para o login</Link>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
```

`SelectProfilePage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Navigate, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Spinner } from '../../auth/guards';
import { useAuth } from '../../auth/AuthProvider';
import { AuthLayout } from '../../ui/AuthLayout';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';

const LABEL: Record<string, { name: string; hint: string }> = {
  personal: { name: 'Personal Trainer', hint: 'Gerencie seus alunos, treinos e finanças.' },
  aluno: { name: 'Aluno', hint: 'Acompanhe seus treinos, progresso e informações.' },
  admin: { name: 'Administrador', hint: 'Gerencie a plataforma, usuários e configurações.' },
};

export function SelectProfilePage() {
  const { status, memberships, selectProfile } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [chosen, setChosen] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (status === 'loading') return <Spinner />;
  if (status === 'anonymous' || status === 'expired') return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;

  const go = async () => {
    if (!chosen) return;
    setError(null);
    try { await selectProfile(chosen); navigate('/app', { replace: true }); } catch { setError('Não foi possível selecionar o perfil.'); }
  };

  return (
    <AuthLayout title="Selecione seu perfil" subtitle="Você possui mais de um perfil de acesso. Escolha com qual deseja continuar.">
      <div role="radiogroup" aria-label="Perfis" className="grid gap-3">
        {memberships.map((m) => {
          const l = LABEL[m.role] ?? { name: m.role, hint: '' };
          return (
            <button key={m.id} type="button" role="radio" aria-checked={chosen === m.id} onClick={() => setChosen(m.id)}
              className={`rounded-md border p-4 text-left ${chosen === m.id ? 'border-primary bg-primary/5' : 'border-gray/50'}`}>
              <div className="font-semibold">{l.name}</div>
              {m.tenantName && <div className="text-xs text-gray">{m.tenantName}</div>}
              <div className="text-xs">{l.hint}</div>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
      {error && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-primary">{error}</p>}
      <Button onClick={go} disabled={!chosen} className="w-full">Continuar</Button>
    </AuthLayout>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';

const linkCls = 'inline-block rounded-md bg-primary px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white';
```

`App.tsx` (rotas públicas e protegidas; o shell entra na Task 16):

```tsx
import { Navigate, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { RequireAuth } from './auth/guards';
import { ForgotPasswordPage } from './pages/auth/ForgotPasswordPage';
import { LoginPage } from './pages/auth/LoginPage';
import { RegisterPage } from './pages/auth/RegisterPage';
import { SelectProfilePage } from './pages/auth/SelectProfilePage';
import { ForbiddenPage, NotFoundPage, SessionExpiredPage } from './pages/auth/StatusPages';
import { VerifyEmailPage } from './pages/auth/VerifyEmailPage';

export function App() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/" element={<Navigate to="/app" replace />} />
      <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />
      <Route path="/register" element={<RegisterPage />} />
      <Route path="/forgot-password" element={<ForgotPasswordPage />} />
      <Route path="/verify-email" element={<VerifyEmailPage />} />
      <Route path="/select-profile" element={<SelectProfilePage />} />
      <Route path="/session-expired" element={<SessionExpiredPage />} />
      <Route path="/forbidden" element={<ForbiddenPage />} />
      <Route path="/app/*" element={<RequireAuth><div>aura-fit</div></RequireAuth>} />
      <Route path="*" element={<NotFoundPage />} />
    </Routes>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { API, ME, server } from '../../test/server';
import { renderRoute } from '../../test/render';
import { App } from '../../App';

const restore = (me = ME) => server.use(
  http.post(`${API}/auth/refresh`, () => HttpResponse.json({ accessToken: 'at', expiresIn: 600 }, { status: 201 })),
  http.get(`${API}/me`, () => HttpResponse.json(me)),
);

describe('AppShell menu', () => {
  it('personal sees Convites but not Permissões', async () => {
    restore();
    renderRoute(<App />, { path: '/*', initial: '/app' });
    const nav = await screen.findByRole('navigation');
    expect(within(nav).getByRole('link', { name: 'Convites' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(nav).queryByRole('link', { name: 'Permissões' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('admin sees Permissões and not Convites', async () => {
    restore({ ...ME, role: 'admin', permissions: ['profile:read', 'roles:read', 'users:read'] } as any);
    renderRoute(<App />, { path: '/*', initial: '/app' });
    const nav = await screen.findByRole('navigation');
    expect(within(nav).getByRole('link', { name: 'Permissões' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(nav).queryByRole('link', { name: 'Convites' })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('typing an admin URL without permission lands on Acesso negado', async () => {
    restore();
    renderRoute(<App />, { path: '/*', initial: '/app/admin/permissions' });
    expect(await screen.findByText('Acesso negado')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('logout returns to /login', async () => {
    restore();
    renderRoute(<App />, { path: '/*', initial: '/app' });
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Sair' }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Entrar' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe('ProfilePage', () => {
  it('saves name and phone with PATCH /me', async () => {
    restore();
    let body: any;
    server.use(http.patch(`${API}/me`, async ({ request }) => {
      body = await request.json();
      return HttpResponse.json({ id: ME.id, email: ME.email, name: body.name, phone: body.phone });
    }));
    renderRoute(<App />, { path: '/*', initial: '/app/profile' });
    const name = await screen.findByLabelText('Nome completo');
    await userEvent.clear(name); await userEvent.type(name, 'Rafael Souza');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Telefone (com DDD)'), '81999999999');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Salvar alterações' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(body).toEqual({ name: 'Rafael Souza', phone: '81999999999' }));
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('E-mail')).toBeDisabled();
  });
});

describe('InvitesPage', () => {
  it('generates an invite and shows a shareable register link', async () => {
    restore();
    server.use(http.post(`${API}/invites`, () => HttpResponse.json({ code: 'CODE1234567890AB', expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 1e8).toISOString() }, { status: 201 })));
    renderRoute(<App />, { path: '/*', initial: '/app/invites' });
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Gerar convite' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/\/register\?invite=CODE1234567890AB/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe('TermsPage', () => {
  it('is public and shows the version', async () => {
    renderRoute(<App />, { path: '/*', initial: '/terms' });
    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Termos de Uso' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/12 de abril de 2025/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`permissions.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { App } from '../../App';
import { API, ME, server } from '../../test/server';
import { renderRoute } from '../../test/render';

const ADMIN = { ...ME, role: 'admin', tenantId: null, permissions: ['profile:read', 'roles:read', 'roles:manage', 'users:read', 'users:manage'] };
const CATALOG = ['profile:read', 'profile:update', 'invites:create', 'roles:manage', 'users:manage'];
const ROLES = [
  { id: 1, name: 'aluno', users: 10, permissions: ['profile:read'] },
  { id: 2, name: 'personal', users: 3, permissions: ['profile:read', 'invites:create'] },
  { id: 3, name: 'admin', users: 1, permissions: ['roles:manage', 'users:manage'] },
];

function setup() {
  server.use(
    http.post(`${API}/auth/refresh`, () => HttpResponse.json({ accessToken: 'at', expiresIn: 600 }, { status: 201 })),
    http.get(`${API}/me`, () => HttpResponse.json(ADMIN)),
    http.get(`${API}/roles`, () => HttpResponse.json(ROLES)),
    http.get(`${API}/permissions`, () => HttpResponse.json(CATALOG)),
  );
  renderRoute(<App />, { path: '/*', initial: '/app/admin/permissions' });
}

describe('Permissões — Funções', () => {
  it('lists roles and marks granted permissions', async () => {
    setup();
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /personal/i }));
    expect(await screen.findByRole('checkbox', { name: 'invites:create' })).toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.getByRole('checkbox', { name: 'roles:manage' })).not.toBeChecked();
  });

  it('saves the edited set with PUT /roles/:id/permissions', async () => {
    let body: any; let url = '';
    server.use(http.put(`${API}/roles/:id/permissions`, async ({ request }) => {
      url = new URL(request.url).pathname; body = await request.json(); return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 });
    }));
    setup();
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /personal/i }));
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('checkbox', { name: 'profile:update' }));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Salvar permissões' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(body).toBeDefined());
    expect(url).toBe('/roles/2/permissions');
    expect([...body.permissions].sort()).toEqual(['invites:create', 'profile:read', 'profile:update']);
  });

  it('shows the friendly message for protected permissions', async () => {
    server.use(http.put(`${API}/roles/:id/permissions`, () =>
      HttpResponse.json({ code: 'protected_permission', message: 'x', correlationId: 'c' }, { status: 400 })));
    setup();
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /admin/i }));
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('checkbox', { name: 'roles:manage' }));
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Salvar permissões' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Esta permissão é obrigatória para o Administrador.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('has no "+ Nova função" (custom roles are out of scope)', async () => {
    setup();
    await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /personal/i });
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /Nova função/ })).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe('Permissões — Usuários', () => {
  const USERS = { total: 1, page: 1, pageSize: 50, items: [{
    id: '66666666-6666-4666-8666-666666666666', email: 'lucas@example.com', name: 'Lucas Ferreira', status: 'ACTIVE',
    emailVerified: true, createdAt: '2025-04-01T00:00:00Z', memberships: [{ id: 'm', status: 'ACTIVE', role: { name: 'aluno' } }],
  }] };

  it('suspends a user with PATCH /users/:id/status', async () => {
    let body: any;
    server.use(
      http.get(`${API}/users`, () => HttpResponse.json(USERS)),
      http.patch(`${API}/users/:id/status`, async ({ request }) => { body = await request.json(); return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 }); }),
    );
    setup();
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('tab', { name: 'Usuários' }));
    const row = (await screen.findByText('lucas@example.com')).closest('tr')!;
    await userEvent.click(within(row).getByRole('button', { name: 'Suspender' }));
    await waitFor(() => expect(body).toEqual({ status: 'SUSPENDED' }));
  });

  it('shows cannot_suspend_self in Portuguese', async () => {
    server.use(
      http.get(`${API}/users`, () => HttpResponse.json(USERS)),
      http.patch(`${API}/users/:id/status`, () => HttpResponse.json({ code: 'cannot_suspend_self', message: 'x', correlationId: 'c' }, { status: 400 })),
    );
    setup();
    await userEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('tab', { name: 'Usuários' }));
    const row = (await screen.findByText('lucas@example.com')).closest('tr')!;
    await userEvent.click(within(row).getByRole('button', { name: 'Suspender' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Você não pode suspender a si mesmo.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import type { Permission } from '@aura/authz';
import { NavLink, Outlet, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../auth/AuthProvider';
import { Button } from '../ui/Button';

const ITEMS: { to: string; label: string; perm?: Permission }[] = [
  { to: '/app', label: 'Início' },
  { to: '/app/profile', label: 'Meu perfil', perm: 'profile:read' },
  { to: '/app/invites', label: 'Convites', perm: 'invites:create' },
  { to: '/app/admin/permissions', label: 'Permissões', perm: 'roles:read' },
];

export function AppShell() {
  const { me, can, logout, memberships } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  return (
    <div className="grid min-h-screen grid-cols-[240px_1fr]">
      <aside className="flex flex-col bg-dark p-4 text-white">
        <span className="mb-6 text-xl font-semibold">aura<span className="text-primary">-fit</span></span>
        <nav aria-label="Principal" className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-1">
          {ITEMS.filter((i) => !i.perm || can(i.perm)).map((i) => (
            <NavLink key={i.to} to={i.to} end={i.to === '/app'}
              className={({ isActive }) => `rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm ${isActive ? 'bg-primary' : 'hover:bg-white/10'}`}>{i.label}</NavLink>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <div className="space-y-2 border-t border-white/10 pt-3 text-xs">
          <div>{me?.name}</div>
          {memberships.length > 1 && <Button variant="text" onClick={() => navigate('/select-profile')}>Trocar perfil</Button>}
          <Button variant="secondary" onClick={async () => { await logout(); navigate('/login'); }}>Sair</Button>
        </div>
      </aside>
      <main className="p-8"><Outlet /></main>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useUpdateMe } from '@aura/contracts';
import { updateMeSchema } from '@aura/schemas';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { useAuth } from '../../auth/AuthProvider';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { Field } from '../../ui/Field';

export function ProfilePage() {
  const { me } = useAuth();
  const update = useUpdateMe();
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const { register, handleSubmit, formState: { errors } } = useForm<{ name: string; phone?: string }>({
    resolver: zodResolver(updateMeSchema), defaultValues: { name: me?.name, phone: me?.phone ?? '' },
  });
  const onSubmit = handleSubmit(async (v) => {
    setMsg(null);
    await update.mutateAsync({ data: { name: v.name, phone: v.phone || null } as any });
    setMsg('Alterações salvas.');
  });
  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="max-w-md space-y-4" noValidate>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">Meu Perfil</h1>
      <Field label="Nome completo" error={errors.name?.message} {...register('name')} />
      <Field label="E-mail" value={me?.email ?? ''} disabled readOnly />
      <Field label="Telefone (com DDD)" inputMode="numeric" error={errors.phone?.message} {...register('phone', { setValueAs: (v) => v || undefined })} />
      {msg && <p role="status" className="text-sm">{msg}</p>}
      {update.isError && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-primary">Não foi possível salvar.</p>}
      <Button type="submit" disabled={update.isPending}>Salvar alterações</Button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { RequirePermission } from '../../auth/guards';
import { useAuth } from '../../auth/AuthProvider';

import { UsersTab } from './UsersTab';

export function PermissionsPage() {

        {can('users:read') && <button role="tab" aria-selected={tab === 'users'} onClick={() => setTab('users')} className="px-3 py-2 text-sm">Usuários</button>}
      </div>
      {tab === 'roles' ? <RolesTab canManage={can('roles:manage')} /> : (
```

`RolesTab.tsx`:

```tsx
import { getListRolesQueryKey, useListPermissions, useListRoles, useSetRolePermissions } from '@aura/contracts';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';

const ERRORS: Record<string, string> = {
  protected_permission: 'Esta permissão é obrigatória para o Administrador.',
  unknown_permission: 'Permissão desconhecida.',
};

export function RolesTab({ canManage }: { canManage: boolean }) {
  const roles = useListRoles();
  const catalog = useListPermissions();
  const save = useSetRolePermissions();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const role = roles.data?.find((r) => r.id === selected);

  useEffect(() => { setDraft(new Set(role?.permissions ?? [])); setError(null); }, [role?.id, role?.permissions.join(',')]);

  const toggle = (p: string) => setDraft((d) => { const n = new Set(d); n.has(p) ? n.delete(p) : n.add(p); return n; });
  const onSave = async () => {
    if (!role) return;
    setError(null);
    try {
      await save.mutateAsync({ id: role.id, data: { permissions: [...draft] } });
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: getListRolesQueryKey() });
    } catch (e: any) {
      setError(ERRORS[e?.response?.data?.code] ?? 'Não foi possível salvar.');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-[260px_1fr]">
      <ul className="space-y-2">
        {roles.data?.map((r) => (
          <li key={r.id}>
            <button onClick={() => setSelected(r.id)} className={`w-full rounded-md border p-3 text-left ${selected === r.id ? 'border-primary' : 'border-gray/40'}`}>
              <span className="font-semibold capitalize">{r.name}</span> <span className="text-xs text-gray">{r.users} usuários</span>
            </button>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {role && (
        <section className="space-y-3">
          <h2 className="font-semibold capitalize">{role.name}</h2>
          <div className="grid gap-1">
            {catalog.data?.map((p) => (
              <label key={p} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                <input type="checkbox" aria-label={p} checked={draft.has(p)} disabled={!canManage} onChange={() => toggle(p)} /> {p}
              </label>
            ))}
          </div>
          {error && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-primary">{error}</p>}
          {canManage && <Button onClick={onSave} disabled={save.isPending}>Salvar permissões</Button>}
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`UsersTab.tsx`:

```tsx
import { getListUsersQueryKey, useListUsers, useSetUserStatus } from '@aura/contracts';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';

const ERRORS: Record<string, string> = { cannot_suspend_self: 'Você não pode suspender a si mesmo.' };

export function UsersTab({ canManage }: { canManage: boolean }) {
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const users = useListUsers({ page, pageSize: 50 });
  const setStatus = useSetUserStatus();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const change = async (id: string, status: 'ACTIVE' | 'SUSPENDED') => {
    setError(null);
    try {
      await setStatus.mutateAsync({ id, data: { status } });
      await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: getListUsersQueryKey() });
    } catch (e: any) { setError(ERRORS[e?.response?.data?.code] ?? 'Não foi possível alterar o status.'); }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {error && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-primary">{error}</p>}
      <table className="w-full text-sm">
        <thead><tr className="text-left text-gray"><th>Usuário</th><th>Papéis</th><th>Status</th><th /></tr></thead>
        <tbody>
          {users.data?.items.map((u) => (
            <tr key={u.id} className="border-t border-gray/20">
              <td className="py-2"><div>{u.name}</div><div className="text-xs text-gray">{u.email}</div></td>
              <td>{u.memberships.map((m) => m.role.name).join(', ')}</td>
              <td>{u.status}</td>
              <td className="text-right">
                {canManage && (u.status === 'SUSPENDED'
                  ? <Button variant="text" onClick={() => change(u.id, 'ACTIVE')}>Reativar</Button>
                  : <Button variant="text" onClick={() => change(u.id, 'SUSPENDED')}>Suspender</Button>)}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <Button variant="secondary" disabled={page <= 1} onClick={() => setPage((p) => p - 1)}>Anterior</Button>
        <Button variant="secondary" disabled={(users.data?.page ?? 1) * 50 >= (users.data?.total ?? 0)} onClick={() => setPage((p) => p + 1)}>Próxima</Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 17: Mobile (Expo) — autenticação do aluno com token no SecureStore

**Decisões:** Expo SDK 52 + Expo Router, estilos com `StyleSheet` e tokens do design system (NativeWind do `product.md` fica para quando houver telas de treino). A lógica de sessão é análoga à do web mas com refresh token em `expo-secure-store` (não há cookies no mobile). A duplicação com `apps/web/src/auth/AuthProvider.tsx` é aceita nesta entrega; extrair um `@aura/auth-core` fica como refatoração futura.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/mobile/package.json`, `app.json`, `babel.config.js`, `metro.config.js`, `tsconfig.json`, `jest.config.js`, `jest.setup.ts`
- Create: `apps/mobile/src/theme.ts`, `src/ui/{Screen,TextField,PrimaryButton}.tsx`
- Create: `apps/mobile/src/auth/secure-token.ts`, `src/auth/AuthProvider.tsx`
- Create: `apps/mobile/app/_layout.tsx`, `app/(auth)/{login,register,forgot-password,verify-email,select-profile,session-expired}.tsx`, `app/(app)/index.tsx`
- Create: `docker-compose.lan.yml`
- Modify: `docker-compose.yml` (serviço `mobile`, profile `mobile`), `.env.example` (`LAN_IP`, `10.0.2.2` em `ALLOWED_HOSTS`)
- Test: `apps/mobile/src/auth/auth.test.tsx`, `apps/mobile/src/screens.test.tsx`, `apps/mobile/src/test/server.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `configureApi` (`platform: 'mobile'`), `login`, `refresh`, `logout`, `getMemberships`, `selectMembership`, `getMe`, `registerAluno`, `forgotPassword`, `resendVerification` de `@aura/contracts`.
- Produces: `tokenStore { get(): Promise<string | null>; set(v: string): Promise<void>; clear(): Promise<void> }`; `AuthProvider`/`useAuth()` com a mesma forma de `AuthApi` do web (`status`, `me`, `memberships`, `login`, `selectProfile`, `logout`, `can`).
- Regras: access token só em memória; refresh token só no SecureStore (`WHEN_UNLOCKED_THIS_DEVICE_ONLY`); cadastro no mobile é **só aluno com convite** (deep link `aurafit://register?invite=CODE`); nenhum token em `AsyncStorage`, logs ou URLs.

- [ ] **Step 1: Escrever os testes que falham**

`src/test/server.ts` (igual ao do web: `API = 'http://api.test'`, `ME`, `M1`, `M2`, handlers e `setupServer`, com `refresh` respondendo 401 por padrão).

`jest.setup.ts`:

```ts
import { server } from './src/test/server';

const store = new Map<string, string>();
jest.mock('expo-secure-store', () => ({
  WHEN_UNLOCKED_THIS_DEVICE_ONLY: 1,
  getItemAsync: jest.fn(async (k: string) => store.get(k) ?? null),
  setItemAsync: jest.fn(async (k: string, v: string) => { store.set(k, v); }),
  deleteItemAsync: jest.fn(async (k: string) => { store.delete(k); }),
}));
(globalThis as any).__secureStore = store;

const mockReplace = jest.fn(); const mockPush = jest.fn();
let mockParams: Record<string, string> = {};
(globalThis as any).__router = { replace: mockReplace, push: mockPush, setParams: (p: Record<string, string>) => { mockParams = p; } };
jest.mock('expo-router', () => ({
  useRouter: () => ({ replace: mockReplace, push: mockPush }),
  useLocalSearchParams: () => mockParams,
  Link: ({ children }: any) => children,
  Stack: () => null,
}));

beforeAll(() => server.listen({ onUnhandledRequest: 'error' }));
afterEach(() => { server.resetHandlers(); store.clear(); mockReplace.mockClear(); mockPush.mockClear(); mockParams = {}; });
afterAll(() => server.close());
```

`src/auth/auth.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { Pressable, Text } from 'react-native';
import { API, M1, ME, server } from '../test/server';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from './AuthProvider';

const store = (globalThis as any).__secureStore as Map<string, string>;
const router = (globalThis as any).__router;

function Probe() {
  const a = useAuth();
  return (
    <>
      <Text testID="status">{a.status}</Text>
      <Pressable testID="login" onPress={() => a.login('a@example.com', 'Sup3r-secret-pw').catch(() => undefined)}><Text>login</Text></Pressable>
      <Pressable testID="logout" onPress={() => a.logout()}><Text>logout</Text></Pressable>
    </>
  );
}
const mount = () => render(<AuthProvider><Probe /></AuthProvider>);
const status = () => screen.getByTestId('status').props.children;

describe('mobile session', () => {
  it('boots anonymous when there is no stored refresh token', async () => {
    mount();
    await waitFor(() => expect(status()).toBe('anonymous'));
  });

  it('restores from SecureStore and stores the rotated refresh token', async () => {
    store.set('aura_refresh_token', 'stored-rt-123456');
    let body: any;
    server.use(http.post(`${API}/auth/refresh`, async ({ request }) => {
      body = await request.json();
      return HttpResponse.json({ accessToken: 'at-2', expiresIn: 600, refreshToken: 'rt-2-rotated-xyz' }, { status: 201 });
    }));
    mount();
    await waitFor(() => expect(status()).toBe('authenticated'));
    expect(body).toEqual({ refreshToken: 'stored-rt-123456' });
    expect(store.get('aura_refresh_token')).toBe('rt-2-rotated-xyz');
  });

  it('login sends x-client: mobile and persists only the refresh token', async () => {
    let client: string | null = null;
    server.use(http.post(`${API}/auth/login`, ({ request }) => {
      client = request.headers.get('x-client');
      return HttpResponse.json({ accessToken: 'at-1', expiresIn: 600, refreshToken: 'rt-1-abcdefghij', memberships: [M1], activeMembershipId: M1.id }, { status: 201 });
    }));
    mount();
    await waitFor(() => expect(status()).toBe('anonymous'));
    screen.getByTestId('login').props.onPress();
    await waitFor(() => expect(status()).toBe('authenticated'));
    expect(client).toBe('mobile');
    expect([...store.keys()]).toEqual(['aura_refresh_token']);
    expect([...store.values()].join()).not.toContain('at-1');
  });

  it('clears the store and goes to /session-expired when refresh fails', async () => {
    store.set('aura_refresh_token', 'stale-rt-1234567');
    mount(); // refresh padrão = 401
    await waitFor(() => expect(status()).toBe('anonymous'));
    expect(store.has('aura_refresh_token')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('logout sends the refresh token and clears SecureStore', async () => {
    store.set('aura_refresh_token', 'stored-rt-123456');
    let body: any;
    server.use(
      http.post(`${API}/auth/refresh`, () => HttpResponse.json({ accessToken: 'at', expiresIn: 600, refreshToken: 'rt-new-1234567' }, { status: 201 })),
      http.post(`${API}/auth/logout`, async ({ request }) => { body = await request.json(); return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 }); }),
    );
    mount();
    await waitFor(() => expect(status()).toBe('authenticated'));
    screen.getByTestId('logout').props.onPress();
    await waitFor(() => expect(status()).toBe('anonymous'));
    expect(body).toEqual({ refreshToken: 'rt-new-1234567' });
    expect(store.size).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

`src/screens.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import LoginScreen from '../app/(auth)/login';
import RegisterScreen from '../app/(auth)/register';
import SelectProfileScreen from '../app/(auth)/select-profile';
import { AuthProvider } from './auth/AuthProvider';
import { API, M1, M2, server } from './test/server';

const router = (globalThis as any).__router;
const wrap = (ui: React.ReactElement) => render(<AuthProvider>{ui}</AuthProvider>);
const err = (code: string, status: number) => HttpResponse.json({ code, message: 'x', correlationId: 'c' }, { status });

describe('LoginScreen', () => {
  it('shows one generic error for wrong credentials', async () => {
    server.use(http.post(`${API}/auth/login`, () => err('invalid_credentials', 401)));
    wrap(<LoginScreen />);
    fireEvent.changeText(await screen.findByPlaceholderText('E-mail'), 'x@example.com');
    fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Senha'), 'whatever-pw');
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByText('Entrar'));
    expect(await screen.findByText('E-mail ou senha inválidos.')).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('goes to /select-profile when there are several profiles', async () => {
    server.use(http.post(`${API}/auth/login`, () => HttpResponse.json(
      { accessToken: 'at', expiresIn: 600, refreshToken: 'rt-abcdefghij1', memberships: [M1, M2], activeMembershipId: null }, { status: 201 })));
    wrap(<LoginScreen />);
    fireEvent.changeText(await screen.findByPlaceholderText('E-mail'), 'x@example.com');
    fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Senha'), 'whatever-pw');
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByText('Entrar'));
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.replace).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/select-profile'));
  });

  it('goes to /verify-email when the e-mail is not verified', async () => {
    server.use(http.post(`${API}/auth/login`, () => err('email_not_verified', 403)));
    wrap(<LoginScreen />);
    fireEvent.changeText(await screen.findByPlaceholderText('E-mail'), 'x@example.com');
    fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Senha'), 'whatever-pw');
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByText('Entrar'));
    await waitFor(() => expect(router.push).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ pathname: '/verify-email', params: { email: 'x@example.com' } }));
  });
});

describe('RegisterScreen (aluno com convite)', () => {
  it('prefills the code from the deep link and posts to register-aluno', async () => {
    router.setParams({ invite: 'ABCDEFGH12345678' });
    let body: any;
    server.use(http.post(`${API}/auth/register-aluno`, async ({ request }) => {
      body = await request.json(); return HttpResponse.json({ status: 'pending_verification' }, { status: 201 });
    }));
    wrap(<RegisterScreen />);
    expect((await screen.findByPlaceholderText('Código de convite')).props.value).toBe('ABCDEFGH12345678');
    fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Nome completo'), 'Lucas Ferreira');
    fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByPlaceholderText('E-mail'), 'lucas@example.com');
    fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Senha'), 'Sup3r-secret-pw');
    fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByPlaceholderText('Confirmar senha'), 'Sup3r-secret-pw');
    fireEvent(screen.getByLabelText('Li e aceito os Termos de Uso'), 'valueChange', true);
    fireEvent.press(screen.getByText('Criar conta'));
    await waitFor(() => expect(body).toMatchObject({ code: 'ABCDEFGH12345678', email: 'lucas@example.com', acceptTerms: true, termsVersion: '2025-04-12' }));
    expect(router.replace).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ pathname: '/verify-email', params: { email: 'lucas@example.com' } });
  });

  it('has no personal/admin sign-up option', async () => {
    wrap(<RegisterScreen />);
    await screen.findByPlaceholderText('Código de convite');
    expect(screen.queryByText(/Personal Trainer/)).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/Admin/i)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('SelectProfileScreen', () => {
  it('selects a profile and continues', async () => {
    let selected: any;
    server.use(
      http.post(`${API}/auth/login`, () => HttpResponse.json({ accessToken: 'at', expiresIn: 600, refreshToken: 'rt-abcdefghij1', memberships: [M1, M2], activeMembershipId: null }, { status: 201 })),
      http.post(`${API}/auth/select-membership`, async ({ request }) => { selected = await request.json(); return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 }); }),
    );
    // autentica primeiro para popular as memberships no provider
    const login = wrap(<><LoginScreen /><SelectProfileScreen /></>);
    fireEvent.changeText(await login.findByPlaceholderText('E-mail'), 'x@example.com');
    fireEvent.changeText(login.getByPlaceholderText('Senha'), 'whatever-pw');
    fireEvent.press(login.getByText('Entrar'));
    fireEvent.press(await login.findByText('Aluno'));
    server.use(http.get(`${API}/auth/memberships`, () => HttpResponse.json({ memberships: [M1, M2], activeMembershipId: M2.id })));
    fireEvent.press(login.getByText('Continuar'));
    await waitFor(() => expect(selected).toEqual({ membershipId: M2.id }));
  });
});
```
