```tsx
// PrimaryButton.tsx
import { Pressable, StyleSheet, Text } from 'react-native';
import { theme } from '../theme';
export function PrimaryButton({ title, onPress, disabled, variant = 'primary' }: { title: string; onPress: () => void; disabled?: boolean; variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' }) {
  return (
    <Pressable accessibilityRole="button" onPress={onPress} disabled={disabled}
      style={[s.btn, variant === 'secondary' && s.secondary, disabled && { opacity: 0.5 }]}>
      <Text style={[s.txt, variant === 'secondary' && { color: theme.primary }]}>{title}</Text>
    </Pressable>
  );
}
const s = StyleSheet.create({
  btn: { backgroundColor: theme.primary, borderRadius: theme.radius, padding: 14, alignItems: 'center' },
  secondary: { backgroundColor: 'transparent', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: theme.primary },
  txt: { color: '#fff', fontWeight: '600' },
});

// TextField.tsx
import { StyleSheet, Text, TextInput, type TextInputProps, View } from 'react-native';
import { theme } from '../theme';
export function TextField({ error, ...p }: TextInputProps & { error?: string }) {
  return (
    <View style={{ gap: 4 }}>
      <TextInput placeholderTextColor={theme.gray} autoCapitalize="none" {...p} style={[s.input, error && { borderColor: theme.primary }]} accessibilityLabel={p.placeholder} />
      {error && <Text accessibilityRole="alert" style={{ color: theme.primary, fontSize: 12 }}>{error}</Text>}
    </View>
  );
}
const s = StyleSheet.create({ input: { borderWidth: 1, borderColor: theme.gray, borderRadius: theme.radius, padding: 12, backgroundColor: '#fff' } });

// Screen.tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { ScrollView, StyleSheet, Text } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { theme } from '../theme';
export function Screen({ title, subtitle, children }: { title: string; subtitle?: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.light }}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={s.body} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
        <Text style={s.brand}>aura<Text style={{ color: theme.primary }}>-fit</Text></Text>
        <Text style={s.title}>{title}</Text>
        {subtitle && <Text style={{ color: theme.gray }}>{subtitle}</Text>}
        {children}
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}
const s = StyleSheet.create({ body: { padding: 24, gap: 14 }, brand: { fontSize: 26, fontWeight: '700', color: theme.dark }, title: { fontSize: 22, fontWeight: '600' } });
```

```ts
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';

const KEY = 'aura_refresh_token';
export const tokenStore = {
  get: () => SecureStore.getItemAsync(KEY),
  set: (v: string) => SecureStore.setItemAsync(KEY, v, { keychainAccessible: SecureStore.WHEN_UNLOCKED_THIS_DEVICE_ONLY }),
  clear: () => SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(KEY),
};
```

`src/auth/AuthProvider.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { Permission } from '@aura/authz';
import { configureApi, getMe, getMemberships, login as apiLogin, logout as apiLogout, refresh as apiRefresh, selectMembership } from '@aura/contracts';
import type { Me, Membership } from '@aura/schemas';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { tokenStore } from './secure-token';

export type AuthStatus = 'loading' | 'anonymous' | 'needs_profile' | 'authenticated' | 'expired';
export type LoginOutcome = 'authenticated' | 'needs_profile';
export class ApiError extends Error { constructor(public code: string, public status: number) { super(code); } }

export interface AuthApi {
  status: AuthStatus; me: Me | null; memberships: Membership[];
  login(email: string, password: string): Promise<LoginOutcome>;
  selectProfile(membershipId: string): Promise<void>;
  logout(): Promise<void>;
  can(permission: Permission): boolean;
}

const Ctx = createContext<AuthApi | null>(null);
export const useAuth = (): AuthApi => { const v = useContext(Ctx); if (!v) throw new Error('useAuth outside AuthProvider'); return v; };
const toApiError = (e: any) => new ApiError(e?.response?.data?.code ?? 'error', e?.response?.status ?? 0);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const access = useRef<string | null>(null); // access token: só em memória
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<AuthStatus>('loading');
  const [me, setMe] = useState<Me | null>(null);
  const [memberships, setMemberships] = useState<Membership[]>([]);

  const loadProfile = useCallback(async (active: string | null, list: Membership[]) => {
    setMemberships(list);
    if (!active) { setMe(null); setStatus('needs_profile'); return 'needs_profile' as const; }
    setMe(await getMe());
    setStatus('authenticated');
    return 'authenticated' as const;
  }, []);

  const rotate = useCallback(async (): Promise<string | null> => {
    const stored = await tokenStore.get();
    if (!stored) return null;
    try {
      const t = await apiRefresh({ refreshToken: stored });
      if (t.refreshToken) await tokenStore.set(t.refreshToken);
      access.current = t.accessToken;
      return t.accessToken;
    } catch {
      await tokenStore.clear();
      return null;
    }
  }, []);

  useMemo(() => {
    configureApi({
      baseURL: process.env.EXPO_PUBLIC_API_URL ?? 'http://api.test', platform: 'mobile',
      getAccessToken: () => access.current,
      refresh: rotate,
      onAuthLost: () => { access.current = null; setMe(null); setStatus('expired'); router.replace('/session-expired'); },
      onProfileSelectionRequired: () => setStatus('needs_profile'),
    });
  }, [rotate, router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    (async () => {
      const token = await rotate();
      if (!token) { if (alive) setStatus('anonymous'); return; }
      try {
        const m = await getMemberships();
        if (alive) await loadProfile(m.activeMembershipId, m.memberships);
      } catch { if (alive) setStatus('anonymous'); }
    })();
    return () => { alive = false; };
  }, [rotate, loadProfile]);

  const api = useMemo<AuthApi>(() => ({
    status, me, memberships,
    async login(email, password) {
      let r;
      try { r = await apiLogin({ email, password }); } catch (e) { throw toApiError(e); }
      access.current = r.accessToken;
      if (r.refreshToken) await tokenStore.set(r.refreshToken);
      return loadProfile(r.activeMembershipId, r.memberships);
    },
    async selectProfile(membershipId) {
      await selectMembership({ membershipId });
      const m = await getMemberships();
      await loadProfile(m.activeMembershipId, m.memberships);
    },
    async logout() {
      const stored = await tokenStore.get();
      try { if (stored) await apiLogout({ refreshToken: stored }); } finally {
        access.current = null; await tokenStore.clear(); setMe(null); setMemberships([]); setStatus('anonymous');
      }
    },
    can: (p) => me?.permissions.includes(p) ?? false,
  }), [status, me, memberships, loadProfile]);

  return <Ctx.Provider value={api}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}
```

Telas (`app/(auth)/…`; mesma lógica e mensagens do web, com as diferenças de plataforma):

```tsx
// login.tsx
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Text } from 'react-native';
import { ApiError, useAuth } from '../../src/auth/AuthProvider';
import { PrimaryButton } from '../../src/ui/PrimaryButton';
import { Screen } from '../../src/ui/Screen';
import { TextField } from '../../src/ui/TextField';

const MESSAGES: Record<string, string> = {
  invalid_credentials: 'E-mail ou senha inválidos.',
  too_many_attempts: 'Muitas tentativas. Tente novamente em alguns minutos.',
};

export default function LoginScreen() {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(''); const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null); const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const submit = async () => {
    setError(null); setBusy(true);
    try {
      const outcome = await login(email.trim(), password);
      router.replace(outcome === 'authenticated' ? '/' : '/select-profile');
    } catch (e) {
      const code = e instanceof ApiError ? e.code : 'error';
      if (code === 'email_not_verified') router.push({ pathname: '/verify-email', params: { email: email.trim() } });
      else setError(MESSAGES[code] ?? 'Não foi possível entrar. Tente novamente.');
    } finally { setBusy(false); }
  };

  return (
    <Screen title="Login" subtitle="Acesse sua conta para continuar">
      <TextField placeholder="E-mail" value={email} onChangeText={setEmail} keyboardType="email-address" autoComplete="username" />
      <TextField placeholder="Senha" value={password} onChangeText={setPassword} secureTextEntry autoComplete="current-password" />
      {error && <Text accessibilityRole="alert" style={{ color: '#b1111b' }}>{error}</Text>}
      <PrimaryButton title="Entrar" onPress={submit} disabled={busy} />
      <PrimaryButton title="Esqueci minha senha" variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.push('/forgot-password')} />
      <PrimaryButton title="Tenho um convite" variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.push('/register')} />
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

`register.tsx` (aluno com convite; valida com `registerAlunoSchema` + confirmação de senha; `Switch` com `accessibilityLabel="Li e aceito os Termos de Uso"`; envia `termsVersion: TERMS_VERSION`; erros `invalid_invite` → "Convite inválido ou expirado." no campo do código; sucesso → `router.replace({ pathname: '/verify-email', params: { email } })`):

```tsx
import { registerAluno } from '@aura/contracts';
import { registerAlunoSchema, TERMS_VERSION } from '@aura/schemas';
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Switch, Text, View } from 'react-native';
import { PrimaryButton } from '../../src/ui/PrimaryButton';
import { Screen } from '../../src/ui/Screen';
import { TextField } from '../../src/ui/TextField';

export default function RegisterScreen() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { invite } = useLocalSearchParams<{ invite?: string }>();
  const [f, setF] = useState({ code: invite ?? '', name: '', email: '', phone: '', password: '', confirm: '' });
  const [terms, setTerms] = useState(false);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const set = (k: keyof typeof f) => (v: string) => setF((s) => ({ ...s, [k]: v }));

  const submit = async () => {
    const parsed = registerAlunoSchema.safeParse({
      code: f.code, name: f.name, email: f.email, phone: f.phone || undefined, password: f.password,
      acceptTerms: terms, termsVersion: TERMS_VERSION,
    });
    const next: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (!parsed.success) for (const i of parsed.error.issues) next[String(i.path[0])] = i.message;
    if (f.password !== f.confirm) next.confirm = 'As senhas não conferem.';
    setErrors(next);
    if (!parsed.success || next.confirm) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await registerAluno(parsed.data);
      router.replace({ pathname: '/verify-email', params: { email: parsed.data.email } });
    } catch (e: any) {
      const code = e?.response?.data?.code;
      setErrors({ [code === 'invalid_invite' ? 'code' : 'form']: code === 'invalid_invite' ? 'Convite inválido ou expirado.' : 'Não foi possível criar a conta.' });
    } finally { setBusy(false); }
  };

  return (
    <Screen title="Cadastro" subtitle="Use o convite enviado pelo seu personal">
      <TextField placeholder="Código de convite" value={f.code} onChangeText={set('code')} error={errors.code} />
      <TextField placeholder="Nome completo" value={f.name} onChangeText={set('name')} error={errors.name} />
      <TextField placeholder="E-mail" value={f.email} onChangeText={set('email')} keyboardType="email-address" error={errors.email} />
      <TextField placeholder="Telefone (com DDD)" value={f.phone} onChangeText={set('phone')} keyboardType="number-pad" error={errors.phone} />
      <TextField placeholder="Senha" value={f.password} onChangeText={set('password')} secureTextEntry error={errors.password} />
      <TextField placeholder="Confirmar senha" value={f.confirm} onChangeText={set('confirm')} secureTextEntry error={errors.confirm} />
      <View style={{ flexDirection: 'row', gap: 8, alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <Switch value={terms} onValueChange={setTerms} accessibilityLabel="Li e aceito os Termos de Uso" />
        <Text>Li e aceito os Termos de Uso</Text>
      </View>
      {errors.acceptTerms && <Text accessibilityRole="alert" style={{ color: '#b1111b' }}>Você precisa aceitar os termos.</Text>}
      {errors.form && <Text accessibilityRole="alert" style={{ color: '#b1111b' }}>{errors.form}</Text>}
      <PrimaryButton title="Criar conta" onPress={submit} disabled={busy} />
    </Screen>
  );
}
```

`forgot-password.tsx` (mesma resposta genérica do web), `verify-email.tsx` (reenvio com cooldown de 60 s, `useLocalSearchParams().email`), `session-expired.tsx` (botão "Fazer login" → `router.replace('/login')`), `select-profile.tsx` (lista `memberships` com os mesmos rótulos do web: Personal Trainer/Aluno/Administrador; "Continuar" → `selectProfile` → `router.replace('/')`), `app/(app)/index.tsx` (`Olá, {primeiro nome}` + botão "Sair"). Cada tela segue exatamente o padrão de `login.tsx` acima: `Screen` + `TextField`/`PrimaryButton`, estado local de erro e chamada à função de `@aura/contracts` correspondente (`forgotPassword`, `resendVerification`).

`app/_layout.tsx` (guarda de rota por status):

```tsx
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Stack, useRouter, useSegments } from 'expo-router';
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from '../src/auth/AuthProvider';

const client = new QueryClient({ defaultOptions: { queries: { retry: false } } });

function Gate() {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  const segments = useSegments();
  const router = useRouter();
  useEffect(() => {
    if (status === 'loading') return;
    const inAuth = segments[0] === '(auth)';
    if (status === 'authenticated' && inAuth) router.replace('/');
    if (status === 'needs_profile' && segments[1] !== 'select-profile') router.replace('/select-profile');
    if ((status === 'anonymous') && !inAuth) router.replace('/login');
  }, [status, segments, router]);
  return <Stack screenOptions={{ headerShown: false }} />;
}

export default function Root() {
  return <QueryClientProvider client={client}><AuthProvider><Gate /></AuthProvider></QueryClientProvider>;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Escrever os testes (scripts) que falham**

`scripts/check-prod-image.sh`:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Falha se a imagem de produção do serviço não seguir as regras do spec §6.4/§6.5.
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
APP=${1:-identity-service}
IMG="aura-$APP:check"
DIGEST=${NODE_IMAGE:?"defina NODE_IMAGE=node:22-alpine@sha256:<digest> (docker buildx imagetools inspect node:22-alpine)"}

# 1) build sem digest deve ser recusado
if docker build -q -f infra/docker/service.Dockerfile --build-arg APP="$APP" --build-arg NODE_IMAGE=node:22-alpine -t "$IMG:nodigest" . 2>/dev/null; then
  echo "FAIL: build aceitou base sem digest"; exit 1
fi

docker build -f infra/docker/service.Dockerfile --build-arg APP="$APP" --build-arg NODE_IMAGE="$DIGEST" -t "$IMG" .

[ "$(docker run --rm --entrypoint id "$IMG" -u)" != "0" ] || { echo "FAIL: roda como root"; exit 1; }
docker run --rm --entrypoint sh "$IMG" -c '! ls -a /app | grep -Eq "^(\.env|\.git|docs|test)$"' || { echo "FAIL: arquivos sensíveis na imagem"; exit 1; }
docker run --rm --entrypoint sh "$IMG" -c '! ls /app/node_modules | grep -Eq "^(vitest|typescript|supertest)$"' || { echo "FAIL: dev deps na imagem"; exit 1; }
docker run --rm -e NODE_ENV=production "$IMG" node -e "process.exit(0)" >/dev/null
# boot em produção com config insegura deve falhar (guard do http-security)
if docker run --rm -e NODE_ENV=production -e ENABLE_SWAGGER=true "$IMG" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "FAIL: subiu em produção com ENABLE_SWAGGER=true"; exit 1
fi
echo "OK"
```

`scripts/check-web-static.sh`:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
NGINX_IMAGE=${NGINX_IMAGE:?"defina NGINX_IMAGE=nginxinc/nginx-unprivileged:1.27-alpine@sha256:<digest>"}
docker build -f infra/docker/web.Dockerfile --build-arg NODE_IMAGE="${NODE_IMAGE:?}" --build-arg NGINX_IMAGE="$NGINX_IMAGE" -t aura-web:check .
cid=$(docker run -d -e API_ORIGIN=https://api.aura.test -p 127.0.0.1:18080:8080 aura-web:check)
trap 'docker rm -f "$cid" >/dev/null' EXIT
for i in $(seq 1 30); do curl -sf -o /dev/null http://127.0.0.1:18080/ && break; sleep 1; done

code() { curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' "http://127.0.0.1:18080$1"; }
[ "$(code /.env)" = "404" ] || { echo "FAIL: /.env"; exit 1; }
[ "$(code /.git/config)" = "404" ] || { echo "FAIL: /.git/config"; exit 1; }
[ "$(code /docker-compose.yml)" = "404" ] || { echo "FAIL: compose"; exit 1; }
[ "$(code /pnpm-lock.yaml)" = "404" ] || { echo "FAIL: lockfile"; exit 1; }
[ "$(code /assets/index.js.map)" = "404" ] || { echo "FAIL: source map"; exit 1; }
[ "$(code /assets/)" != "200" ] || { echo "FAIL: directory listing"; exit 1; }
[ "$(code /login)" = "200" ] || { echo "FAIL: SPA fallback"; exit 1; }

h=$(curl -sI http://127.0.0.1:18080/)
echo "$h" | grep -qi "content-security-policy:.*default-src 'self'" || { echo "FAIL: CSP"; exit 1; }
echo "$h" | grep -qi "content-security-policy:.*connect-src 'self' https://api.aura.test" || { echo "FAIL: CSP connect-src"; exit 1; }
! echo "$h" | grep -qi "unsafe-inline" || { echo "FAIL: unsafe-inline"; exit 1; }
echo "$h" | grep -qi "x-content-type-options: nosniff" || { echo "FAIL: nosniff"; exit 1; }
echo "$h" | grep -qi "x-frame-options: DENY" || { echo "FAIL: XFO"; exit 1; }
echo "$h" | grep -qi "strict-transport-security:" || { echo "FAIL: HSTS"; exit 1; }
! echo "$h" | grep -Eqi "^server: nginx/[0-9]" || { echo "FAIL: versão do nginx exposta"; exit 1; }
echo "OK"
```

`scripts/e2e-smoke.sh` (roda **dentro** do container `workspace`, contra o stack real):

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
BASE=${BASE:-http://bff:3000}; KC=${KEYCLOAK_URL:-http://keycloak:8080}
PW='Sup3r-secret-pw'; TS=$(date +%s)
fail() { echo "FAIL: $*"; exit 1; }
req() { curl -sS -H 'Host: bff' -H 'content-type: application/json' "$@"; }
status() { curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -H 'Host: bff' -H 'content-type: application/json' "$@"; }
expect() { [ "$1" = "$2" ] || fail "$3 (esperado $2, veio $1)"; }

KC_TOKEN=$(curl -sS -d "client_id=admin-cli&username=$KEYCLOAK_ADMIN&password=$KEYCLOAK_ADMIN_PASSWORD&grant_type=password" "$KC/realms/master/protocol/openid-connect/token" | jq -r .access_token)
verify_email() {
  local id; id=$(curl -sS -H "authorization: Bearer $KC_TOKEN" "$KC/admin/realms/aura/users?email=$1&exact=true" | jq -r '.[0].id')
  curl -sS -X PUT -H "authorization: Bearer $KC_TOKEN" -H 'content-type: application/json' -d '{"emailVerified":true}' "$KC/admin/realms/aura/users/$id" >/dev/null
}
reg() { # email name path [extra-json]
  req -X POST "$BASE$3" -d "{\"email\":\"$1\",\"name\":\"$2\",\"password\":\"$PW\",\"acceptTerms\":true,\"termsVersion\":\"2025-04-12\"${4:-}}"
}

P="personal$TS@example.com"; A="aluno$TS@example.com"

echo "-- cadastro do personal (mesma resposta para e-mail repetido)"
r1=$(reg "$P" "Rafael Silva" /auth/register); r2=$(reg "$P" "Rafael Silva" /auth/register)
expect "$(echo "$r1" | jq -r .status)" pending_verification "register"
expect "$r1" "$r2" "registro repetido vazou diferença"

echo "-- login bloqueado até verificar o e-mail"
expect "$(status -X POST "$BASE/auth/login" -d "{\"email\":\"$P\",\"password\":\"$PW\"}")" 403 "login sem verificação"
verify_email "$P"

echo "-- login web (cookie) e mobile (corpo)"
hdr=$(mktemp)
web=$(req -D "$hdr" -X POST "$BASE/auth/login" -d "{\"email\":\"$P\",\"password\":\"$PW\"}")
TOKEN=$(echo "$web" | jq -r .accessToken)
[ "$(echo "$web" | jq 'has("refreshToken")')" = "false" ] || fail "web recebeu refreshToken no corpo"
grep -qi 'set-cookie: aura_rt=.*HttpOnly' "$hdr" || fail "cookie aura_rt sem HttpOnly"
mob=$(req -H 'x-client: mobile' -X POST "$BASE/auth/login" -d "{\"email\":\"$P\",\"password\":\"$PW\"}")
RT=$(echo "$mob" | jq -r .refreshToken)

echo "-- /me e permissões do personal"
me=$(req "$BASE/me" -H "authorization: Bearer $TOKEN")
expect "$(echo "$me" | jq -r .role)" personal "papel"
expect "$(status "$BASE/roles" -H "authorization: Bearer $TOKEN")" 403 "personal em /roles"
expect "$(status "$BASE/me")" 401 "sem token"

echo "-- convite de uso único e cadastro do aluno"
code=$(req -X POST "$BASE/invites" -H "authorization: Bearer $TOKEN" -d '{"ttlHours":24}' | jq -r .code)
expect "$(reg "$A" "Lucas Ferreira" /auth/register-aluno ",\"code\":\"$code\"" | jq -r .status)" pending_verification "register-aluno"
verify_email "$A"
expect "$(status -X POST "$BASE/auth/register-aluno" -d "{\"email\":\"x$TS@example.com\",\"name\":\"Outro\",\"password\":\"$PW\",\"acceptTerms\":true,\"termsVersion\":\"2025-04-12\",\"code\":\"$code\"}")" 400 "reuso do convite"
ATOKEN=$(req -X POST "$BASE/auth/login" -d "{\"email\":\"$A\",\"password\":\"$PW\"}" | jq -r .accessToken)
expect "$(req "$BASE/me" -H "authorization: Bearer $ATOKEN" | jq -r .role)" aluno "papel do aluno"
expect "$(status -X POST "$BASE/invites" -H "authorization: Bearer $ATOKEN" -d '{}')" 403 "aluno criando convite"

echo "-- rotação e detecção de reuso do refresh"
r=$(req -H 'x-client: mobile' -X POST "$BASE/auth/refresh" -d "{\"refreshToken\":\"$RT\"}")
NEW=$(echo "$r" | jq -r .refreshToken); [ "$NEW" != "$RT" ] || fail "refresh não rotacionou"
expect "$(status -X POST "$BASE/auth/refresh" -d "{\"refreshToken\":\"$RT\"}")" 401 "reuso do refresh antigo"
expect "$(status -X POST "$BASE/auth/refresh" -d "{\"refreshToken\":\"$NEW\"}")" 401 "família revogada após reuso"

echo "-- credenciais erradas: mesma resposta para e-mail existente e inexistente"
b1=$(req -X POST "$BASE/auth/login" -d "{\"email\":\"$P\",\"password\":\"wrong-password\"}" | jq -c 'del(.correlationId)')
b2=$(req -X POST "$BASE/auth/login" -d "{\"email\":\"ghost$TS@example.com\",\"password\":\"wrong-password\"}" | jq -c 'del(.correlationId)')
expect "$b1" "$b2" "enumeração de e-mails no login"

echo "-- lockout progressivo"
L="lock$TS@example.com"; c=0
for i in 1 2 3 4 5 6; do c=$(status -X POST "$BASE/auth/login" -d "{\"email\":\"$L\",\"password\":\"wrong-password\"}"); done
expect "$c" 429 "lockout após 5 falhas"

echo "-- cabeçalhos, Host e erros sem vazamento"
h=$(curl -sI -H 'Host: bff' "$BASE/health")
for k in 'x-content-type-options: nosniff' 'x-frame-options: DENY' "content-security-policy:.*default-src 'none'" 'referrer-policy: no-referrer'; do
  echo "$h" | grep -Eqi "$k" || fail "header ausente: $k"
done
! echo "$h" | grep -qi 'x-powered-by' || fail "x-powered-by exposto"
expect "$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -H 'Host: evil.example' "$BASE/health")" 421 "Host fora da allowlist"
err=$(req "$BASE/does-not-exist" -H "authorization: Bearer $TOKEN")
echo "$err" | jq -e '.correlationId and .code' >/dev/null || fail "erro sem formato padrão"
! echo "$err" | grep -Eqi 'stack|prisma|SELECT|node_modules' || fail "erro vazou detalhes internos"

echo "-- CSRF no refresh por cookie"
expect "$(status -X POST "$BASE/auth/refresh" -H 'Cookie: aura_rt=whatever-refresh-token; aura_csrf=abc' -d '{}')" 403 "refresh por cookie sem Origin/CSRF"

echo "OK"
```

`infra/docker/node.Dockerfile`: trocar a linha de pacotes por `apk add --no-cache openssl bash curl jq`.

`infra/docker/service.Dockerfile` (produção; **uma imagem por serviço**, `--build-arg APP=bff|identity-service`):

```dockerfile
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1
ARG NODE_IMAGE=node:22-alpine
FROM ${NODE_IMAGE} AS guard
ARG NODE_IMAGE
RUN case "$NODE_IMAGE" in *@sha256:*) ;; *) echo "NODE_IMAGE precisa estar pinada por digest" >&2; exit 1;; esac

FROM ${NODE_IMAGE} AS build
COPY --from=guard /etc/os-release /tmp/.guard
ARG APP
ENV PNPM_HOME=/pnpm-store
RUN corepack enable && corepack prepare pnpm@9.15.0 --activate && apk add --no-cache openssl
WORKDIR /repo
COPY . .
RUN pnpm install --frozen-lockfile \
 && pnpm turbo run build --filter=@aura/${APP}... \
 && pnpm --filter @aura/${APP} deploy --prod /out \
 && if [ -d apps/${APP}/prisma ]; then cd /out && npx prisma generate; fi

FROM ${NODE_IMAGE}
RUN apk add --no-cache openssl && addgroup -S app && adduser -S app -G app
ENV NODE_ENV=production
WORKDIR /app
COPY --from=build --chown=app:app /out /app
USER app
CMD ["node", "dist/main.js"]
```

`infra/docker/web.Dockerfile`:

```dockerfile
# syntax=docker/dockerfile:1
ARG NODE_IMAGE=node:22-alpine
ARG NGINX_IMAGE=nginxinc/nginx-unprivileged:1.27-alpine
FROM ${NODE_IMAGE} AS build
ENV PNPM_HOME=/pnpm-store
RUN corepack enable && corepack prepare pnpm@9.15.0 --activate
WORKDIR /repo
COPY . .
RUN pnpm install --frozen-lockfile && pnpm turbo run build --filter=@aura/web...

FROM ${NGINX_IMAGE}
COPY --from=build --chown=nginx:nginx /repo/apps/web/dist /usr/share/nginx/html
COPY infra/nginx/default.conf.template /etc/nginx/templates/default.conf.template
ENV API_ORIGIN=https://api.aura-fit.example
EXPOSE 8080
```

`infra/nginx/default.conf.template` (o Nginx expande `${API_ORIGIN}` via `envsubst` no start):

```nginx
server_tokens off;

server {
  listen 8080;
  root /usr/share/nginx/html;
  index index.html;
  autoindex off;

  add_header Content-Security-Policy "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data:; font-src 'self'; connect-src 'self' ${API_ORIGIN}; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'self'" always;
  add_header Strict-Transport-Security "max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains; preload" always;
  add_header X-Content-Type-Options "nosniff" always;
  add_header X-Frame-Options "DENY" always;
  add_header Referrer-Policy "no-referrer" always;
  add_header Permissions-Policy "camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=(), payment=(), usb=()" always;
  add_header Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy "same-origin" always;

  # nunca servir dotfiles, source maps, manifests, lockfiles ou configs
  location ~ /\. { return 404; }
  location ~* \.(map|env|yml|yaml|lock|toml|ini|conf|log|sh|md)$ { return 404; }
  location ~* ^/(docker-compose|package|pnpm-lock|Dockerfile) { return 404; }

  location /assets/ {
    try_files $uri =404;
    add_header Cache-Control "public, max-age=31536000, immutable" always;
    add_header X-Content-Type-Options "nosniff" always;
  }

  location / {
    try_files $uri /index.html;
    add_header Cache-Control "no-store" always;
  }
}
```

(Como `add_header` em `location` descarta os do nível `server`, repetir em `location /assets/` e `location /` os headers de segurança via `include` de um snippet — extrair as 6 linhas de `add_header` para `infra/nginx/security-headers.conf` e `include` em ambos os `location`; o teste `check-web-static.sh` valida `/` e detecta regressão.)

`.github/workflows/ci.yml`:

```yaml
name: ci
on: { pull_request: {}, push: { branches: [main] } }
permissions: { contents: read }
jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - run: cp .env.example .env
      - run: docker compose up -d --wait postgres redis keycloak mailpit otel-collector
      - name: Install (lockfile congelado), build e testes dentro do compose
        run: docker compose run --rm workspace sh -c "pnpm install --frozen-lockfile && pnpm build && pnpm test"
      - name: Dependências (falha em alta/crítica)
        run: docker compose run --rm workspace pnpm audit --audit-level=high
      - name: Stack completo + smoke e2e
        run: |
          docker compose up -d --wait identity-service bff
          docker compose run --rm workspace bash scripts/e2e-smoke.sh
          make admin
          make contract
      - if: always()
        run: docker compose logs --no-color > compose.log
  secrets:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with: { fetch-depth: 0 }
      - uses: gitleaks/gitleaks-action@v2
        env: { GITHUB_TOKEN: "${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}" }
  images:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: test
    env:
      NODE_IMAGE: ${{ vars.NODE_IMAGE }}   # node:22-alpine@sha256:<digest>
      NGINX_IMAGE: ${{ vars.NGINX_IMAGE }} # nginxinc/nginx-unprivileged:1.27-alpine@sha256:<digest>
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - run: bash scripts/check-prod-image.sh identity-service
      - run: bash scripts/check-prod-image.sh bff
      - run: bash scripts/check-web-static.sh
      - uses: aquasecurity/trivy-action@0.28.0
        with: { image-ref: aura-bff:check, severity: "HIGH,CRITICAL", exit-code: "1", ignore-unfixed: true }
      - uses: aquasecurity/trivy-action@0.28.0
        with: { image-ref: aura-identity-service:check, severity: "HIGH,CRITICAL", exit-code: "1", ignore-unfixed: true }
      - uses: aquasecurity/trivy-action@0.28.0
        with: { image-ref: aura-web:check, severity: "HIGH,CRITICAL", exit-code: "1", ignore-unfixed: true }
      - name: SBOM (CycloneDX)
        run: docker compose run --rm workspace sh -c "pnpm dlx @cyclonedx/cdxgen -o sbom.json ." 
      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with: { name: sbom, path: sbom.json }
```

`scripts/contract-check.ts` — valida as **respostas reais** do stack contra os schemas de `@aura/schemas` (impede que o backend mude de formato sem o contrato acompanhar). Roda no `workspace` depois do smoke e de `make admin`:

```ts
import * as S from '@aura/schemas';
import { z } from 'zod';

const BASE = process.env.BASE ?? 'http://bff:3000';
const KC = process.env.KEYCLOAK_URL ?? 'http://keycloak:8080';
const PW = 'Sup3r-secret-pw';

async function api(path: string, init: RequestInit & { token?: string } = {}) {
  const r = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    ...init,
    headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', ...(init.token ? { authorization: `Bearer ${init.token}` } : {}), ...(init.headers ?? {}) },
  });
  const text = await r.text();
  return { status: r.status, body: text ? JSON.parse(text) : undefined };
}
function check<T>(name: string, schema: z.ZodType<T>, body: unknown) {
  const r = schema.safeParse(body);
  if (!r.success) throw new Error(`contract drift em ${name}: ${JSON.stringify(r.error.issues)}`);
  console.log(`ok  ${name}`);
}
async function kcToken() {
  const r = await fetch(`${KC}/realms/master/protocol/openid-connect/token`, {
    method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' },
    body: new URLSearchParams({ client_id: 'admin-cli', grant_type: 'password', username: process.env.KEYCLOAK_ADMIN!, password: process.env.KEYCLOAK_ADMIN_PASSWORD! }),
  });
  return ((await r.json()) as { access_token: string }).access_token;
}
async function verifyEmail(email: string) {
  const t = await kcToken();
  const [u] = (await (await fetch(`${KC}/admin/realms/aura/users?email=${encodeURIComponent(email)}&exact=true`, { headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${t}` } })).json()) as { id: string }[];
  await fetch(`${KC}/admin/realms/aura/users/${u.id}`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { authorization: `Bearer ${t}`, 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ emailVerified: true }) });
}

async function main() {
  const email = `contract${Date.now()}@example.com`;
  const reg = await api('/auth/register', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ email, name: 'Rafael Silva', password: PW, acceptTerms: true, termsVersion: S.TERMS_VERSION }) });
  check('POST /auth/register', S.registerResponseSchema, reg.body);
  await verifyEmail(email);

  const login = await api('/auth/login', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'x-client': 'mobile' }, body: JSON.stringify({ email, password: PW }) });
  check('POST /auth/login', S.loginResponseSchema, login.body);
  const token = login.body.accessToken as string;
  check('GET /auth/memberships', S.membershipsResponseSchema, (await api('/auth/memberships', { token })).body);
  check('GET /me', S.meSchema, (await api('/me', { token })).body);
  check('POST /invites', S.inviteResponseSchema, (await api('/invites', { method: 'POST', token, body: JSON.stringify({ ttlHours: 24 }) })).body);
  check('POST /auth/refresh', S.tokenResponseSchema, (await api('/auth/refresh', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'x-client': 'mobile' }, body: JSON.stringify({ refreshToken: login.body.refreshToken }) })).body);
  check('erro 401', S.errorSchema, (await api('/me')).body);

  const admin = await api('/auth/login', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ email: process.env.ADMIN_EMAIL, password: process.env.ADMIN_PASSWORD }) });
  const at = admin.body.accessToken as string;
  check('GET /roles', z.array(S.roleSchema), (await api('/roles', { token: at })).body);
  check('GET /permissions', z.array(z.string()), (await api('/permissions', { token: at })).body);
  check('GET /users', S.usersPageSchema, (await api('/users', { token: at })).body);
}
main().catch((e) => { console.error(e.message); process.exit(1); });
```

Executar com `pnpm --filter @aura/contracts exec tsx ../../scripts/contract-check.ts` (dentro do `workspace`, com `pnpm --filter @aura/schemas build` feito antes).

### Task 19: Login social com Google (provider-agnóstico)

**Estado:** esboço aprovado por escopo; o detalhamento passo a passo (código e testes completos) é feito no **Sprint Planning da Sprint 4**, após as decisões abaixo. Vem depois do BFF (Task 11) e das telas de auth (Task 15).

**Objetivo:** "Continuar com Google" no web, sem código específico de Google nos fluxos. Google é só uma entrada de configuração; outro provedor entra depois sem mudar a lógica.

**Arquivos previstos**
- `infra/keycloak/realm-aura.json`: `identityProviders` com alias `google` (`clientId`/`clientSecret` por `${GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID}`/`${GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET}`) e fluxo de primeiro login configurado.
- `docker-compose.yml`: `KC_HOSTNAME=http://localhost:8081`, variáveis `GOOGLE_*` no serviço `keycloak`; BFF com `KEYCLOAK_ISSUER=http://localhost:8081/realms/aura`, `WEB_ORIGIN`.
- `scripts/load-google-secrets.sh`: lê `secrets/client_secret_*.json` (via `jq`, dentro do `workspace`) e grava `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID`/`GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET` no `.env`; nunca imprime o segredo.
- identity: `src/social/social.service.ts`, `social.controller.ts` (rota interna `POST /internal/social/complete`), allowlist `SOCIAL_PROVIDERS`.
- BFF: `POST /auth/social/:provider/start` e `POST /auth/social/callback`; estado em Redis.
- `@aura/schemas` e registry do contrato: schemas e operações `socialStart`/`socialCallback`.
- web: `SocialButtons` (gerado de `VITE_SOCIAL_PROVIDERS`), página `/auth/callback`.

**Desenho**
1. `start`: o BFF gera `state` e o par PKCE (`code_verifier`/`code_challenge`), guarda `{ provider, codeVerifier, inviteCode?, termsVersion }` em `social:state:{state}` com TTL de 10 min e uso único, e devolve `{ url }` (via `buildSocialLoginUrl`). O `redirect_uri` vem da configuração do servidor (`WEB_ORIGIN/auth/callback`), nunca do cliente (sem *open redirect*).
2. `callback`: o BFF consome o `state` (uso único; repetição → 400), chama o identity, que troca o `code` pela porta, exige e-mail verificado pelo provedor, localiza ou provisiona o usuário e devolve o mesmo resultado do login por senha (`LoginResult`); o BFF aplica cookies/CSRF como no login normal.
3. Provisionamento: usuário existente entra; novo sem convite vira **Personal** com tenant (mesma regra do cadastro público, com o aceite dos termos vindo do `start`); novo com `inviteCode` vira **Aluno** daquele tenant.
4. Provedor desconhecido (fora de `SOCIAL_PROVIDERS`) → 404 genérico.

**Decisões a confirmar no planning da Sprint 4**
1. Conta já existente por senha com o mesmo e-mail: **vincular automaticamente quando o Google informar e-mail verificado** (recomendado) ou exigir a senha antes.
2. Cadastro novo via Google sem convite cria Personal (recomendado, coerente com o cadastro público). Alternativa: só permitir login de contas já existentes.
3. Login social no **mobile** fica como acompanhamento (navegador do sistema + `aurafit://`), fora desta task.

**Testes previstos (todos escritos antes)**: estado de uso único e com expiração; `state` divergente ou repetido → 400; `redirect_uri` nunca aceito do cliente; e-mail não verificado pelo provedor → recusado; usuário existente vinculado; novo Personal criado com termos registrados; convite → Aluno; provedor fora da allowlist → 404; realm importado contém o IdP `google` sem segredo em claro no JSON; página `/auth/callback` trata erro e sucesso; smoke real contra o Keycloak até a tela do Google (o consentimento final é manual).

**Extensão futura (Apple etc.)**: registrar o IdP no realm, incluir o id em `SOCIAL_PROVIDERS` e em `VITE_SOCIAL_PROVIDERS`, adicionar rótulo/ícone em `SocialButtons`. Nenhum fluxo muda.

| §4 Login social | Google: **Task 19**; Apple e outros: fora desta fase, código preparado |

## Pendências e decisões a confirmar antes da execução

1. **Login social — só Google nesta fase (Task 19); Apple e outros ficam para depois.**
   - **Google:** credenciais OAuth já criadas e guardadas em `secrets/` (pasta no `.gitignore`; **nunca commitar**). Um script (`scripts/load-google-secrets.sh`) copia `client_id`/`client_secret` do JSON para o `.env` local (também ignorado), e o compose os repassa ao Keycloak. No console do Google: URI de redirecionamento `http://localhost:8081/realms/aura/broker/google/endpoint` (prod: `https://<dominio-de-auth>/realms/aura/broker/google/endpoint`) e origem JavaScript `http://localhost:8081` (sem caminho e sem `/` no fim).
   - **Preparado para outros provedores:** o provedor é um id (string) validado contra `SOCIAL_PROVIDERS`; o realm, o BFF, o identity e o web tratam qualquer id igual. Acrescentar Apple depois = registrar o IdP no realm + incluir o id na config + entrada nos botões, sem alterar os fluxos.
   - **Apple:** não iniciado; será verificado em outro momento (exige HTTPS, sem `localhost`).
   - **Keycloak roda no compose** (já é o serviço `keycloak`); a Task 19 acrescenta `KC_HOSTNAME=http://localhost:8081` e ajusta `KEYCLOAK_ISSUER` do BFF para `http://localhost:8081/realms/aura`.
2. **Contrato por Zod → OpenAPI** em vez de `@nestjs/swagger` (Task 13): o BFF só encaminha e os DTOs são Zod; o teste de *drift* garante que toda rota documentada existe.
3. **Login por senha via grant `password` do Keycloak** (Task 4) para telas próprias; mitigado por brute-force protection do realm e throttle. Avaliar Authorization Code + PKCE depois.
4. **Texto dos Termos de Uso** (Task 16) é estrutural; o texto jurídico definitivo deve ser fornecido e `TERMS_VERSION` atualizado junto.
5. **Digests das imagens base** (`NODE_IMAGE`, `NGINX_IMAGE`) precisam ser obtidos e configurados como variáveis do CI antes do primeiro build de produção.
6. **Escala do BFF:** o throttle do `@nestjs/throttler` é em memória (instância única); com várias réplicas, trocar por storage Redis.
